Juristic Platform · Documentation

Engineering Specification & Implementation Plan

Owner: Principal Software EngineerDate: 2026-09-06Status: Draft v1.0Version: 1.0
Contents

Introduction

This document is the engineering contract for Juristic Platform — a server-rendered web application with which the juristic person of one apartment building bills each room for water, electricity and the common area fee, takes the money in cash at the office counter, issues a numbered receipt, and keeps every tenant informed through a LINE Official Account. It charges no rent and holds no lease data (PRD §24, D-05).

It is written so that a student who has never seen the project can open it on a Monday morning, build the schema, implement an endpoint, and know from the page alone whether the result is correct. Every rule cites its source: a PRD section, a product decision id (D-01…D-47), or the architecture decision record condensed in Architecture Decisions.

Purpose

Three jobs, and nothing else:

  1. Fix the contract. Table names, column types, constraint names, route paths, JSON payload keys, error codes, Thai message text and enum casing are decided here once, so two students working in parallel cannot invent two spellings of the same thing.
  2. Make the arithmetic checkable. The PRD §28 demo — room 205, 30.00 ตร.ม., water 1,250 → 1,260 = 10 หน่วย × 18 = 180, electricity 5,280 → 5,350 = 70 หน่วย × 8 = 560, common area 30 × 35 = 1,050, total 1,790.00 บาท, due 5 ก.ย. 2026, paid 3 ก.ย. 2026, receipt REC-202609-000852 — appears in every worked example, and is the one test that may never go red.
  3. Make the plan executable. Eight milestones over fourteen weeks, sized in person-days, ordered so that the graded demo path runs end to end five weeks before the defence.

Audience

How to read this document

  • Prose is English; product copy is Thai. Every visible string in the running app — labels, buttons, LINE messages, error text — is Thai, because the users are Thai. The English page identifiers of the PRD (Login, Dashboard, Rooms, Tenants, Meter Reading, Monthly Billing, Bill Detail, Payment, Receipt, Announcements, Reports, Settings) survive in routes, filenames, payloads and test hooks (D-02).
  • Bill statuses are exactly Draft Unpaid Paid Overdue, and only the first three are ever stored (D-21).
  • Money is THB. 1,790.00 in tables, on Bill Detail and on the receipt; 1,790 บาท in LINE messages and sentence text; ฿1,790 only in this documentation set. Timezone Asia/Bangkok, Gregorian years, ISO 8601 in every payload (D-03).
  • Citations are load-bearing. D-xx is the product decision log; ADR §x and A-x are the architecture record; ENG-1…ENG-5 are the five mechanism corrections the ADR makes to the decision log; FR-xx / NFR-xx are the requirement ids introduced in §1 and used by the test plan.
  • Where two rules could disagree, the order is: ADR-001 → the decision log → this document. A conflict resolved in favour of one over the other is always visible as a decision callout, never as a silent edit.
  • The PRD §24 exclusion list is absolute and is restated in §1.7: no rent, no lease contracts, no mobile app, no payment gateway, no cards, no online banking, no slip upload, no QR payment, no IoT or automatic meters, no AI/ML.

The one number to remember

If a change makes room 205's September bill anything other than 1,790.00 — three items, 180.00 + 560.00 + 1,050.00, the total being the SQL sum of the already-rounded lines — the change is wrong, whatever else it improves.

Architecture Decisions

Condensed from ADR-001 — Juristic Platform: system architecture (Accepted), which is binding on every section below and on every sibling document. Where the ADR and a proposal disagreed, the ADR won; where the ADR and the product decision log disagree, the decision log wins except at the five points marked ENG-1…ENG-5, which are mechanism corrections that preserve the decided policy. The full record — including the scoring of the two competing proposals — lives in ADR-001; this section carries what an implementer has to obey.

The five ENG corrections, in one place
IdCorrectionWhy the literal decision could not be implementedDetail
ENG-1tenants.active_room_id — a stored generated column IF(status='Active', room_id, NULL) with uq_tenants_active_roomD-10's one-Active-tenant-per-room rule left to a service is a SELECT-then-INSERT race with a guaranteed loser§2.3.4
ENG-2uq_bill_items_bill_type (bill_id, item_type)"Exactly three items per bill" (D-18) is otherwise a convention a retry can break§2.3.8
ENG-3notifications.dedupe_key — a stored generated CONCAT_WS column with a unique indexD-30's literal UNIQUE(type, bill_id, announcement_id, tenant_id, send_date) cannot enforce the rule: InnoDB treats NULLs as distinct and one of the two id columns is always NULL, so every duplicate would be accepted§2.3.10, §5.6.4
ENG-4LINK_OK, LINK_FAIL and the auto-reply go out with the Reply API and write no notifications rowD-34 files link replies under "Push"; replying to an inbound message is what D-29 actually describes, and reply messages do not consume the 500-message allowance§5.5.1
ENG-5TENANCY_END is an audited actionD-39 requires a Thai reason on the สิ้นสุดสัญญาเช่า dialog and audit_log.reason is the only place it can be stored; collecting a reason and discarding it is a defect§1.5 FR-36

One further correction applies to both competing proposals: previous_period is VARCHAR(8), never VARCHAR(7), because it holds the literal 'baseline' as well as a YYYY-MM period (D-06).

Stack

Ten runtime dependencies, three dev dependencies, zero build steps, one deployable process. package-lock.json is committed and npm ci is what reproduces a build. Versions are floors pinned at kickoff: a minor bump is routine, a major bump is a decision.

LayerChoiceVersionWhy this and not the obvious alternative
RuntimeNode.js, Active LTS24.x (engines: ">=24 <25")One language for pages, jobs and the LINE client. fetch, node:test, --env-file and Intl are built in — four dependencies deleted.
HTTP frameworkExpress^5.1Named by PRD §23 and fixed by D-43. Express 5 forwards async handler rejections to the error middleware, so a forgotten try/catch in a money path is a logged 500, not a hung request.
ViewsEJS, server-rendered^3.1.10D-43 forbids a separate frontend build. Thai copy is written where it is read.
Client JSPlain ES modules from /public, no bundler—Four interactive surfaces only — per-room reading save, the send-drain loop, the change calculator, the announcement preview — roughly 200 lines in total.
ChartsChart.js from cdnjs4.5.xPRD §23 and D-41's four charts: one <script> tag, no toolchain.
DatabaseMySQL8.4 (minimum 8.0.16 for CHECK)PRD §23. D-26 is written in MySQL 8 dialect: stored generated columns, ON DUPLICATE KEY UPDATE … LAST_INSERT_ID(seq+1).
Data accessmysql2/promise pool, prepared statements, hand-written SQL. No ORM.^3.15See A-2. mysql2 returns DECIMAL as a string, which is exactly what D-16 requires.
Schema managementCommitted db/schema.sql + db/seed.sql, rebuilt from empty—See A-3. No migration framework, no schema_migrations.
Session authexpress-session + express-mysql-session^1.18 / ^3.0D-37 verbatim. A free instance restarts; memory sessions log everyone out mid-demo.
Password hashingbcryptjs, cost 10^3.0D-37 fixes bcrypt cost 10; the pure-JS build is the same algorithm with no native toolchain on a free host.
Validationzod^4One schema per endpoint produces both the 422 payload and D-40's Thai inline field message from one definition.
LINE SDK@line/bot-sdk^10validateSignature and the explicit 400 that D-30 keys auto-unbinding on are the two places a hand-rolled client fails silently. Push, multicast and reply come with it.
Security headershelmet^8CSP allowing only cdnjs (Chart.js) and Google Fonts.
Login rate limitexpress-rate-limit^8D-37: 10 failed logins per IP per 15 minutes, with app.set('trust proxy', 1).
CSRFOwn 25-line synchronizer token (middleware/csrf.js)—csurf is deprecated; a session-stored random token compared with crypto.timingSafeEqual is smaller than any package that wraps it. Unit tested.
SchedulerExternal cron (cron-job.org free), two schedules—D-32: no node-cron — an in-process timer never fires on a sleeping instance. Render Cron Jobs are the paid fallback D-32 allows.
Printwindow.print() on a dedicated A5 route plus a print stylesheet—D-28 verbatim. No PDF library, and therefore no Thai font embedding problem.
CSV export15-line shared helper, UTF-8 with BOM—D-42's four exports ship already-shaped rows.
Thai baht in wordsOwn lib/thaiBaht.js—D-27 names it a deliverable with a required unit test.
Dates and money formattingIntl (th-TH-u-ca-gregory-nu-latn, Asia/Bangkok)stdlibThe -u-ca-gregory keyword is D-03's "never Buddhist Era" rule expressed once. No dayjs, no date-fns.
Logging10-line lib/log.js, JSON to stdout—Render captures stdout.
Lint / formatESLint flat config + Prettier + eslint-config-prettier^9 / ^3Two config files, no plugins.
Testsnode:test + node:assert/strict + built-in fetch for tiers 1–3; @playwright/test (dev-only) for tier 4stdlib / ^1.5Zero test dependencies in the three tiers that guard the money. Tier 4 is the scoped A-9 amendment granted in §6.5.1 — one devDependency, nothing in the runtime.
Web hostingRender free web service—D-43: one fixed HTTPS origin for the webhook, for the life of the project.
DB hostingAiven free managed MySQL 8 (Clever Cloud as alternate)—D-43. Connection string in environment variables only.
CIGitHub Actions, three jobs — verify, api, e2e — against MySQL 8.4 service containers. The file lives in test plan §2.11 and nowhere else.—Nothing else. No coverage gate, no Node version matrix, no deploy step, no secret (§6.5.3).

Language: JavaScript (ESM), not TypeScript. No build step for server or client, matching D-43. Type safety where it matters comes from zod at the trust boundary and DECIMAL in the schema — a compiler cannot validate a meter reading a human typed.

Explicitly rejected dependencies: any ORM (A-2), a migration framework (A-3), csurf, csrf-sync, dotenv, native bcrypt, moment/dayjs/date-fns, any PDF library, any logging framework, Jest and Vitest, supertest, React/Next.js (A-1), Vercel (D-43), Redis, and any queue or broker.

One exception, granted in writing. ADR §1 also rejected Playwright, and A-9 rejected it by name. That rejection is amended — narrowly, on stated terms, at a stated cost of 2 person-days added to M8 — so that the seven browser scenarios of the test plan can run as tier 4. The amendment, its scope and its cut order are in §6.5.1; it adds one devDependency and changes nothing the deployed process runs.

Runtime architecture

flowchart LR
  ADM["Admin browser, desktop 1280px"]

  TEN["Tenant, LINE app"]
  CRON["cron-job.org, two schedules"]
  LP["LINE Messaging API"]

  subgraph RENDER["Render free web service, one Node 24 process"]
    MW["Middleware chain: helmet, static, session, CSRF, auth, role"]
    PAGES["EJS page routes"]
    API["JSON routes under /api"]
    HOOK["POST /line/webhook, signature, ACK 200 first"]
    JOB["POST /internal/jobs/daily, X-Job-Token"]
    HZ["GET /healthz"]
    SVC["Services: billing, payment, notify, linking, reports, audit"]
  end

  DB[("MySQL 8.4 managed: app data and session store")]

  ADM --> MW
  STF --> MW
  MW --> PAGES --> SVC
  MW --> API --> SVC
  SVC --> DB
  TEN --> LP
  LP --> HOOK --> SVC
  SVC -->|"push and multicast"| LP
  LP --> TEN
  CRON -->|"daily 09:00 Asia/Bangkok"| JOB --> SVC
  CRON -->|"every 10 minutes"| HZ
One process, one database, two inbound integrations. Nothing is scheduled inside the process and no state lives in process memory. The deployment topology — GitHub, Render, Aiven, cron-job.org, the LINE channel and the office printer — is drawn in §6.3.1.

Request path, in order. helmet → express.static('/public') (before the session, so assets never touch MySQL) → express-session (MySQL store, httpOnly, SameSite=Lax, Secure in production, rolling 8 h) → csrf (issue on GET, verify on every POST) → requireAuth → requireRole('admin'|'staff') → zod parse → route handler → exactly one service call → EJS render or JSON → middleware/errors.js as the last handler.

Three routes bypass that chain, each with its own authentication (D-29, D-32, D-37): POST /line/webhook (X-Line-Signature), POST /internal/jobs/daily (X-Job-Token) and GET /healthz (public, one SELECT 1). The login rate limiter never applies to any of the three.

The transaction rule

A service opens a transaction for anything that changes money or bill state, writes its audit row on the same connection (D-38), commits, and only then talks to LINE. A LINE timeout can never roll back a payment (D-30).

Two environments, deliberately. Local (.env, local MySQL, LINE test mode ON, webhook disabled) and production (Render + Aiven, LINE test mode ON by default per D-34, switched off only for the rehearsed demo). There is no staging environment — a third environment is a third place for the webhook URL to be wrong. Environment variables: DATABASE_URL, SESSION_SECRET, LINE_CHANNEL_SECRET, LINE_CHANNEL_ACCESS_TOKEN, JOB_TOKEN, SEED_ADMIN_PASSWORD, TZ=Asia/Bangkok, NODE_ENV (plus PORT, injected by the host).

Conventions

Naming

ThingRuleExample
Tablessnake_case, pluralmeter_readings, bill_items
Columnssnake_case, singularprevious_reading, received_by_user_id
Foreign keys<singular_table>_idbill_id, actor_user_id
Generated columnsThe meaning, named for the constraint that needs themactive_bill_id, active_room_id, dedupe_key
Indexesuq_<table>_<cols> / ix_<table>_<cols>uq_bills_room_period
Page routeslowercase, plural, hyphenated/bills, /bills/:id, /readings
JSON routes/api/<resource>[/<action>], no version segment/api/bills/approve
JS modules and assetscamelCase functions and server modules, kebab-case public assetsbillStatus(), send-drain.js
EJS localssnake_case when they mirror a column, camelCase when computeddue_date, displayStatus
Test hooksdata-status="Draft|Unpaid|Paid|Overdue" on every element rendering a status (D-02)—

Page identifiers stay English in routes, code, payloads and tests; every visible string is Thai (D-02).

API style and the seven JSON endpoints

Server-rendered pages with POST-Redirect-GET are the default. A form POST goes to the page's own path and redirects on success; on failure it re-renders with the typed values preserved and an inline Thai error (D-40) — never a redirect, never a lost form. JSON lives under /api with no version segment, because there is one deployment and no external consumer. Exactly seven JSON endpoints exist, and adding an eighth is a decision, not a convenience:

MethodPathPurpose
POST/api/readingsPer-room reading save; returns the room's saved state and the X/Y counter (D-15)
POST/api/bills/generateThe generation run for a period; returns the D-18 summary counts
POST/api/bills/approveApprove 1–200 bills in one transaction; returns the result summary (D-19)
POST/api/notifications/drainPush at most one batch of queued rows; returns running counts (D-19)
GET/api/dashboard/charts?period=YYYY-MMThe four chart series (D-41)
POST/api/receipts/:id/printIncrement print_count before window.print() (D-27)
GET/api/announcements/preview-countLive {rooms} / {linked} count (D-35)

Plus the three machine endpoints listed above. Every JSON response carries exactly one top-level key — data or error — and no endpoint returns a bare array. error.code is a stable UPPER_SNAKE identifier the tests assert on; error.message is Thai and is what the user sees; error.fields appears only on a validation failure. The complete route list, payloads and the exhaustive error catalogue are §3.

Money, time and identifiers

RuleStatement
Money typesAmounts DECIMAL(10,2), rates DECIMAL(8,2), area DECIMAL(6,2), readings and units INT UNSIGNED. FLOAT and DOUBLE are forbidden schema-wide (D-16).
Money arithmeticJavaScript never multiplies, sums or rounds money. amount = ROUND(quantity * unit_price, 2) happens in the INSERT; the total is the SQL SUM of already-rounded items; there is no second rounding, no rounding to whole baht and no satang suppression. The pool sets decimalNumbers off, so mysql2 returns DECIMAL as a string.
Money rendering1,790.00 in tables, on Bill Detail and on the receipt; 1,790 บาท in LINE messages and sentence text; ฿1,790 only in this documentation set and in static mockups (D-03).
InstantsTIMESTAMP. MySQL stores it as UTC and converts on read with the session time zone; the process runs TZ=Asia/Bangkok and every pooled connection runs SET time_zone = '+07:00', so storage is UTC and every read is already Bangkok wall time — with zero conversion code (A-5).
Business datesDATE with dateStrings: true on the pool, so a JS Date constructor cannot shift due_date across a day boundary and move a bill into Overdue a day early.
PeriodsCHAR(7), YYYY-MM. String order is chronological, which is what makes ORDER BY period DESC free and index-friendly. No periods table, no open/close state (D-11).
previous_periodVARCHAR(8) on meter_readings and bill_items — it holds a period or the literal 'baseline' (D-06).
IdentifiersSurrogate id BIGINT UNSIGNED AUTO_INCREMENT everywhere except counters (name) and line_followers (line_user_id). No UUIDs anywhere except notifications.retry_key, which LINE requires in that shape. Only two identifiers are ever shown to a human: rooms.room_code and receipts.receipt_no (REC-YYYYMM-NNNNNN).
PayloadsISO 8601 dates (2026-09-05) in every payload and every database value; Thai formatting happens at render time only.

Validation in four layers, and testing in four tiers

  1. The form — inputmode="numeric", min, required, and D-13's live check that disables Save when the current reading is below the previous. Convenience only.
  2. zod at the boundary — every route parses req.body/req.query before the handler body runs, so the API message and the inline form message are the same Thai string.
  3. The service — the rules that need the database: one Active tenant, the billable-room test, end-of-tenancy gating, revert-newest-only, meter-replacement arithmetic.
  4. The database has the last word — thirteen unique keys, three stored generated columns and twenty-one CHECK constraints. This is the layer that still holds under a race, under a handler that forgot a check, and under a student running an UPDATE by hand.

Tests run on node --test with no framework and no coverage gate: unit (no database — every calculation and format, including the PRD §28 arithmetic), integration (a scratch schema and the races that lose money), and a rehearsal script that walks the demo path end to end with LINE test mode ON. A fourth tier, browser E2E, was added by amending A-9: seven Playwright scenarios covering what only a rendered page can prove, dev-only and first in the cut order. The tier contents are §6.5.

Decisions and rejected alternatives

IdDecisionRationale, in one lineRejected — and why
A-1Server-rendered EJS on one long-lived Express process; no SPA, no build stepThe only genuinely interactive surfaces are the phone reading round, the drain loop, the change calculator and the announcement preview — about 200 lines of plain JSReact/Next.js on Vercel (PRD §23 lists it): serverless cannot give the LINE webhook one stable long-lived endpoint, a hard requirement of D-29 and D-43. A bundler "just for the reading page". TypeScript, which reintroduces the build step D-43 removes.
A-2mysql2 with hand-written SQL; no ORMThe four hardest guarantees in the decision log are SQL constructs: a stored generated column with a unique index, ON DUPLICATE KEY UPDATE seq = LAST_INSERT_ID(seq+1), the greatest-effective-date rate lookup, and the display-status CASEPrisma and Sequelize — each adds a schema language, a generate/migrate step and a translation layer between what the decision log specifies and what reaches the database. Knex — a query builder for queries that are already the clearest thing in the codebase.
A-3Committed db/schema.sql + db/seed.sql, rebuilt from empty; no migration frameworkThere is no production data to preserve, and D-43/D-44 already require a committed seed that recreates the graded demo in one command; the schema is a thesis artifact examiners readA numbered migrations folder plus a runner — real value only once data must survive a schema change, which is the trigger to add it. Prisma Migrate, which requires A-2's ORM.
A-4Money arithmetic executes in SQL on DECIMAL; JavaScript formats onlyThe strongest form of D-16 is a design where JavaScript cannot do money arithmetic: mysql2 hands back strings and lib/money.js contains no operator at alldecimal.js or big.js — a dependency and a second rounding site to keep in agreement with the database. Float arithmetic anywhere: the 1789.9999999 class of demo-day defect.
A-5TIMESTAMP for instants, DATE for business dates, dateStrings: true, time_zone = '+07:00'Both halves of D-03 — UTC storage and Bangkok display — with zero conversion codeDATETIME for instants: no UTC storage and no conversion, so the rule would live only in a convention. Epoch integers. Any date library.
A-6Two roles, one middleware, and money absent from Staff responses rather than hidden in the templateHiding a column in EJS while shipping the amount in the JSON is a defect; the Staff queries do not select amounts or rates at all, and navigation is generated from the same matrixA permissions table. A third role. Template-level hiding.
A-7Three layers — routes/, services/, lib/ — and no repos/The enforceable rule is one sentence: routes do HTTP, services do transactions and rules and their own SQL, lib/ is pure functionsA four-layer routes → services → repos → db: a passthrough for most tables and an abstraction with exactly one implementation. A models/ folder — A-2's ORM under another name.
A-8A 25-line CSRF synchronizer token in middleware/csrf.jsA random token in the session compared with crypto.timingSafeEqual — unit tested and explainable in the defencecsurf (deprecated and unmaintained). csrf-sync — a dependency for twenty-five lines. Double-submit cookies with no session component, weaker for no saving.
A-9node:test plus built-in fetch and a 15-line cookie helperNode 24 ships the runner, the assertions and an HTTP client; what matters is the list of tests, not the harnesssupertest — its only contribution is a cookie jar. Jest or Vitest — a config file and a transform for a project with no build step. Playwright — a browser download and a week of setup in a semester that needs the twelfth screen. This is the one item since amended: admitted as a dev-only tier 4 at 2 person-days in M8, on the terms set out in §6.5.1, and first in the cut order.
A-10Correctness lives in the database: thirteen unique keys, three generated columns, twenty-one CHECKsENG-1 makes D-10 true by construction, which is what lets the billable-room test read an unambiguous state and lets generation join a single tenant with no LIMIT 1 that could silently pick the wrong oneEnforcing uniqueness in a service with a SELECT then an INSERT — a race with a guaranteed loser. Application-level advisory locks as the primary guard. Trusting the disabled button.
A-11JSON under /api with no version segment; page forms use POST-Redirect-GETOne deployment, no external consumer, and seven JSON endpoints that exist only where a page must not reload/api/v1 — a number nobody will bump. A JSON API for every action with the pages calling it — doubles the surface and makes every form a fetch. REST purity: POST /api/bills/approve is an action, and naming it one is clearer than modelling approval as a resource.
A-12The LINE binding is three columns on tenants; there is no line_accounts tableD-29 keys uniqueness to the tenant, which tenants.id satisfies by construction; PRD §21's LineAccounts entry is descriptive, not normativeA separate line_accounts table with UNIQUE(tenant_id) — the same constraint, one more join on every send.

A-13 · Recorded assumptions

  1. Versions above are floors at kickoff; package-lock.json plus npm ci reproduce the build.
  2. bcryptjs substitutes for native bcrypt — same algorithm, same cost 10 (D-37), no compiler on the deploy host.
  3. GET_LOCK is available on the managed MySQL; the documented fallback is FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED (risk R-09).
  4. X-Line-Retry-Key is settable on the pinned SDK major; if not, it is set through the SDK's HTTP config, and if that fails, idempotency layers 1, 2, 4 and 5 still stand.
  5. The A5 print output is verified on a real office printer by week 9. Browser print CSS is the one part of this stack that cannot be unit tested.
  6. One process is enough — every acceptance number in D-45 is a single-instance figure, and every guard lives in the database anyway.
  7. PRD §12's grant of Confirm Cash Payment to เจ้าหน้าที่ is a drafting error; PRD §4 and D-36 win, and every document flags it. This is the single place a decision overrides literal PRD text.
  8. PRD §16's 18,500 and 62,500 and PRD §19's 35 ตร.ม. row are illustrative and are never used as expected values in a seed or a test (D-44).
  9. Nothing in the architecture reintroduces a PRD §24 exclusion.

A-14 · The agreed cut order, decided now rather than in week 14

The demo path is never cut: Login → จดมิเตอร์ → generate → อนุมัติและส่งบิล → LINE BILL → ยืนยันรับเงินสด → receipt with its number and print view → LINE PAID → the Dashboard money tiles.

Cut in this order if the semester runs short. The Retires column names what each cut removes from the requirement set, so nobody discovers in week 14 that a cut silently dropped PRD scope.

#CutRetires
1Report 4, ห้องที่ใช้น้ำ/ไฟสูงสุดPRD scope FR-29 in part, and PRD §17's ห้องที่ใช้น้ำสูงที่สุด / ห้องที่ใช้ไฟสูงที่สุด entirely — report 4 is their only realisation (§4.13.1)
2CSV export on all four reports, screens keptPRD scope FR-43, and half of FR-29's acceptance sentence
3Two of the four dashboard charts, keeping 12-month water units and 12-month revenueFR-30's chart half only; the per-room table still satisfies function 30
4The floor-scoped announcement audience, shipping ทุกห้องที่มีผู้เช่า onlyNothing PRD-mandated — D-35 offers the audience as an option
5Report 3, ประวัติรายห้อง 12 เดือนPRD scope FR-25 (PRD §18 function 25) entirely — it is the only per-room payment history — and the per-room half of FR-30 (function 30)
6The meter-replacement branch, keeping the hard block on current-below-previousNothing PRD-mandated
7The soft usage flagsNothing PRD-mandated
8The Bill Detail audit timeline UI, keeping the audit_log writesNothing PRD-mandated — the rows are still written (D-38)
9Bulk approve, keeping Bill Detail's single approve — but only if the daily job survives, since it is the delivery safety netNothing PRD-mandated; approval itself is never cut

Cuts 1, 2 and 5 retire PRD-mandated scope and must be declared to the advisor before they are taken; cuts 3, 4, 6, 7, 8 and 9 do not. The three-student fallback of §6.4.6 mandates cuts 1–3 at the start of M7, so that team declares cuts 1 and 2 in week 12.

Never cut, in any circumstance

The thirteen unique keys, three generated columns and twenty-one CHECKs of validation layer 4 · the audit rows on approve, payment and void · LINE test mode defaulting ON · the derived Overdue helper · seed.sql. Each costs days to retrofit, and each is a class of bug that stays invisible until it has already produced a wrong number.

1. System Overview & Requirements

Juristic Platform is a server-rendered web application that lets the juristic person of a single apartment building charge each room for water, electricity and the common area fee, collect the money in cash at the office counter, issue a numbered receipt, and keep every tenant informed through a LINE Official Account. It does not charge rent and it holds no lease data (PRD §24, D-05).

This section fixes what the system must do and how well, in terms a sibling section can implement, test or draw against without asking a question. Names, stack, table names, enum values and conventions are taken from ADR-001 and are not restated here where the ADR already binds them; every rule that comes from the product decision log cites its decision id (D-xx).

1.1 Purpose and operating model

The building runs on a monthly rhythm. Staff walk the meters in the first days of a month, the office generates and approves that month's bills, LINE delivers them, tenants pay cash at the counter, and the Dashboard and reports explain the month afterwards. One calendar month is one billing period — a plain YYYY-MM string, with no open/close state machine anywhere in the system (D-11).

Product name
Juristic Platform (English identifier). The running app's header shows the apartment name from Settings; the receipt letterhead carries only the apartment's own identity (D-01).
Scale
One property, one building, 80–300 rooms (D-04). The seeded demo is 80 rooms — 67 Occupied, 13 Vacant (D-44).
Users
Two staffed roles (admin, staff) plus tenants who never log in (D-36).
Money
THB only, DECIMAL only, cash only. 1,790.00 in tables and on the receipt, 1,790 บาท in LINE and sentence text (D-03, D-16, D-24).
Time
Asia/Bangkok everywhere, Gregorian years (2026), ISO 8601 in every payload (D-03).
Language
Thai UI, English identifiers. No i18n framework, no language switcher (D-02).
Delivery target
3–4 students, one semester, free-tier hosting, one deployable process, zero build steps (ADR §1, D-43).

The system's reason to exist is the arithmetic and the audit trail around it. Everything else is a screen over it:

units = current_reading − previous_reading  ·  amount = ROUND(quantity × unit_price, 2)  ·  total = SUM(three bill items)

Worked once, with the numbers this document set uses everywhere (PRD §28, D-44):

ค่าน้ำ   1,260 − 1,250 = 10 หน่วย × 18.00 = 180.00
ค่าไฟ   5,350 − 5,280 = 70 หน่วย × 8.00 = 560.00
ค่าส่วนกลาง   30.00 ตร.ม. × 35.00 = 1,050.00
ยอดรวม   180.00 + 560.00 + 1,050.00 = 1,790.00 บาท  ·  ห้อง 205  ·  กำหนดชำระ 5 ก.ย. 2026
Binding: the arithmetic is reproducible, the calendar is not

Every document uses the PRD §28 values verbatim in examples — including 5 ก.ย. 2026, 3 ก.ย. 2026 and REC-202609-000852. The live demo reproduces the arithmetic (10 หน่วย, 70 หน่วย, 180, 560, 1,050, 1,790), never the dates, because the due date, the Overdue display and the receipt number are all functions of the run date (D-20, D-21, D-27, D-44). PRD §16's 18,500 and 62,500 and PRD §19's 35 ตร.ม. row are illustrative and are never used as expected values.

role staff		 เจ้าหน้าที่ / ช่าง Web app, Chrome for Android, 360 × 640 minimum Same session mechanism, different role column Reduced ภาพรวม (room counts and this period's reading progress), read-only ห้องพัก, จดมิเตอร์ create/edit incl. meter replacement, ส่งให้นิติตรวจสอบ, a room's reading history (D-36, D-13, D-18) Any amount, any rate, any payment, any receipt, ผู้เช่า, ผู้ใช้งานระบบ, ตั้งค่า — absent from the response body, not hidden in the template (D-36)

machine		 — cron-job.org, two schedules X-Job-Token header on the daily job; /healthz is public (D-32, D-43) POST /internal/jobs/daily at 09:00 Asia/Bangkok; GET /healthz every 10 minutes No session, no pages
LINE platform
machine		 — Webhook → the app's one fixed HTTPS origin X-Line-Signature validated on the raw bytes; a bad signature is 401 and nothing else happens (D-29) Deliver follow and message events No session, no pages
One place a decision overrides literal PRD text

PRD §12 says เจ้าหน้าที่ presses Confirm Cash Payment. That contradicts PRD §4, which grants รับชำระเงิน and ออกใบเสร็จ to Admin only. §4 wins: the counter is operated by an Admin login (D-36, assumption A-13.7). Every document flags this; the test plan asserts a 403 for Staff on POST /payments.

1.4 The monthly cycle, and where each requirement sits

flowchart LR
  P1["Reading round, FR-10 FR-11, staff on a phone"]
  P2["Auto units and amounts, FR-12 to FR-16, computed in SQL"]
  P3["Generation, FR-17, one Draft bill per billable room"]
  P4["Approval, FR-18, Admin only, one gate"]
  P5["Delivery, FR-19, queue then drain"]
  P6["Reminders, FR-20 FR-21, daily job at 09:00"]
  P7["Cash at the counter, FR-22 FR-23"]
  P8["Receipt, FR-24, number allocated in the transaction"]
  P9["Dashboard and reports, FR-28 to FR-30"]
  P10["Settlement, FR-36, end of tenancy"]

  P1 --> P2 --> P3 --> P4 --> P5 --> P7 --> P8 --> P9
  P5 --> P6 --> P7
  P8 --> P10
  P4 -.->|"correction: revert to Draft, FR-34"| P3
  P8 -.->|"correction: void payment, FR-35"| P7
Nine forward phases and two sanctioned correction paths. Both corrections are Admin-only, both require a Thai reason of at least 10 characters, and both are audited inside the transaction that makes the change (D-22, D-25, D-38).

1.5 Functional requirements

FR-01 … FR-30 map one-to-one, in order, to the thirty functions of PRD §18. FR-31 and above are requirements the decision log adds that PRD §18 does not name. Each acceptance criterion is one sentence a tester can execute against the D-44 seed; "Module" is the file that owns the behaviour under ADR §5.

IDPRD §18 functionAcceptance criterion — one testable sentenceModuleSources
FR-011. Login Correct credentials create a session row and land Admin on ภาพรวม and Staff on จดมิเตอร์; the 11th failed attempt from one IP within 15 minutes is refused without touching the password hash. routes/auth.jsD-37, D-36, D-02
FR-022. Role Management For every Admin-only route a Staff session receives HTTP 403 and the Thai 403 page, and no amount, rate or total appears anywhere in a Staff response body. middleware/auth.jsD-36, D-40
FR-033. จัดการผู้ใช้งานระบบ Admin creates a staff user with a unique ชื่อผู้ใช้; setting สถานะ = Inactive blocks login while an existing receipt still prints that user's ชื่อที่แสดง as ผู้รับเงิน; ตั้งรหัสผ่านใหม่ is the only recovery path. routes/users.jsD-46, D-37
FR-044. จัดการข้อมูลห้อง Creating room 205 with floor 2, area 30.00 ตร.ม. and baselines 1,250 / 5,280 persists and lists under ห้องพัก; a duplicate room_code is refused with an inline Thai field error; a room referenced by any bill offers ปิดใช้งานห้อง and no delete. routes/rooms.jsD-47, D-04, D-06
FR-055. จัดการข้อมูลผู้เช่า A second Active tenant on room 205 is refused by the database, not by a service; เบอร์โทรศัพท์ must match 0XXXXXXXXX; LINE User ID is read-only on the form in every state. routes/tenants.jsD-10, D-29, D-36
FR-066. บันทึกขนาดพื้นที่ห้อง Area is DECIMAL(6,2), Admin-only, and every change writes a ROOM_AREA_CHANGE audit row; a bill approved at 30.00 ตร.ม. still prints 30.00 × 35.00 = 1,050.00 after the room is later changed to 32.00. routes/rooms.jsD-09, D-47, D-38
FR-077. ตั้งอัตราค่าน้ำ Saving น้ำ 18.00 INSERTs a rates row and never UPDATEs one; two saves on the same day for the same utility are refused by uq_rates(utility_type, effective_from). routes/settings.jsD-17, D-16
FR-088. ตั้งอัตราค่าไฟ Same rule for ไฟ 8.00; generation for period 2026-09 resolves the row with the greatest effective_from ≤ 2026-09-01, so a rate saved on 20 Sep never disturbs a September bill already approved. routes/settings.jsD-17
FR-099. ตั้งอัตราค่าส่วนกลาง Same rule for ส่วนกลาง 35.00 บาท/ตร.ม.; if Draft bills exist for a period a back-dated rate touches, Settings shows มีบิลฉบับร่าง {N} ใบในรอบนี้ ยังใช้อัตราเดิมอยู่ with a คำนวณบิลฉบับร่างใหม่ button. routes/settings.jsD-17, D-18
FR-1010. กรอกเลขมิเตอร์น้ำ Saving water current 1,260 for room 205 period 2026-09 stores previous 1,250 (read-only on screen), previous_period and units 10 in one row, and a double-submitted save UPSERTs to the same row rather than creating a second. routes/readings.jsD-12, D-14, D-15
FR-1111. กรอกเลขมิเตอร์ไฟ Same for electricity 5,350 over 5,280 = 70 units; the room counts as จดแล้ว and increments the sticky counter (จดแล้ว 42/67) only once both utilities are saved for that period. routes/readings.jsD-15, D-12
FR-1212. คำนวณหน่วยน้ำอัตโนมัติ Units are computed and stored at save time as a snapshot: 1,260 − 1,250 = 10; a current below previous is refused 422 READING_BELOW_PREVIOUS for every role unless the meter-replacement branch is used, and CHECK (units >= 0) holds regardless. services/billing.jsD-13, D-14, D-16
FR-1313. คำนวณหน่วยไฟอัตโนมัติ Same path for electricity: 5,350 − 5,280 = 70 units; with no earlier reading the room's baseline_electric applies and previous_period stores the literal 'baseline'. services/billing.jsD-06, D-14
FR-1414. คำนวณค่าน้ำ The water bill item stores quantity 10, unit_price 18.00 and amount ROUND(10 × 18.00, 2) = 180.00, computed in SQL — no JavaScript multiplies money anywhere in the path. services/billing.jsD-16, D-17
FR-1515. คำนวณค่าไฟ The electricity bill item stores 70 × 8.00 = 560.00 by the same expression, with the rate copied onto the item so every later screen renders the snapshot and never joins live to rates. services/billing.jsD-16, D-17
FR-1616. คำนวณค่าส่วนกลางตามขนาดห้อง The common item stores quantity = the bill's snapshotted area 30.00 and unit_price 35.00, amount 1,050.00; a Vacant room produces no common item and no bill at all. services/billing.jsD-07, D-09, D-16
FR-1717. สร้างบิลรายเดือน A run creates one Draft bill per billable room with exactly three items and total 1,790.00 for room 205; running it a second time returns สร้างบิลใหม่ 0 ใบ, recalculates Drafts in place and leaves every Unpaid and Paid bill untouched. services/billing.jsD-18, D-07, D-23
FR-1818. ตรวจสอบและอนุมัติบิล อนุมัติและส่งบิล (Admin only) moves the bill Draft → Unpaid, freezes every snapshot, sets due_date = MAX( LEAST(due day, last day of the period month), approval + 3 days ) — 5 Sep 2026 in the worked example (§4.8.2; the clamp binds the due-day candidate only) — and writes a BILL_APPROVE audit row in the same transaction; a second approve affects 0 rows and changes nothing. routes/bills.jsD-19, D-20, D-22, D-38
FR-1919. ส่งบิลผ่าน LINE OA Approval queues exactly one notifications row per bill inside the transaction and sends only after commit; the result screen drains at most 20 pushes per call and reports อนุมัติ {N} ใบ · ส่ง LINE สำเร็จ {x} · ยังไม่ได้ส่ง (ไม่มี LINE) {y} · ส่งไม่สำเร็จ {z}. services/notify.jsD-19, D-30, D-31, D-34
FR-2020. แจ้งเตือนวันครบกำหนด The 09:00 job queues one PRE_DUE per Unpaid bill whose due_date − today = 2 (3 Sep 2026 for a 5 Sep due date) and nothing else, and a second run the same day sends nothing. services/jobs.jsD-32, D-33
FR-2121. แจ้งเตือนยอดค้างชำระ The job queues OVERDUE only when days_overdue IN (1, 8, 15) — 6, 13 and 20 Sep 2026 for a 5 Sep due date — skipping Paid bills and bills whose latest payment was voided, so a bill receives at most four automatic messages in its entire life. services/jobs.jsD-33, D-25, D-21
FR-2222. รับชำระเงินสด ยืนยันรับเงินสด (Admin only) records one payment of exactly the bill total 1,790.00 — never an input field — with method='cash' and received_by_user_id; a double click produces one payment and the second call returns 409 บิลนี้ถูกชำระไปแล้ว. services/payment.jsD-24, D-26, D-36
FR-2323. เปลี่ยนสถานะ Paid / Unpaid The status change is UPDATE bills SET status='Paid' WHERE id=? AND status='Unpaid'; Overdue is never written by anything, and voiding a payment returns the bill to Unpaid, which then displays as เกินกำหนด if the due date has passed. services/payment.jsD-21, D-25, D-26
FR-2424. ออกใบเสร็จ The receipt number is allocated inside the payment transaction and renders as REC-202609-000852; the A5 view carries D-27's fields in order — letterhead (apartment name, address, โทร, and เลขประจำตัวผู้เสียภาษี only when set), the title ใบเสร็จรับเงิน, เลขที่ REC-202609-000852, วันที่ชำระ 3 กันยายน 2026, ห้อง 205, ชื่อผู้เช่า from the bill snapshot, รอบบิล กันยายน 2026, the three line items each with quantity × unit price = amount, ยอดรวม 1,790.00 บาท with the Thai amount in words, ชำระโดย เงินสด, ผู้รับเงิน and the ลงชื่อผู้รับเงิน signature line — and fits one page (the field list FR-38 subtracts from). routes/receipts.jsD-26, D-27, D-28
FR-2525. ดูประวัติการชำระ A room's history lists every bill with its period, total, display status, payment date and receipt number — including bills belonging to a previous, now Inactive tenant — and a voided payment still appears, stamped ยกเลิก. services/reports.jsD-08, D-25, D-42
FR-2626. ดูประวัติมิเตอร์ A room's reading history lists each period's previous, current and units per utility, shows 'baseline' as the source for the first period, and renders a skipped month as a gap rather than as a zero row. services/reports.jsD-14, D-06, D-42
FR-2727. ส่งประกาศผ่าน LINE OA An announcement (หัวข้อ ≤ 60, เนื้อหา ≤ 400) sent to ทุกห้องที่มีผู้เช่า writes one notifications row per recipient tenant, is delivered by multicast in chunks of at most 500 userIds, and is thereafter immutable — no edit, no resend, no recall. routes/announcements.jsD-35, D-30, D-34
FR-2828. Dashboard For the selected period the tiles reconcile on screen: ยอดเรียกเก็บทั้งหมด − รับชำระแล้ว = ค้างชำระ, money figures counting only bills whose stored status is Unpaid or Paid; against the seed ห้องทั้งหมด 80, มีผู้เช่า 67, ห้องว่าง 13 and ค่าส่วนกลาง 69,300.00 (66 × 30 × 35) before the demo approval, 70,350.00 (67 × 30 × 35) immediately after room 205 is approved. services/reports.jsD-41, D-44
FR-2929. รายงาน Exactly four reports exist, each with a CSV export (UTF-8 with BOM, report-{name}-{period}.csv); in รายการค้างชำระ the four ageing bucket counts sum to the row count and the four bucket amounts to the report total. routes/reports.jsD-42
FR-3030. ดูข้อมูลการใช้น้ำและไฟย้อนหลัง Twelve-month water-unit and electricity-unit charts render for the building and a 12-period table renders for one room; a period with no reading is plotted as a gap, never as 0. services/reports.jsD-41, D-42, D-14
Additional requirements the decision log creates — not named in PRD §18
FR-31LINE account linking Admin issues a printable 6-character code (alphabet ABCDEFGHJKLMNPQRSTUVWXYZ23456789, 7 days, single use); the tenant sends it to the OA and the webhook binds the userId to that tenant, expires the code, audits TENANT_LINE_LINK and replies naming ห้อง 205 — a wrong or expired code gets LINK_FAIL and binds nothing. services/linking.jsD-29
FR-32Notification log and delivery state Every intended message is one row carrying type, recipient, status, attempt_count, http_status and last_error; the same message type for the same bill and tenant on the same day can never produce two rows, and Monthly Billing badges each bill with D-30's three labels — ส่งแล้ว / ยังไม่ได้ส่ง — ไม่มี LINE / ส่งไม่สำเร็จ — rendering — while a row is still queued or the bill is Draft. services/notify.jsD-30, ADR §7.6
FR-33Meter replacement With เปลี่ยนมิเตอร์ในรอบนี้ ticked, both roles may enter old_final and new_start with a required Thai reason; units = (old_final − previous_reading) + (current_reading − new_start), validated as old_final ≥ previous_reading AND current_reading ≥ new_start, and the row is audited as METER_REPLACEMENT. routes/readings.jsD-13, D-38
FR-34Revert a bill to Draft ตีกลับเป็นฉบับร่าง (Admin, reason ≥ 10 Thai characters) returns an Unpaid or Overdue bill to Draft and unfreezes its readings, and is refused server-side — not merely disabled — when a later bill exists for that room. routes/bills.jsD-22, D-12
FR-35Void a payment ยกเลิกการชำระเงิน (Admin, reason ≥ 10 characters) marks payment and receipt voided without deleting either — the number 000852 stays taken — returns the bill to Unpaid, sends no LINE message, and suppresses that bill from automatic PRE_DUE and OVERDUE sends with the badge ระงับการแจ้งเตือนอัตโนมัติ. services/payment.jsD-25, D-33
FR-36End of tenancy as a settlement สิ้นสุดสัญญาเช่า is blocked unless a final reading exists for both utilities and the bill covering it is Paid; the successful action sets the tenant Inactive, stamps วันที่ย้ายออก, clears the LINE binding and sets the room Vacant in one transaction, audited as TENANCY_END with the reason. routes/tenants.jsD-08, D-10, ADR ENG-5
FR-37Scheduled job and manual trigger POST /internal/jobs/daily with a valid X-Job-Token writes one job_runs row per day and is a no-op on a repeat; Settings' รันงานแจ้งเตือนตอนนี้ runs the identical code as daily_manual, skips that guard, and reports ส่งแจ้งเตือนก่อนครบกำหนด {a} ราย · เลยกำหนด {b} ราย · ส่งซ้ำที่ล้มเหลว {c} ราย. routes/internal.jsD-32, D-33
FR-38Printable ใบแจ้งค่าใช้จ่าย Any non-Draft bill offers พิมพ์ใบแจ้งหนี้ on the same A5 route as the receipt, carrying the letterhead, ห้อง, ชื่อผู้เช่า, รอบบิล, กำหนดชำระ and the same three line items — and no receipt number, no payment date, no ผู้รับเงิน and no signature line. routes/bills.jsD-28, D-30
FR-39Audit trail and bill timeline Seventeen action types write an audit_log row on the same connection as the change they record (D-38's thirteen entries, with ROOM_CREATE/ROOM_EDIT and TENANT_LINE_LINK/TENANT_LINE_UNLINK split, plus TENANCY_END per ENG-5 and ROOM_ACTIVATE for the reinstatement counterpart of §3.2.3 — the audit_log.action enum of §1.8.1); Bill Detail's Admin-only timeline is one indexed query on (bill_id, created_at) with no join. lib/audit.jsD-38, ADR §3.3
FR-40LINE test mode and quota display โหมดทดสอบ LINE defaults ON in every environment and redirects every outbound message to one whitelisted userId while still recording the intended recipient; Settings shows ส่งข้อความเดือนนี้: {N}/500 counting only sent rows this month, in warn colour above 450, and throttles nothing. services/notify.jsD-34, D-05
FR-41Opening readings on the room เลขมิเตอร์น้ำเริ่มต้น, เลขมิเตอร์ไฟเริ่มต้น and วันที่เริ่มต้น are mandatory on the Rooms form, are Admin-only, are editable only while the room has no reading row at all, and never produce a synthetic meter_readings row. routes/rooms.jsD-06, D-14
FR-42Missing-reading visibility Monthly Billing shows a persistent warn callout listing every billable room with no bill — ยังไม่ได้จดมิเตอร์ 3 ห้อง: 108, 312, 405, each code linking to that room's reading form — approval is not blocked, and the count must reach 0 for the period to be complete. routes/bills.jsD-23, D-11
FR-43CSV export All four reports export through one helper producing UTF-8 with a BOM so Excel opens Thai correctly, with already-shaped rows and the filename pattern report-{name}-{period}.csv. lib/csv.jsD-42
FR-44Soft usage flags ใช้สูงผิดปกติ appears only when the room's previous-period usage for that utility was ≥ 10 units and the current usage exceeds 3× it; ไม่มีการใช้งาน appears on 0 units for a room with an Active tenant; neither ever blocks a save, a generation run or an approval. routes/readings.jsD-13
FR-45Room deactivation A room referenced by any bill can only be deactivated, not deleted; a deactivated room is excluded from billing and from every list except an explicit ปิดใช้งาน filter, and the action is audited as ROOM_DEACTIVATE. Deactivation is reversible: เปิดใช้งานอีกครั้ง on Room detail posts /rooms/:id/activate and is audited as ROOM_ACTIVATE. routes/rooms.jsD-47, D-09
FR-46Confirmation dialogs All six irreversible actions use the one shared dialog — never the browser's confirm() — stating the exact effect with counts in Thai and repeating the verb on the primary button; the void and end-of-tenancy dialogs keep that button disabled until a reason of at least 10 characters is typed. public/js/confirm-dialog.jsD-39
FR-47Health endpoint GET /healthz is public, runs one SELECT 1, returns 200 with no session and no rate limiting, and is pinged on its own 10-minute schedule that is separate from the daily job. routes/internal.jsD-43, D-32
FR-48Standard failure treatment A failed save re-renders the form with every typed value preserved and an inline Thai error plus ลองอีกครั้ง — never a redirect and never a full-page reload on the phone reading screen; every empty list renders the shared empty state with its specified Thai sentence. middleware/errors.jsD-40, D-15
FR-49Change your own password Any signed-in user opens เปลี่ยนรหัสผ่าน from the account block of their own navigation and submits รหัสผ่านปัจจุบัน, รหัสผ่านใหม่ (≥ 8 characters) and ยืนยันรหัสผ่านใหม่; a wrong current password returns 422 and leaves password_hash byte-identical, a new password under 8 characters returns รหัสผ่านต้องมีอย่างน้อย 8 ตัวอักษร and a mismatched confirmation returns รหัสผ่านไม่ตรงกัน; on success the hash is rewritten at bcrypt cost 10, the session is kept, and the old password no longer signs in. Until the user has changed the seeded password, Dashboard shows the dismissible banner คุณยังใช้รหัสผ่านเริ่มต้นอยู่ — กรุณาเปลี่ยนรหัสผ่าน; this is a change, never a self-service reset, and D-37's Admin-only ตั้งรหัสผ่านใหม่ (FR-03) stays the sole recovery path for a forgotten password. routes/auth.jsD-37, D-40, D-46

FR-49 realises D-37's "first login forces a change" without a forced redirect. The users table carries no must_change_password column and §3.7(8) refuses to invent one, so the requirement is discharged by a reachable screen plus a Dashboard banner rather than by a gate. The three test-plan cases TC-AUTH-019, 020 and 021 discharge it.

1.6 Non-functional requirements

Every number below is committed and measurable. Where a target came from the decision log it cites it; the three marked authored are added by this specification and are recorded as assumptions in §1.9.

IDQualityRequirement, with the numberHow it is verifiedSource
NFR-01Capacity One building, 80–300 rooms, 12 periods of history — the seed is 80 rooms × 12 periods ≈ 800 bills. No property scoping, no building column, no sharding. Every list page and report is exercised against the full seed.D-04, D-44
NFR-02Capacity ceiling The free LINE allowance of 500 messages/month binds first: roughly 3 messages per occupied room per month (bill + one reminder + payment confirmation) means about 160 occupied rooms is the ceiling before delivery silently stops. Above that the office needs a paid LINE plan; the software is unchanged. Settings' {N}/500 counter; listed limitation.D-34, D-05
NFR-03Performance Any list page renders in ≤ 2 s against the full seed.Stopwatch against the seed.D-45 (2)
NFR-04Performance A 67-room reading round completes on a phone in ≤ 15 min, measured from opening จดมิเตอร์ to the counter reading 67/67.Stopwatch, one operator, one phone.D-45 (1), D-15
NFR-05Performance A single อนุมัติและส่งบิล delivers the LINE bill in ≤ 30 s.Stopwatch on the demo path, test mode ON.D-45 (3)
NFR-06Performance A 67-bill bulk approval commits in ≤ 5 s and is fully delivered within ≤ 3 min — two numbers, measured separately, because approval and delivery are two round trips.Stopwatch on each half.D-45 (4), D-19
NFR-07Performance ยืนยันรับเงินสด to a printable receipt on screen in ≤ 5 s.Stopwatch on the demo path.D-45 (5)
NFR-08Availability No uptime commitment is made. The free instance sleeps after roughly 15 minutes of inactivity; a 10-minute GET /healthz ping on its own schedule keeps it awake, and POST /line/webhook answers 200 before doing any work so a cold start still ACKs inside LINE's timeout. Cron history; a cold-start webhook delivery.D-43, D-32, D-45
NFR-09Security — sessions Server-side sessions in MySQL, cookie httpOnly + SameSite=Lax + Secure in production, rolling 8 h; logout deletes the row. No JWT, no token in localStorage. Restart the instance mid-session and stay logged in.D-37
NFR-10Security — credentials bcrypt cost 10; no self-service reset; the seeded admin password comes from SEED_ADMIN_PASSWORD and seeding fails if it is unset; no secret is ever committed. Grep the repo; seed with the variable unset.D-37, D-43
NFR-11Security — request integrity A CSRF token on every POST except the two machine endpoints; 10 failed logins per IP per 15 minutes with trust proxy set so the limiter keys on the real client IP; helmet CSP allowing only cdnjs and Google Fonts. Unit test on the token compare; 11 scripted logins.D-37
NFR-12Security — machine endpoints The webhook is authenticated by X-Line-Signature over the raw bytes (bad signature → 401, nothing processed), the daily job by X-Job-Token; the login limiter never applies to either, nor to /healthz. Replay with a mangled signature and with no token.D-29, D-32, D-37
NFR-13Data integrity — money DECIMAL only; FLOAT and DOUBLE appear nowhere in the schema; JavaScript never multiplies, sums or rounds money; the printed total always equals the sum of the printed lines (180.00 + 560.00 + 1,050.00 = 1,790.00) with no second rounding. Unit test on the PRD §28 arithmetic; grep the schema.D-16, ADR A-4
NFR-14Data integrity — constraints Correctness is enforced by 13 unique keys, 3 stored generated columns and 21 CHECK constraints, so it holds under a race, under a handler that forgot a check, and under a hand-typed UPDATE. Integration tests that fire the races concurrently.ADR §6.8, D-26, D-10
NFR-15Data integrity — idempotency A repeated action never produces a second bill, a second live payment, a second receipt number, a second job run or a second message: one guard per case, each in the database. The named integration tier — double confirm, double approve, double generation, double job run, same-day resend.D-18, D-26, D-30, D-32
NFR-16Data integrity — no destructive deletes Users, tenants, payments, receipts and rooms with bills are never deleted, only Inactive / voided / deactivated, so every historical attribution and every receipt number stays resolvable forever. Reprint an old receipt after the room, tenant and cashier have all changed.D-09, D-25, D-46, D-47
NFR-17Auditability Seventeen action types write an audit_log row on the same connection as the change (D-38's thirteen entries, with ROOM_CREATE/ROOM_EDIT and TENANT_LINE_LINK/TENANT_LINE_UNLINK split, plus TENANCY_END per ENG-5 and ROOM_ACTIVATE, which is the seventeen-value audit_log.action enum); approvals, reverts, payments, voids and Draft-period reading edits also carry bill_id so one bill's whole history is one indexed query. Approve → revert → re-approve → pay → void and read the timeline.D-38
NFR-18Mobile usability (staff) Chrome for Android at 360 × 640 minimum, tap targets ≥ 44 × 44 px, inputmode="numeric" on every meter field, one save per room so a dropped connection loses at most the room being typed, and a sticky progress counter. The reading round performed on a real 360 px phone.D-45, D-15, D-40
NFR-19Desktop usability (admin) Current Chrome and Edge at ≥ 1280 px. The Admin surface is explicitly not required to be usable below 768 px. Visual check at 1280 px.D-45
NFR-20Thai language Every visible string is Thai, written directly in the templates — no i18n framework, no language switcher. English survives only as identifiers, and every element rendering a status carries data-status="Draft|Unpaid|Paid|Overdue". Text renders in Noto Sans Thai with a system fallback. Grep the templates for English copy; assert on the test hook.D-02, D-45
NFR-21Format correctness Asia/Bangkok everywhere, Gregorian years only, ISO 8601 in every payload, 1,790.00 in tables, 1,790 บาท in LINE, 3 ก.ย. 2026 in lists, 3 กันยายน 2026 in LINE and on the receipt, 14:30 น. for times. No UTC ever reaches a user and no date arithmetic happens in the browser. Unit tests on the date and money helpers.D-03
NFR-22Observability JSON lines to stdout captured by the host; one job_runs row per scheduled run with a summary; every notification row carries status, attempt count, HTTP status and last error; Settings surfaces the monthly message count; /healthz proves the database is reachable. Read the log after a failed push and after a job run.D-30, D-32, D-34, D-43
NFR-23Backup and restore db/schema.sql + db/seed.sql rebuild the entire graded dataset from an empty database in one command; a mysqldump is committed before every milestone and the day before the defence. authored Recovery target: a working demo database is back within 10 minutes from a committed dump, using the documented local fallback (local app, local MySQL, webhook disabled, test mode ON). Drop the schema and rebuild, timed.D-43, D-44
NFR-24Print output A5 portrait through the browser's own dialog, @page { size: A5 portrait; margin: 10mm }; the receipt fits exactly one A5 page with three line items, the total and the number. Verified on a real office printer by week 9. Printed page, measured.D-28, ADR A-13.5
NFR-25Accessibility basics authored Every input has a bound label; focus is always visible; status is conveyed by the badge's Thai text and never by colour alone; the confirm dialog traps focus and closes on Escape. Keyboard-only pass over the demo path.authored (§1.9)
NFR-26Maintainability authored Zero build steps; npm ci && npm run lint && npm test is the whole pipeline, run in CI against a MySQL 8.4 service container. No coverage gate. The PRD §28 arithmetic test must never go red. CI on every push.ADR §1, §6.9

1.7 Out of scope — restated and absolute

PRD §24 exclusions — never reintroduced in any document, mockup, test case or seed row (D-05)
  • ค่าเช่าห้อง — no rent of any kind, in any table, on any screen
  • ระบบสัญญาเช่า — no lease contracts, no contract dates that drive money
  • No mobile application — tenants use LINE, staff use the mobile web
  • No payment gateway
  • No credit or debit cards
  • No online banking
  • No slip upload
  • No QR payment (no PromptPay, no QR of any kind)
  • No IoT meters and no automatic meter reading — a human types every number
  • No AI and no machine learning — the anomaly flags of FR-44 are two fixed arithmetic thresholds, and calling them anything else in any document is a defect

The decision log adds twelve v1 limitations that are equally binding and must appear verbatim in every document with a Limitations section (D-05):

#LimitationConsequence in practiceSource
1No proration for mid-period move-in or move-outThe tenant Active on the generation date owes the whole period — full water, full electricity, full 1,050.00 common feeD-08
2No partial payment1,790.00 is paid in full or the bill stays รอชำระ; a tenant who is short returns laterD-24
3No late fee, interest or penaltyOverdue changes the badge and the reminder text, never the amountD-21
4No refund, credit note or balance in the systemA mis-keyed payment is voided and the cash goes back across the counterD-25
5No multi-building supportOne Settings profile, no building column, no property filterD-04
6No VAT and no tax invoiceThe document is a non-tax ใบเสร็จรับเงิน; the tax ID line is optional on the letterheadD-27
7No tenant-facing web loginTenants only ever receive LINE messages; there is no tenant portal to design or secureD-05
8No email and no SMS channelAn unlinked tenant is handed paper — ใบแจ้งค่าใช้จ่าย (FR-38)D-30
9A blocked Official Account is indistinguishable from a delivered messageLINE reports both as success, so the word "blocked" appears in no badge, report or testD-30
10Receipt numbers are unique and monotonic but not gaplessA rolled-back transaction consumes a number; no document or test may claim gaplessnessD-26, D-27
11A receipt printed from the browser's own menu carries no สำเนา markThe copy mark is driven by the พิมพ์ใบเสร็จ button, which is the only hookable eventD-27
12The 500-message monthly allowance is displayed but never enforcedExhausting it silently stops delivery; see NFR-02D-34
Also deliberately absent from v1

No period open/close state and no "closed" anywhere (D-11) · no reading status column or state machine (ADR §3.4) · no third role and no per-user permission table (D-36) · no Cancelled or Void bill status, no adjustment line item, no credit carried forward (D-22) · no per-bill editable due date (D-20) · no per-room announcement audience, no scheduling, no recall (D-35) · no global audit browser screen (D-38) · no PDF export and no report builder (D-42) · no Flex Messages, no images, no rich menu — seven plain-text LINE templates and nothing else (D-31) · no staging environment (ADR §2.2).

1.8 Domain glossary and enums

These terms are used with exactly these meanings in every sibling section. Where a term maps to a column or a helper, the code identifier is given.

Term (Thai where it is user-facing)MeaningWhere it lives
Billing period · รอบบิลOne calendar month, the string YYYY-MM (2026-09, displayed กันยายน 2026). String order is chronological. There is no periods table and no open/close state.bills.period, meter_readings.period (D-11)
Billable roomA room that is Occupied and has exactly one Active tenant on the generation date. Only billable rooms are billed, drive the reading counter, or receive a message.derived in services/billing.js (D-07)
Baseline · เลขมิเตอร์เริ่มต้นA room's opening water and electricity readings, stored on the room, never as a reading row. Editable only while the room has no readings at all.rooms.baseline_water, baseline_electric, baseline_date (D-06)
Previous readingThe current_reading of the row with the greatest period strictly earlier than the one being saved, for the same room and utility; the room's baseline when none exists.meter_readings.previous_reading (D-14)
previous_periodWhere that previous value came from: a period (2026-08) or the literal 'baseline' — hence VARCHAR(8), never 7.meter_readings.previous_period (ADR §6.7)
Units · หน่วยWhole integer consumption for one room, period and utility, snapshotted at save time. 10 หน่วย water, 70 หน่วย electricity for room 205.meter_readings.units (D-16)
Reading roundOne walk of the building for one period. Complete when the sticky counter reads Y/Y — 67/67 against the seed.จดมิเตอร์ page (D-15, D-45)
Meter replacement · เปลี่ยนมิเตอร์ในรอบนี้The single branch in unit arithmetic: units = (old_final − previous_reading) + (current_reading − new_start), with a required Thai reason. A rolled dial is entered this way; there is no rollover detection.meter_readings.meter_replaced (D-13)
Generation runOne idempotent pass over a period creating Draft bills and recalculating existing Drafts in place. Triggered by ส่งให้นิติตรวจสอบ or สร้าง/อัปเดตบิลฉบับร่าง.services/billing.js (D-18)
Bill itemOne of exactly three rows per bill — water, electric, common — always all three, even at 0 units and 0.00. Each carries quantity, the snapshotted unit price and the rounded amount.bill_items (D-18, D-41)
SnapshotA value copied onto a bill or reading at write time and rendered from there forever: room code, area, tenant name, unit prices, previous readings, units, amounts. Nothing on a bill joins live to rooms, tenants or rates.bills.*_snapshot, bill_items (D-09, D-17)
FrozenThe state of a bill from approval onward: readings, units, prices, area, tenant name, amounts, total and due date are all immutable through every endpoint. The only way back is a revert.(D-22)
Display statusThe four-value status a human sees, produced by one SQL CASE and one JS helper. Overdue is derived from Unpaid + a past due date and is never stored.lib/billStatus.js (D-21)
Due date · กำหนดชำระFrozen at approval as MAX( LEAST(due day of the period month, last day of that month), approval date + 3 days ) — the clamp applies to the due-day candidate only, so the result may fall in the following month (§4.8.2). Never hand-editable. 5 ก.ย. 2026 in the worked example.bills.due_date (D-20)
Revert · ตีกลับเป็นฉบับร่างAdmin returns an Unpaid or Overdue bill to Draft with a reason, unfreezing its readings. Allowed only on a room's most recent bill.routes/bills.js (D-22)
Void · ยกเลิกการชำระเงินAdmin marks a payment and its receipt voided — never deleted, so the receipt number stays taken — after the cash has gone back across the counter. The bill returns to Unpaid and is suppressed from automatic reminders.payments.voided_at (D-25)
Receipt numberREC-YYYYMM-NNNNNN from one continuous counter that never resets, allocated inside the payment transaction. REC-202609-000852. Unique and monotonic, not gapless.receipts.receipt_no (D-26, D-27)
Link code · รหัสผูก LINEA 6-character single-use code, valid 7 days, from the alphabet ABCDEFGHJKLMNPQRSTUVWXYZ23456789, handed to a tenant on a printed slip with the OA QR code.link_codes (D-29)
BindingThe link between one LINE userId and one tenant row. Unique per tenant, not per LINE account — one account may hold two rooms, and every message names its room.tenants.line_user_id (D-29)
Notification rowOne row per intended recipient per message — never one per API call. A multicast to 54 tenants writes 54 rows.notifications (D-30)
Dedupe keyThe stored generated column that makes "one message type per bill or announcement per tenant per day" a database fact rather than a convention.notifications.dedupe_key (ADR ENG-3)
Retry keyA UUID stored on the notification row at enqueue time and reused on every automatic attempt, so LINE deduplicates a retry whose first result was lost. The Admin's manual resend regenerates it (§5.6.4).notifications.retry_key (ADR §7.6, narrowed in §5.6.4)
DrainOne call that pushes at most 20 queued notifications and returns running counts. Bulk approval drains repeatedly; the daily job drains whatever is left.services/notify.js (D-19)
Test mode · โหมดทดสอบ LINERedirects every outbound message to one whitelisted userId while still recording the intended recipient. Defaults ON in every environment.settings.line_test_mode (D-34)
Job runOne recorded execution of the daily reminder job. daily is guarded to once per date; daily_manual deliberately is not.job_runs (D-32)
Settlement · สิ้นสุดสัญญาเช่าEnding a tenancy: blocked until the final reading exists and its bill is Paid, then one transaction sets the tenant Inactive, stamps the move-out date, clears the binding and sets the room Vacant.routes/tenants.js (D-08)
Arrears · ค้างชำระBills whose stored status is Unpaid, aged into the non-overlapping buckets 1-7 / 8-30 / 31-60 / 61+ วัน in report 2.services/reports.js (D-42)
Soft flagA non-blocking warn badge on a reading: ใช้สูงผิดปกติ or ไม่มีการใช้งาน. Never blocks a save, a run or an approval.(D-13)

1.9 Assumptions recorded by this section

Authored here, not inherited
  1. The 80–300 room range comes from the project brief, not from the PRD; the PRD and the seed both describe an 80-room building. Nothing in the schema or the queries is room-count dependent, so the range costs nothing — but the LINE allowance ceiling of NFR-02 is real and is stated rather than hidden.
  2. NFR-23's 10-minute recovery target is authored. The decision log commits to a committed seed and periodic dumps (D-43, D-44) but names no recovery time; ten minutes is what a mysql < seed.sql against a local instance actually takes, and it is the number the runbook's backup and restore procedure is held to.
  3. NFR-25 (accessibility basics) is authored. No decision covers keyboard access or non-colour status conveyance; both are cheap here because the UI is server-rendered HTML with real form controls, and dropping them would be a regression no later section could recover.
  4. NFR-26 (maintainability) restates ADR §1 and §6.9 as a committed requirement so the test plan has an ID to reference.
  5. The FR / NFR identifier scheme (FR-01…FR-48, NFR-01…NFR-26) is introduced by this section. FR-01…FR-30 are pinned to PRD §18's order and must never be renumbered; FR-31 and above may be appended to but not reordered, because the test plan and the traceability matrix key on these ids.
  6. The nine cycle phase names of §1.4 — Reading round, Generation, Approval, Delivery, Reminders, Collection, Receipt, Settlement, Reporting — are authored labels for grouping requirements. They are documentation vocabulary only: no column, route or enum uses them.
Two places where a document must not "tidy up" what looks like an inconsistency
  • previous_period is VARCHAR(8), not VARCHAR(7), because it holds the literal 'baseline' as well as a YYYY-MM period.
  • Enum casing is mixed on purpose — Draft/Unpaid/Paid and Active/Inactive are capitalised, admin/staff, water/electric/common and queued/sent/failed/skipped_no_line are lowercase, and notification types are upper snake. Test selectors and template keys already depend on it.

2. Data Model & Database

Eighteen tables on MySQL 8.4 (minimum 8.0.16 for CHECK), InnoDB, utf8mb4 throughout, accessed through mysql2/promise with hand-written prepared statements and no ORM (ADR §1, A-2). The schema is not a transcription of the object graph — it is where the rules that cost money live. Thirteen unique keys, three stored generated columns and twenty-one CHECK constraints hold under a race, under a handler that forgot a guard, and under a student running an UPDATE by hand (ADR §6.8 layer 4, A-10).

Schema management
Committed db/schema.sql + db/seed.sql, database rebuilt from empty. No migration framework, no schema_migrations (ADR A-3).
Surrogate key
id BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT on every table except counters (name), line_followers (line_user_id), settings (fixed id = 1) and sessions (session_id).
Money
DECIMAL(10,2) amounts, DECIMAL(8,2) rates, DECIMAL(6,2) area. FLOAT and DOUBLE appear nowhere (D-16).
Time
TIMESTAMP for instants (stored UTC, read back as Asia/Bangkok), DATE for business dates, dateStrings: true on the pool (D-03, ADR A-5).
Naming
Tables snake_case plural; columns snake_case singular; foreign keys <singular_table>_id; indexes uq_<table>_<cols> / ix_<table>_<cols>; foreign keys fk_<table>_<role>; checks ck_<table>_<rule> (ADR §6.1).
Deletion
Nothing on the money path is ever deleted. Retirement is a column, not a DELETE — see §2.6.6.
One deviation from the section brief, recorded rather than silently applied

The brief for this section says money is DECIMAL(12,2). D-16 is binding and says DECIMAL(10,2), and the ADR restates it in §6.4. This document uses DECIMAL(10,2). The ceiling is 99,999,999.99 THB on a single amount, against a real maximum around 3,000 THB per bill and 120,000 THB per month across 67 rooms — six orders of magnitude of headroom. No sibling section should write DECIMAL(12,2).

2.1 Entity relationship diagram

erDiagram
  users ||--o{ meter_readings : records
  users ||--o{ payments : receives
  users ||--o{ rates : sets
  users ||--o{ audit_log : writes
  rooms ||--o{ tenants : houses
  rooms ||--o{ meter_readings : has
  rooms ||--o{ bills : billed
  tenants ||--o{ bills : owes
  tenants ||--o{ link_codes : invited
  tenants ||--o{ notifications : receives
  bills ||--|{ bill_items : contains
  bills ||--o{ payments : settled
  bills ||--o{ audit_log : timeline
  payments ||--|| receipts : issues
  announcements ||--o{ notifications : broadcasts
  settings {
    tinyint id "always 1"
  }
  counters {
    varchar name "receipt"
  }
  job_runs {
    date run_date "one row per job per day"
  }
  line_followers {
    varchar line_user_id "natural key"
  }
  sessions {
    varchar session_id "express-mysql-session"
  }
Eighteen tables. The billing spine carries every foreign key; five tables stand alone by design — settings is a singleton, counters holds one row, job_runs is a scheduler ledger, line_followers is keyed by LINE's own identifier, and sessions is owned by express-mysql-session.
There is no line_accounts table

PRD §21 lists LineAccounts; that entry is descriptive, not normative. D-29 keys the binding to the tenant, and tenants.id already gives that uniqueness by construction, so the binding is three columns on tenants — line_user_id, line_display_name, line_linked_at — plus link_codes for the one-time code and line_followers for the pre-binding follow event (ADR A-12). One LINE account may be bound to two Active tenants (a parent renting two rooms); every template names the room, and each room's bill writes its own notifications row.

PRD §21 also splits rates into UtilityRates and CommonAreaRates. One rates table with utility_type ENUM('water','electric','common') replaces both: the resolution query, the effective-dating rule and the Settings screen are identical for all three, and two tables would mean two copies of D-17.
