2.3 Per-table specification

Nullability is stated per column; every column not marked NULL is NOT NULL. "Why" is the reason the column exists at all — a column with no reason is a column that should not be in a capstone schema.

2.3.1 users — accounts and receipt attribution

ColumnTypeNullWhy
idBIGINT UNSIGNED AInoReferenced by five tables; never reused.
usernameVARCHAR(50)noLogin identifier. uq_users_username — the only unique key.
display_nameVARCHAR(100)noPrints as ผู้รับเงิน on the receipt (D-27, D-46). Not derivable from username.
password_hashCHAR(60)nobcrypt cost 10 output is exactly 60 characters (D-37). CHAR, not VARCHAR: a hash of another length is a bug.
roleENUM('admin','staff')noThe whole D-36 matrix. No third role, no permission table.
statusENUM('Active','Inactive')no, default ActiveAn Inactive user cannot log in but keeps every historical attribution (D-46).
created_atTIMESTAMPno, default CURRENT_TIMESTAMPUsers page ordering.

2.3.2 settings — the singleton apartment profile

ColumnTypeNullWhy
idTINYINT UNSIGNEDno, default 1ck_settings_singleton CHECK (id = 1) makes a second property impossible to insert (D-04). Every read is WHERE id = 1.
apartment_nameVARCHAR(120)noHeader, receipt letterhead, LINE signature — นิติบุคคล {apartment_name} (D-01).
addressVARCHAR(255)noReceipt letterhead.
tax_idVARCHAR(13)NULLOptional — the letterhead omits เลขประจำตัวผู้เสียภาษี when empty (D-27). Not CHAR(13): an empty value must be NULL, not thirteen spaces.
phoneVARCHAR(20)noLetterhead and LINE fallback text. Landline formats vary, so not CHAR(10).
office_hoursVARCHAR(60)noA placeholder in the BILL and PRE_DUE templates, never a literal (D-31).
due_dayTINYINT UNSIGNEDno, default 5ck_settings_due_day CHECK (due_day BETWEEN 1 AND 28) — the range is what makes D-20's short-month clamp unreachable in practice.
line_test_modeTINYINT(1)no, default 1Test mode defaults ON in every environment (D-34); a stray send cannot reach a real tenant or wipe a seeded binding.
line_test_user_idVARCHAR(64)NULLThe single whitelisted recipient while test mode is on. NULL — the seeded default — means the send is swallowed: no API call, the row written sent with last_error='test mode, no whitelisted recipient', and nothing charged to the monthly 500. The rule is stated once in §5.6.5.
updated_atTIMESTAMPno, ON UPDATE CURRENT_TIMESTAMPSettings profile edits are not audited (D-38); this column is the only trace, and it is free.

2.3.3 rooms — 80 rooms and their meter baselines

ColumnTypeNullWhy
idBIGINT UNSIGNED AIno—
room_codeVARCHAR(10)noOne of only two identifiers a human ever sees (ADR §6.6). uq_rooms_room_code. Free text so A-205 needs no migration (D-04).
floorTINYINT UNSIGNEDnoEntered, never parsed from the code (D-47) — A-205 cannot be parsed. Read by D-15's floor grouping and D-35's floor audience.
room_typeVARCHAR(20)NULLPRD §5's ประเภทห้อง. Display-only label; it never affects any amount.
area_sqmDECIMAL(6,2)noMultiplicand of the common-area fee. Admin-only and audited as ROOM_AREA_CHANGE because it silently changes every future fee (D-09, D-38). ck_rooms_area CHECK (area_sqm > 0).
baseline_waterINT UNSIGNEDnoOpening meter value. Living here rather than in a synthetic reading row is what removes every first-period special case and stops a placeholder colliding with the first real reading under uq_readings_room_period_utility (D-06).
baseline_electricINT UNSIGNEDnoAs above.
baseline_dateDATEnoวันที่เริ่มต้น, shown next to the baselines so the office can see how old they are. Not used in arithmetic.
statusENUM('Occupied','Vacant')no, default VacantMaintained by the tenant transaction only, never independently editable (D-07, D-10). Half of the billable-room test.
is_activeTINYINT(1)no, default 1ปิดใช้งานห้อง. A room referenced by any bill is deactivated, never deleted, or D-09's reprint guarantee breaks (D-47).
created_atTIMESTAMPno, default CURRENT_TIMESTAMP—

2.3.4 tenants — tenancy and the LINE binding

ColumnTypeNullWhy
idBIGINT UNSIGNED AInoIts own uniqueness is D-29's per-tenant LINE binding rule.
room_idBIGINT UNSIGNEDnofk_tenants_room. A tenant record belongs to exactly one room; re-letting creates a new record (D-10).
full_nameVARCHAR(120)noSnapshotted onto every bill, so the live value may change without rewriting history (D-09).
phoneCHAR(10)noThai mobile 0XXXXXXXXX, fixed width. ck_tenants_phone CHECK (phone REGEXP '^0[0-9]{9}$') — the arrears report dials this number, so a malformed one is a defect, not a cosmetic issue.
move_in_dateDATEnoDisplayed only; no proration exists (D-08).
move_out_dateDATENULLWritten by สิ้นสุดสัญญาเช่า, never hand-edited, never future-dated (D-10).
statusENUM('Active','Inactive')no, default ActiveSet by an action, never derived from a date — so D-07's billable-room test always reads a settled value with nothing scheduled to run.
line_user_idVARCHAR(64)NULLFilled only by the webhook (D-29); staff can never type it. NULL is the common case on day one — 13 of the 67 seeded tenants (D-44).
line_display_nameVARCHAR(120)NULLCopied from line_followers at bind time so the Tenants page can show who was bound.
line_linked_atTIMESTAMPNULLShown next to the binding; NULL and line_user_id NULL always agree.
created_atTIMESTAMPno, default CURRENT_TIMESTAMP—
active_room_idBIGINT UNSIGNED, STORED generatedNULL when InactiveIF(status='Active', room_id, NULL) with uq_tenants_active_room. ENG-1: at most one Active tenant per room, enforced by InnoDB rather than by a service that could lose a race (D-10). MySQL permits repeated NULLs in a unique index, so any number of past tenants coexist.

2.3.5 meter_readings — the snapshot chain

ColumnTypeNullWhy
idBIGINT UNSIGNED AIno—
room_idBIGINT UNSIGNEDnofk_readings_room.
periodCHAR(7)noYYYY-MM. Fixed width means string order is chronological order, which is what makes the D-14 lookup an index range scan. ck_readings_period rejects 2026-13.
utility_typeENUM('water','electric')noTwo values only — common is not metered.
previous_readingINT UNSIGNEDnoSnapshot at save time, not a join. This is why D-22 restricts reverting to a room's newest bill (D-12).
current_readingINT UNSIGNEDnoWhole units only; the input rejects decimals (D-16).
unitsINT UNSIGNEDnoStored, not computed at read time, because the meter-replacement branch makes it a two-segment sum. ck_readings_units CHECK (units >= 0) is redundant against UNSIGNED and is written anyway — D-13 names it, and it documents the invariant where a reader looks for it.
previous_periodVARCHAR(8)noHolds 'baseline' or a YYYY-MM. Not VARCHAR(7), which truncates 'baseline' to 'baselin' — the ADR corrects both proposals here. Drives Bill Detail's ครอบคลุมการใช้ตั้งแต่รอบ line and the BILL template's gap note (D-14, D-31).
meter_replacedTINYINT(1)no, default 0The single branch in the whole reading path (D-13).
old_finalINT UNSIGNEDNULLเลขสุดท้ายของมิเตอร์เดิม. NULL exactly when meter_replaced = 0, enforced by ck_readings_replacement.
new_startINT UNSIGNEDNULLเลขเริ่มต้นของมิเตอร์ใหม่, defaults to 0 on the form.
replace_reasonVARCHAR(200)NULLRequired Thai reason when the box is ticked; audited as METER_REPLACEMENT (D-13, D-38). Collecting a reason and discarding it would be a defect.
recorded_byBIGINT UNSIGNEDnofk_readings_user. PRD names this column explicitly; D-38 keeps it here rather than folding it into audit_log.
recorded_atTIMESTAMPno, ON UPDATE CURRENT_TIMESTAMPAn edit is a re-save, so the timestamp follows the edit. Never used for ordering — D-14 orders by period, so a late entry resolves exactly as a punctual one would.
There is no reading status and no period table

meter_readings has no status column and no state machine, and there is no periods table and no closed state anywhere in the system (D-11, ADR §3.4). A reading's editability is derived: editable while its room's bill for that period is Draft or does not exist, frozen otherwise (D-12). Storing it would create a second source of truth that a revert would have to keep in sync.

2.3.6 rates — append-only, effective-dated

ColumnTypeNullWhy
idBIGINT UNSIGNED AIno—
utility_typeENUM('water','electric','common')noThree values — one table replaces PRD §21's UtilityRates + CommonAreaRates.
unit_priceDECIMAL(8,2)no18.00 / 8.00 / 35.00 today; 7.50 is legal, which is why the receipt's amount-in-words helper must handle satang (D-16, D-27). ck_rates_price CHECK (unit_price >= 0).
effective_fromDATEnouq_rates_utility_effective (utility_type, effective_from) makes a same-day tie impossible, and is also the index the resolution query scans backwards. No second index is needed.
created_byBIGINT UNSIGNEDnofk_rates_user; PRD names it. Every insert is audited as RATE_CHANGE.
created_atTIMESTAMPno, default CURRENT_TIMESTAMPDistinguishes "when it was decided" from "when it takes effect" — a back-dated rate has created_at > effective_from.

No row is ever UPDATEd or DELETEd. A Settings save inserts (D-17). That is the same amount of code as an update and gives Settings a complete audit story for free.

2.3.7 bills — frozen at approval, snapshotted at generation

ColumnTypeNullWhy
idBIGINT UNSIGNED AIno—
room_idBIGINT UNSIGNEDnofk_bills_room.
tenant_idBIGINT UNSIGNEDnofk_bills_tenant. The bill stays attached to the departing tenant forever (D-08); this is also the LINE recipient lookup.
periodCHAR(7)noWith room_id, the idempotency key of the whole billing run.
room_code_snapshotVARCHAR(10)noReprint fidelity (D-09): every screen, print view and LINE message renders from the bill's own snapshot and never joins live to rooms/tenants.
room_area_sqmDECIMAL(6,2)noThe area actually billed. Changing a room's area affects only bills generated afterwards.
tenant_name_snapshotVARCHAR(120)noชื่อผู้เช่า on the receipt, fixed at generation.
totalDECIMAL(10,2)no, default 0.00The SQL SUM of already-rounded items. There is no second rounding here (D-16). ck_bills_total CHECK (total >= 0).
statusENUM('Draft','Unpaid','Paid')no, default DraftThree stored values. Overdue is never stored, never written by a job, and adds no charge (D-21).
due_dateDATEnoNominal at Draft, recomputed once and frozen at approval. DATE, not TIMESTAMP: a bill is due on a day, not at an instant, and dateStrings stops a JS Date shifting it into Overdue a day early.
approved_byBIGINT UNSIGNEDNULLfk_bills_approver. NULL while Draft.
approved_atTIMESTAMPNULLck_bills_approved ties it to the status: Draft ⇒ NULL, otherwise NOT NULL. A revert clears both columns (§2.5).
created_at / updated_atTIMESTAMPnoupdated_at moves on every in-place recalculation, which is the cheap way to see whether a Draft is stale after a rate change.

2.3.8 bill_items — always exactly three

ColumnTypeNullWhy
idBIGINT UNSIGNED AIno—
bill_idBIGINT UNSIGNEDnofk_bill_items_bill, ON DELETE CASCADE. Bills are never deleted in production; the cascade exists so the integration tier can drop a scratch bill in one statement.
item_typeENUM('water','electric','common')nouq_bill_items_bill_type (bill_id, item_type) — ENG-2. A fourth item or a duplicate item is impossible, which is what makes the Dashboard tiles sum to the total, the receipt print three lines, and D-42's split columns never null.
quantityDECIMAL(10,2)noUnits for water/electric (10.00, 70.00), ตร.ม. for common (30.00). One column, one multiplication rule. ck_bill_items_quantity CHECK (quantity >= 0).
unit_priceDECIMAL(8,2)noRate snapshot, copied at generation. An approved bill is never disturbed by a later rate change (D-17).
amountDECIMAL(10,2)noROUND(quantity * unit_price, 2), computed and rounded in SQL (D-16, ADR A-4). ck_bill_items_amount CHECK (amount >= 0).
previous_readingINT UNSIGNEDNULLMeter columns for the two metered lines; NULL on common. ck_bill_items_meter enforces both directions, so a common-area line can never carry a meter number and a water line can never lack one.
current_readingINT UNSIGNEDNULLPrinted on the receipt as 1,250 → 1,260 without touching meter_readings.
previous_periodVARCHAR(8)NULLCopied from the reading so a reprint can still explain a gap-covering bill years later.

2.3.9 payments and receipts

ColumnTypeNullWhy
payments
bill_idBIGINT UNSIGNEDnofk_payments_bill.
amountDECIMAL(10,2)noAlways equal to bills.total — not an input (D-24). Because of that there is no partial payment, no overpayment, no refund and no SUM in any query. ck_payments_amount CHECK (amount > 0).
paid_atTIMESTAMPno, default CURRENT_TIMESTAMPIts month is the YYYYMM label of the receipt number.
received_by_user_idBIGINT UNSIGNEDnofk_payments_receiver; this user's display_name prints as ผู้รับเงิน.
methodENUM('cash')no, default cashOne value. The column exists because PRD §12 and D-24 name วิธีชำระเงิน on the receipt — printing a literal would be a lie about where the value comes from. PRD §24 forbids every other method.
voided_at / voided_by / void_reasonTIMESTAMP / BIGINT UNSIGNED / VARCHAR(255)NULLAll three NULL or all three set, and the reason at least 10 characters — ck_payments_void encodes D-25's dialog rule in the schema. A payment is never deleted.
active_bill_idBIGINT UNSIGNED, STORED generatedNULL when voidedIF(voided_at IS NULL, bill_id, NULL) with uq_payments_active_bill (D-26, binding). At most one live payment per bill; any number of voided ones. Must not be "simplified" to UNIQUE(bill_id), which would make a corrected bill unpayable forever, nor to a filtered index, which MySQL 8 does not have.
receipts
payment_idBIGINT UNSIGNEDnouq_receipts_payment — one receipt per payment, the second half of the double-click guard.
receipt_noVARCHAR(20)nouq_receipts_no plus ck_receipts_no CHECK (receipt_no REGEXP '^REC-[0-9]{6}-[0-9]{6}$'). 18 characters today; VARCHAR(20) leaves room without inviting a format change.
issued_atTIMESTAMPno, default CURRENT_TIMESTAMPAllocation instant, inside the payment transaction.
print_countINT UNSIGNEDno, default 00 renders the original; ≥ 1 renders สำเนา and the button reads พิมพ์สำเนา (D-27). Incremented by POST /api/receipts/:id/print before window.print().
voided_atTIMESTAMPNULLVoided, never deleted — that, and not gaplessness, is what voiding buys: the number stays taken and every render is stamped ยกเลิก (D-25).

2.3.10 notifications — one row per recipient, never per API call

ColumnTypeNullWhy
typeENUM('BILL','BILL_CORRECTED','PRE_DUE','OVERDUE','PAID','ANNOUNCEMENT')noSix values; the seventh and eighth templates (LINK_OK, LINK_FAIL) go out through the Reply API and write no row at all (ENG-4).
bill_idBIGINT UNSIGNEDNULLfk_notifications_bill. Exactly one of bill_id / announcement_id is set — ck_notifications_target.
announcement_idBIGINT UNSIGNEDNULLfk_notifications_announcement.
tenant_idBIGINT UNSIGNEDnofk_notifications_tenant. The intended recipient, recorded even when test mode redirects the send elsewhere (D-34).
recipient_user_idVARCHAR(64)NULLThe LINE userId targeted. NULL with status='skipped_no_line' is the day-one common case (D-30).
statusENUM('queued','sent','failed','skipped_no_line')no, default queuedThe three settled values are badged with D-30's three labels — ส่งแล้ว / ยังไม่ได้ส่ง — ไม่มี LINE / ส่งไม่สำเร็จ. queued has no badge of its own: it renders —, and the drain progress bar carries the in-flight state (§5.6.7). The word "blocked" appears nowhere: LINE reports a blocked recipient as success (D-05 item 9).
attempt_countINT UNSIGNEDno, default 0Automatic retries stop at 3; the Admin's manual ส่งแจ้งเตือนอีกครั้ง is never capped (D-30, D-33).
retry_keyCHAR(36)noA UUID written at enqueue and reused on every attempt as X-Line-Retry-Key. The only identifier in the system that is a UUID, and only because LINE requires that shape. Closes the gap the table cannot: a push that succeeded at LINE but whose result was lost. Automatic retries reuse it; the Admin's manual ส่งแจ้งเตือนอีกครั้ง regenerates it — a deliberate narrowing of ADR §7.6 layer 3's "reused on every attempt", recorded as a decision callout in §5.6.4.
last_error / http_statusVARCHAR(255) / SMALLINT UNSIGNEDNULLAn explicit 400 from the push endpoint is the only trigger for auto-unbinding a tenant, and never for a Useed seed id (D-30, D-44).
last_attempt_atTIMESTAMPNULLNULL means never attempted.
send_dateDATEnoAsia/Bangkok calendar day, part of the dedupe key. DATE, so two attempts 23 hours apart on the same day still collide.
created_atTIMESTAMPno, default CURRENT_TIMESTAMPEnqueue instant, distinct from last_attempt_at.
dedupe_keyVARCHAR(80), STORED generatednoCONCAT_WS(':', type, IFNULL(bill_id,0), IFNULL(announcement_id,0), tenant_id, send_date) with uq_notifications_dedupe — ENG-3. D-30's literal UNIQUE(type, bill_id, announcement_id, tenant_id, send_date) cannot enforce the rule: InnoDB treats NULLs as distinct and one of the two id columns is always NULL, so every duplicate would be accepted. Same semantics, working mechanism. Every attempt UPSERTs this row.

2.3.11 audit_log, announcements, link_codes, line_followers, counters, job_runs, sessions

TableColumnsConstraints, indexes and why
audit_logid, entity_type VARCHAR(30), entity_id BIGINT UNSIGNED, bill_id NULL, action ENUM (17 values), actor_user_id, before_json JSON NULL, after_json JSON NULL, reason VARCHAR(255) NULL, created_atix_audit_bill_created (bill_id, created_at) makes Bill Detail's Admin-only timeline exactly SELECT * FROM audit_log WHERE bill_id = ? ORDER BY created_at — no cross-entity join to invent (D-38). entity_id points at four different tables, so it carries no foreign key; ix_audit_entity (entity_type, entity_id, created_at) serves per-entity lookups instead. Rows are written on the same connection as the change they describe, inside its transaction. Append-only: no update path, no delete path, no global audit browser in v1.
announcementsid, title VARCHAR(60), body VARCHAR(400), audience ENUM('all','floors'), audience_floors VARCHAR(50) NULL, created_by, sent_atImmutable once sent — there is no edit endpoint and no recall (D-35). ck_announcements_floors pairs the two columns: all ⇒ audience_floors NULL, floors ⇒ a comma-separated list of digits. A comma-separated list rather than a join table because it is written once, read once, never queried by floor, and capped at five floors.
link_codesid, tenant_id, code CHAR(6), expires_at, used_at NULL, created_by, created_atuq_link_codes_code — the webhook looks a code up by its value alone, so it must be globally unique, not unique per tenant. ck_link_codes_alphabet pins the D-29 alphabet ABCDEFGHJKLMNPQRSTUVWXYZ23456789 (no O/0/I/1, because the code is read off a printed slip). Single use: used_at IS NULL AND expires_at > NOW() is the whole validity test.
line_followersline_user_id VARCHAR(64) PK, display_name NULL, followed_at, unfollowed_at NULLNatural primary key — LINE's identifier is the only key that exists at follow time, before any tenant is known. Upserted by the follow event so the code-matching step has a name to show. No foreign key to tenants: a follower may never bind, and a binding may outlive an unfollow.
countersname VARCHAR(20) PK, seq BIGINT UNSIGNEDExactly one row, name='receipt'. Natural key so the allocation statement needs no lookup (D-26). A second counter would need a decision, not an insert.
job_runsid, job_name ENUM('daily','daily_manual'), run_date DATE, started_at, finished_at NULL, summary_json JSON NULLuq_job_runs_name_date — a retrying scheduler cannot run the day twice (D-32). The demo button writes daily_manual and deliberately skips the guard, so a 14:00 rehearsal still works after the 09:00 cron ran; it relies on uq_notifications_dedupe instead.
sessionssession_id VARCHAR(128) PK, expires INT UNSIGNED, data MEDIUMTEXTOwned by express-mysql-session; the schema is the library's own, reproduced in schema.sql so a rebuild from empty is complete. Never touched by application code (D-37). Collation utf8mb4_bin because session ids are case-sensitive.

2.3.12 Index rationale

Every index below exists for a named query. There are no speculative indexes — 80 rooms and roughly 800 bills a year is a data set where a wrong index costs more in confusion than a table scan costs in milliseconds.

IndexServes
uq_readings_room_period_utility (room_id, period, utility_type)The D-12 UPSERT, and the correctness guarantee that a double submit is a non-event.
ix_readings_lookup (room_id, utility_type, period)The D-14 previous-reading query: an equality prefix plus a backwards range scan on period, LIMIT 1. The unique key has the wrong column order for this.
ix_readings_period (period)The Meter Reading page's per-period list and the จดแล้ว X/Y counter.
uq_rates_utility_effective (utility_type, effective_from)Both the same-day-tie constraint and the greatest-effective-date resolution scan.
uq_bills_room_period (room_id, period)The idempotency key of the entire generation run (D-18), and D-22's "is there a later bill for this room" probe.
ix_bills_period_status (period, status)Monthly Billing's list and the Dashboard money tiles for a selected period (D-41).
ix_bills_status_due (status, due_date)The daily job's two queries — PRE_DUE at due_date − 2 and OVERDUE at days_overdue IN (1,8,15) — and the arrears report's ageing (D-33, D-42).
ix_bills_tenant (tenant_id)The departing tenant's history, which must survive the tenancy (D-08).
uq_bill_items_bill_type (bill_id, item_type)ENG-2, and the item UPSERT during recalculation.
uq_payments_active_bill (active_bill_id)One live payment per bill under concurrency (D-26).
ix_payments_paid_at (paid_at)The revenue chart and the monthly collection report.
uq_notifications_dedupe (dedupe_key)ENG-3 — one message type per bill per tenant per day.
ix_notifications_drain (status, attempt_count, id)The drain batch: WHERE status='queued' OR (status='failed' AND attempt_count < 3) ORDER BY id LIMIT 20.
ix_notifications_status_send_date (status, send_date)Settings' ส่งข้อความเดือนนี้ {N}/500 counter, which counts only status='sent' (D-34).
ix_audit_bill_created (bill_id, created_at)Bill Detail's timeline in one query with no join (D-38).
ix_rooms_floor_code (floor, room_code)D-15's floor-grouped reading list and D-35's floor audience.
ix_tenants_room_status (room_id, status)The billable-room test and the per-room tenancy history.
ix_tenants_line_user (line_user_id)The webhook resolving an inbound userId to its tenancies — deliberately not unique (D-29: one account, two rooms).

2.4 Complete DDL — db/schema.sql

Runnable as-is against an empty MySQL 8.4 schema; tables are declared in foreign-key order so the file executes top to bottom with no deferred constraints. This is the whole of schema management — there is no migration runner and no schema_migrations table (ADR A-3).

-- db/schema.sql — Juristic Platform, MySQL 8.4 (minimum 8.0.16 for CHECK)
-- Rebuild: mysql -u root juristic < db/schema.sql && mysql -u root juristic < db/seed.sql

SET NAMES utf8mb4;
SET time_zone = '+07:00';
SET FOREIGN_KEY_CHECKS = 1;

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------- users
CREATE TABLE users (
  id            BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  username      VARCHAR(50)  NOT NULL,
  display_name  VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,           -- prints as ผู้รับเงิน (D-27)
  password_hash CHAR(60)     NOT NULL,           -- bcryptjs cost 10 (D-37)
  role          ENUM('admin','staff')     NOT NULL,
  status        ENUM('Active','Inactive') NOT NULL DEFAULT 'Active',
  created_at    TIMESTAMP    NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
  PRIMARY KEY (id),
  UNIQUE KEY uq_users_username (username)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci;

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------- settings
CREATE TABLE settings (
  id                TINYINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
  apartment_name    VARCHAR(120) NOT NULL,
  address           VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
  tax_id            VARCHAR(13)  NULL,           -- optional letterhead line (D-27)
  phone             VARCHAR(20)  NOT NULL,
  office_hours      VARCHAR(60)  NOT NULL,       -- a template placeholder (D-31)
  due_day           TINYINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL DEFAULT 5,
  line_test_mode    TINYINT(1)   NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,   -- ON everywhere (D-34)
  line_test_user_id VARCHAR(64)  NULL,
  updated_at        TIMESTAMP    NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
                                 ON UPDATE CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
  PRIMARY KEY (id),
  CONSTRAINT ck_settings_singleton CHECK (id = 1),
  CONSTRAINT ck_settings_due_day   CHECK (due_day BETWEEN 1 AND 28)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci;

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------- counters
CREATE TABLE counters (
  name VARCHAR(20)     NOT NULL,
  seq  BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY (name)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci;

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------- line_followers
CREATE TABLE line_followers (
  line_user_id  VARCHAR(64)  NOT NULL,
  display_name  VARCHAR(120) NULL,
  followed_at   TIMESTAMP    NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
  unfollowed_at TIMESTAMP    NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY (line_user_id)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci;

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------- rooms
CREATE TABLE rooms (
  id                BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  room_code         VARCHAR(10)      NOT NULL,   -- '205', tolerates 'A-205' (D-04)
  floor             TINYINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,   -- entered, never parsed (D-47)
  room_type         VARCHAR(20)      NULL,       -- display-only label
  area_sqm          DECIMAL(6,2)     NOT NULL,
  baseline_water    INT UNSIGNED     NOT NULL,   -- opening meter values (D-06)
  baseline_electric INT UNSIGNED     NOT NULL,
  baseline_date     DATE             NOT NULL,
  status            ENUM('Occupied','Vacant') NOT NULL DEFAULT 'Vacant',
  is_active         TINYINT(1)       NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
  created_at        TIMESTAMP        NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
  PRIMARY KEY (id),
  UNIQUE KEY uq_rooms_room_code (room_code),
  KEY ix_rooms_floor_code (floor, room_code),
  KEY ix_rooms_status (status, is_active),
  CONSTRAINT ck_rooms_area CHECK (area_sqm > 0)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci;

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------- tenants
CREATE TABLE tenants (
  id                BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  room_id           BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  full_name         VARCHAR(120) NOT NULL,
  phone             CHAR(10)     NOT NULL,
  move_in_date      DATE         NOT NULL,
  move_out_date     DATE         NULL,
  status            ENUM('Active','Inactive') NOT NULL DEFAULT 'Active',
  line_user_id      VARCHAR(64)  NULL,           -- filled only by the webhook (D-29)
  line_display_name VARCHAR(120) NULL,
  line_linked_at    TIMESTAMP    NULL,
  created_at        TIMESTAMP    NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
  -- ENG-1: at most one Active tenant per room, enforced by InnoDB (D-10)
  active_room_id    BIGINT UNSIGNED
        GENERATED ALWAYS AS (IF(status = 'Active', room_id, NULL)) STORED,
  PRIMARY KEY (id),
  UNIQUE KEY uq_tenants_active_room (active_room_id),
  KEY ix_tenants_room_status (room_id, status),
  KEY ix_tenants_line_user (line_user_id),
  CONSTRAINT fk_tenants_room FOREIGN KEY (room_id) REFERENCES rooms(id),
  CONSTRAINT ck_tenants_phone CHECK (phone REGEXP '^0[0-9]{9}$')
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci;

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------- link_codes
CREATE TABLE link_codes (
  id         BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  tenant_id  BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  code       CHAR(6)   NOT NULL,
  expires_at TIMESTAMP NOT NULL,                 -- issued_at + 7 days (D-29)
  used_at    TIMESTAMP NULL,                     -- single use
  created_by BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  created_at TIMESTAMP NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
  PRIMARY KEY (id),
  UNIQUE KEY uq_link_codes_code (code),
  KEY ix_link_codes_tenant (tenant_id),
  CONSTRAINT fk_link_codes_tenant FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(id),
  CONSTRAINT fk_link_codes_user   FOREIGN KEY (created_by) REFERENCES users(id),
  CONSTRAINT ck_link_codes_alphabet
        CHECK (code REGEXP '^[ABCDEFGHJKLMNPQRSTUVWXYZ23456789]{6}$')
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci;

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------- meter_readings
CREATE TABLE meter_readings (
  id               BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  room_id          BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  period           CHAR(7)      NOT NULL,        -- 'YYYY-MM' (D-11)
  utility_type     ENUM('water','electric') NOT NULL,
  previous_reading INT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,        -- snapshot at save time (D-12)
  current_reading  INT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  units            INT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  previous_period  VARCHAR(8)   NOT NULL,        -- 'baseline' or 'YYYY-MM' — NOT (7)
  meter_replaced   TINYINT(1)   NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  old_final        INT UNSIGNED NULL,
  new_start        INT UNSIGNED NULL,
  replace_reason   VARCHAR(200) NULL,
  recorded_by      BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  recorded_at      TIMESTAMP    NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
                                ON UPDATE CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
  PRIMARY KEY (id),
  UNIQUE KEY uq_readings_room_period_utility (room_id, period, utility_type),
  KEY ix_readings_lookup (room_id, utility_type, period),
  KEY ix_readings_period (period),
  CONSTRAINT fk_readings_room FOREIGN KEY (room_id)     REFERENCES rooms(id),
  CONSTRAINT fk_readings_user FOREIGN KEY (recorded_by) REFERENCES users(id),
  CONSTRAINT ck_readings_period      CHECK (period REGEXP '^[0-9]{4}-(0[1-9]|1[0-2])$'),
  CONSTRAINT ck_readings_prev_period CHECK (previous_period = 'baseline'
        OR previous_period REGEXP '^[0-9]{4}-(0[1-9]|1[0-2])$'),
  CONSTRAINT ck_readings_units CHECK (units >= 0),          -- D-13, stated explicitly
  CONSTRAINT ck_readings_replacement CHECK (
        (meter_replaced = 0
             AND old_final IS NULL AND new_start IS NULL AND replace_reason IS NULL
             AND current_reading >= previous_reading)
     OR (meter_replaced = 1
             AND old_final IS NOT NULL AND new_start IS NOT NULL
             AND replace_reason IS NOT NULL
             AND old_final >= previous_reading
             AND current_reading >= new_start))
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci;

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------- rates
CREATE TABLE rates (
  id             BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  utility_type   ENUM('water','electric','common') NOT NULL,
  unit_price     DECIMAL(8,2) NOT NULL,
  effective_from DATE         NOT NULL,
  created_by     BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  created_at     TIMESTAMP    NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
  PRIMARY KEY (id),
  UNIQUE KEY uq_rates_utility_effective (utility_type, effective_from),
  CONSTRAINT fk_rates_user  FOREIGN KEY (created_by) REFERENCES users(id),
  CONSTRAINT ck_rates_price CHECK (unit_price >= 0)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci;
-- Insert-only. No UPDATE, no DELETE, ever (D-17).

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------- bills
CREATE TABLE bills (
  id                   BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  room_id              BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  tenant_id            BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  period               CHAR(7)      NOT NULL,
  room_code_snapshot   VARCHAR(10)  NOT NULL,    -- D-09 reprint fidelity
  room_area_sqm        DECIMAL(6,2) NOT NULL,
  tenant_name_snapshot VARCHAR(120) NOT NULL,
  total                DECIMAL(10,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0.00,
  status               ENUM('Draft','Unpaid','Paid') NOT NULL DEFAULT 'Draft',
  due_date             DATE         NOT NULL,
  approved_by          BIGINT UNSIGNED NULL,
  approved_at          TIMESTAMP    NULL,
  created_at           TIMESTAMP    NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
  updated_at           TIMESTAMP    NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
                                    ON UPDATE CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
  PRIMARY KEY (id),
  UNIQUE KEY uq_bills_room_period (room_id, period),   -- run idempotency (D-18)
  KEY ix_bills_period_status (period, status),
  KEY ix_bills_status_due (status, due_date),
  KEY ix_bills_tenant (tenant_id),
  CONSTRAINT fk_bills_room     FOREIGN KEY (room_id)     REFERENCES rooms(id),
  CONSTRAINT fk_bills_tenant   FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id)   REFERENCES tenants(id),
  CONSTRAINT fk_bills_approver FOREIGN KEY (approved_by) REFERENCES users(id),
  CONSTRAINT ck_bills_period CHECK (period REGEXP '^[0-9]{4}-(0[1-9]|1[0-2])$'),
  CONSTRAINT ck_bills_total  CHECK (total >= 0),
  CONSTRAINT ck_bills_approved CHECK (
        (status =  'Draft' AND approved_at IS NULL)
     OR (status <> 'Draft' AND approved_at IS NOT NULL))
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci;

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------- bill_items
CREATE TABLE bill_items (
  id               BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  bill_id          BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  item_type        ENUM('water','electric','common') NOT NULL,
  quantity         DECIMAL(10,2) NOT NULL,       -- units, or ตร.ม. for 'common'
  unit_price       DECIMAL(8,2)  NOT NULL,       -- rate snapshot (D-17)
  amount           DECIMAL(10,2) NOT NULL,       -- ROUND(quantity * unit_price, 2)
  previous_reading INT UNSIGNED  NULL,
  current_reading  INT UNSIGNED  NULL,
  previous_period  VARCHAR(8)    NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY (id),
  UNIQUE KEY uq_bill_items_bill_type (bill_id, item_type),   -- ENG-2
  CONSTRAINT fk_bill_items_bill FOREIGN KEY (bill_id)
        REFERENCES bills(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  CONSTRAINT ck_bill_items_quantity CHECK (quantity >= 0),
  CONSTRAINT ck_bill_items_amount   CHECK (amount   >= 0),
  CONSTRAINT ck_bill_items_meter CHECK (
        (item_type =  'common'
             AND previous_reading IS NULL AND current_reading IS NULL
             AND previous_period IS NULL)
     OR (item_type <> 'common'
             AND previous_reading IS NOT NULL AND current_reading IS NOT NULL
             AND previous_period IS NOT NULL))
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci;

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------- payments
CREATE TABLE payments (
  id                  BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  bill_id             BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  amount              DECIMAL(10,2) NOT NULL,    -- always = bills.total (D-24)
  paid_at             TIMESTAMP NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
  received_by_user_id BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  method              ENUM('cash') NOT NULL DEFAULT 'cash',
  voided_at           TIMESTAMP NULL,
  voided_by           BIGINT UNSIGNED NULL,
  void_reason         VARCHAR(255) NULL,
  -- D-26, BINDING: one live payment per bill; voided rows are all NULL here
  active_bill_id      BIGINT UNSIGNED
        GENERATED ALWAYS AS (IF(voided_at IS NULL, bill_id, NULL)) STORED,
  PRIMARY KEY (id),
  UNIQUE KEY uq_payments_active_bill (active_bill_id),
  KEY ix_payments_bill (bill_id),
  KEY ix_payments_paid_at (paid_at),
  CONSTRAINT fk_payments_bill     FOREIGN KEY (bill_id) REFERENCES bills(id),
  CONSTRAINT fk_payments_receiver FOREIGN KEY (received_by_user_id) REFERENCES users(id),
  CONSTRAINT fk_payments_voider   FOREIGN KEY (voided_by) REFERENCES users(id),
  CONSTRAINT ck_payments_amount CHECK (amount > 0),
  CONSTRAINT ck_payments_void CHECK (
        (voided_at IS NULL     AND voided_by IS NULL     AND void_reason IS NULL)
     OR (voided_at IS NOT NULL AND voided_by IS NOT NULL
             AND void_reason IS NOT NULL
             AND CHAR_LENGTH(void_reason) >= 10))
     -- the explicit IS NOT NULL matters: without it a row with voided_at set and
     -- void_reason NULL makes the second branch UNKNOWN, and MySQL accepts a CHECK
     -- that evaluates to UNKNOWN. COALESCE(CHAR_LENGTH(void_reason),0) >= 10 is equivalent.
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci;

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------- receipts
CREATE TABLE receipts (
  id          BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  payment_id  BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  receipt_no  VARCHAR(20)  NOT NULL,             -- REC-YYYYMM-NNNNNN (D-27)
  issued_at   TIMESTAMP    NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
  print_count INT UNSIGNED NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,   -- >= 1 renders สำเนา
  voided_at   TIMESTAMP    NULL,                 -- keeps its number (D-25)
  PRIMARY KEY (id),
  UNIQUE KEY uq_receipts_payment (payment_id),
  UNIQUE KEY uq_receipts_no (receipt_no),
  CONSTRAINT fk_receipts_payment FOREIGN KEY (payment_id) REFERENCES payments(id),
  CONSTRAINT ck_receipts_no CHECK (receipt_no REGEXP '^REC-[0-9]{6}-[0-9]{6}$')
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci;

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------- announcements
CREATE TABLE announcements (
  id              BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  title           VARCHAR(60)  NOT NULL,
  body            VARCHAR(400) NOT NULL,
  audience        ENUM('all','floors') NOT NULL DEFAULT 'all',
  audience_floors VARCHAR(50)  NULL,             -- '2,3,4' when audience='floors'
  created_by      BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  sent_at         TIMESTAMP    NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
  PRIMARY KEY (id),
  KEY ix_announcements_sent (sent_at),
  CONSTRAINT fk_announcements_user FOREIGN KEY (created_by) REFERENCES users(id),
  CONSTRAINT ck_announcements_floors CHECK (
        (audience = 'all'    AND audience_floors IS NULL)
     OR (audience = 'floors' AND audience_floors IS NOT NULL
             AND audience_floors REGEXP '^[0-9]+(,[0-9]+)*$'))
     -- same UNKNOWN hole as ck_payments_void: without IS NOT NULL a 'floors'
     -- announcement with no floor list would be accepted.
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci;

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------- notifications
CREATE TABLE notifications (
  id                BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  type              ENUM('BILL','BILL_CORRECTED','PRE_DUE','OVERDUE',
                         'PAID','ANNOUNCEMENT') NOT NULL,
  bill_id           BIGINT UNSIGNED NULL,
  announcement_id   BIGINT UNSIGNED NULL,
  tenant_id         BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,    -- the INTENDED recipient
  recipient_user_id VARCHAR(64) NULL,
  status            ENUM('queued','sent','failed','skipped_no_line')
                        NOT NULL DEFAULT 'queued',
  attempt_count     INT UNSIGNED NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,   -- auto retries stop at 3
  retry_key         CHAR(36)     NOT NULL,            -- X-Line-Retry-Key (UUID)
  last_error        VARCHAR(255) NULL,
  http_status       SMALLINT UNSIGNED NULL,           -- explicit 400 => unbind
  last_attempt_at   TIMESTAMP    NULL,
  send_date         DATE         NOT NULL,
  created_at        TIMESTAMP    NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
  -- ENG-3: the enforceable form of D-30's unique key
  dedupe_key        VARCHAR(80) GENERATED ALWAYS AS (
        CONCAT_WS(':', type, IFNULL(bill_id, 0), IFNULL(announcement_id, 0),
                  tenant_id, send_date)) STORED,
  PRIMARY KEY (id),
  UNIQUE KEY uq_notifications_dedupe (dedupe_key),
  KEY ix_notifications_drain (status, attempt_count, id),
  KEY ix_notifications_bill (bill_id),
  KEY ix_notifications_status_send_date (status, send_date),
  CONSTRAINT fk_notifications_bill FOREIGN KEY (bill_id) REFERENCES bills(id),
  CONSTRAINT fk_notifications_announcement FOREIGN KEY (announcement_id)
        REFERENCES announcements(id),
  CONSTRAINT fk_notifications_tenant FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(id),
  CONSTRAINT ck_notifications_target CHECK (
        (type =  'ANNOUNCEMENT' AND announcement_id IS NOT NULL AND bill_id IS NULL)
     OR (type <> 'ANNOUNCEMENT' AND bill_id IS NOT NULL AND announcement_id IS NULL))
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci;

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------- audit_log
CREATE TABLE audit_log (
  id            BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  entity_type   VARCHAR(30)     NOT NULL,        -- 'bill','payment','room',...
  entity_id     BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,        -- polymorphic: no FK by design
  bill_id       BIGINT UNSIGNED NULL,            -- set on every bill-attributable row
  action        ENUM('BILL_APPROVE','BILL_REVERT','PAYMENT_CONFIRM','PAYMENT_VOID',
                     'RATE_CHANGE','ROOM_CREATE','ROOM_EDIT','ROOM_AREA_CHANGE',
                     'ROOM_DEACTIVATE','ROOM_ACTIVATE','READING_CREATE','READING_EDIT',
                     'METER_REPLACEMENT','TENANT_LINE_LINK','TENANT_LINE_UNLINK',
                     'TENANCY_END','ANNOUNCEMENT_SEND') NOT NULL,
  actor_user_id BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  before_json   JSON NULL,
  after_json    JSON NULL,
  reason        VARCHAR(255) NULL,               -- required on revert/void/replace
  created_at    TIMESTAMP NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
  PRIMARY KEY (id),
  KEY ix_audit_bill_created (bill_id, created_at),
  KEY ix_audit_entity (entity_type, entity_id, created_at),
  CONSTRAINT fk_audit_user FOREIGN KEY (actor_user_id) REFERENCES users(id),
  CONSTRAINT fk_audit_bill FOREIGN KEY (bill_id)       REFERENCES bills(id)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci;

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------- job_runs
CREATE TABLE job_runs (
  id           BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  job_name     ENUM('daily','daily_manual') NOT NULL,
  run_date     DATE      NOT NULL,
  started_at   TIMESTAMP NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
  finished_at  TIMESTAMP NULL,
  summary_json JSON      NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY (id),
  UNIQUE KEY uq_job_runs_name_date (job_name, run_date)   -- D-32
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci;

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------- sessions
-- Schema owned by express-mysql-session; never touched by application code (D-37).
CREATE TABLE sessions (
  session_id VARCHAR(128) COLLATE utf8mb4_bin NOT NULL,
  expires    INT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  data       MEDIUMTEXT   COLLATE utf8mb4_bin,
  PRIMARY KEY (session_id)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_bin;
What this schema makes impossible without a single line of JavaScript
  • Two Active tenants in one room · two bills for one room and period · a fourth line item on a bill
  • Two live payments on one bill · two receipts for one payment · two receipts sharing a number
  • A negative unit count · a current reading below the previous one without a declared meter replacement
  • The same message type sent to the same tenant about the same bill twice on the same day
  • The daily job running twice on one date · two rate rows for one utility on one effective date
  • A second apartment profile · a due day of 31 · an approved bill with no approved_at

2.5 Bill state machine

Three stored statuses, four displayed. Draft ฉบับร่าง Unpaid รอชำระ Paid ชำระแล้ว Overdue เกินกำหนด — the fourth is derived and is never written to a column.

stateDiagram-v2
  [*] --> Draft: generation run creates the bill, D-18
  Draft --> Draft: reading saved, bill recalculated in place, D-12
  Draft --> Unpaid: อนุมัติและส่งบิล — Admin only, freezes the bill, D-19 D-20
  Unpaid --> Paid: ยืนยันรับเงินสด — Admin only, issues the receipt, D-24 D-26
  Unpaid --> Draft: ตีกลับเป็นฉบับร่าง — Admin only, reason, newest bill only, D-22
  Paid --> Unpaid: ยกเลิกการชำระเงิน — Admin only, cash returned first, D-25
  Paid --> [*]
  note right of Unpaid
    Overdue is a DISPLAY value.
    Unpaid plus due_date before today
    renders Overdue. Nothing writes it,
    no job produces it, it adds no charge.
  end note
Two reverse edges, both Admin-only, both requiring a Thai reason of at least 10 characters, both audited inside the transaction that makes the change.
TransitionTrigger and actorGuardDatabase effectSide effects, after commit
— → Draftส่งให้นิติตรวจสอบ on Meter Reading, or สร้าง/อัปเดตบิลฉบับร่าง on Monthly Billing. Staff or Admin (D-18).rooms.status='Occupied' and exactly one Active tenant (D-07); both readings present; uq_bills_room_period.Insert bill + exactly three bill_items; total = SUM(amount); due_date = due day of the period month.None. Nothing is sent for a Draft.
Draft → DraftA reading is saved or corrected. Staff or Admin (D-12).Bill is Draft or absent — otherwise the reading is read-only.UPSERT the three items, recompute total, bump updated_at. Rate stays the period's rate (D-17).None.
Draft → Unpaidอนุมัติและส่งบิล. Admin only (D-19, D-36).UPDATE … WHERE id = ? AND status = 'Draft'; 0 rows affected counts the bill in already_approved — never a 409, so one already-approved id cannot abort a 67-bill batch (§3.3.9).Freeze; set approved_by, approved_at; recompute and freeze due_date = MAX(due day of period month, approval + 3 days); write BILL_APPROVE audit row; queue one notifications row per bill — all in one transaction.POST /api/notifications/drain pushes the BILL message, at most 20 per call. A LINE outage cannot roll back the approval (D-30).
Unpaid → Paidยืนยันรับเงินสด on Payment. Admin only (D-24).UPDATE bills SET status='Paid' WHERE id = ? AND status = 'Unpaid'; 0 rows ⇒ 409 บิลนี้ถูกชำระไปแล้ว. Then uq_payments_active_bill and uq_receipts_payment.Insert payments (amount = bills.total, method cash); allocate the receipt number from counters; insert receipts; write PAYMENT_CONFIRM audit row.Queue and push the PAID message carrying receipt_no. Receipt available at A5 print.
Unpaid → Draftตีกลับเป็นฉบับร่าง on Bill Detail. Admin only (D-22).Reason ≥ 10 characters; refused when a later bill exists: SELECT 1 FROM bills WHERE room_id = ? AND period > ? LIMIT 1.status='Draft', approved_by and approved_at cleared (required by ck_bills_approved); readings unfrozen; BILL_REVERT audit row with before/after JSON.None on the revert itself. Re-approval sends BILL_CORRECTED, whose own dedupe_key lets it go out on the same day as the original BILL.
Paid → Unpaidยกเลิกการชำระเงิน. Admin only, cash returned across the counter first (D-25).Reason ≥ 10 characters (ck_payments_void).Payment marked voided — active_bill_id becomes NULL, freeing the bill for a fresh payment; receipt marked voided but keeps its number; PAYMENT_VOID audit row.No LINE message — the office explains in person. The bill is thereafter excluded from automatic PRE_DUE/OVERDUE and shows ระงับการแจ้งเตือนอัตโนมัติ (D-25, D-33).

The display status is one expression in SQL and one function in JavaScript, and exists nowhere else (D-21):

CASE WHEN b.status = 'Unpaid' AND b.due_date < CURDATE()
     THEN 'Overdue' ELSE b.status END AS display_status

Aggregations that deliberately mean "approved" (D-41's money tiles reading status IN ('Unpaid','Paid')) or "not yet settled" (D-42's arrears ageing) read the stored column directly. That is not a violation: D-21 is a display rule.

2.6 Rules, with worked examples

2.6.1 Previous-reading resolution

One query, no special cases (D-06, D-14):

SELECT current_reading, period
  FROM meter_readings
 WHERE room_id = ? AND utility_type = ? AND period < ?   -- strictly earlier
 ORDER BY period DESC
 LIMIT 1;

A row ⇒ previous_reading / previous_period. No row ⇒ the room's baseline_water / baseline_electric with previous_period = 'baseline'.

CaseInputResolves toUnits
First ever readingRoom 205 water, period 2025-10 — the earliest seeded period; no earlier row; baseline_water = 1,140previous_reading = 1,140, previous_period = 'baseline'1,150 − 1,140 = 10
PRD §28 demoRoom 205 water, period 2026-09; 2026-08 row holds 1,250previous_reading = 1,250, previous_period = '2026-08'1,260 − 1,250 = 10
PRD §28 demoRoom 205 electric, period 2026-09; 2026-08 row holds 5,280previous_reading = 5,280, previous_period = '2026-08'5,350 − 5,280 = 70
Skipped monthPeriod 2026-11; rows exist for 2026-09 onlyprevious_period = '2026-09'; Bill Detail and the BILL message add ครอบคลุมการใช้ตั้งแต่รอบ กันยายน 2026Two months' consumption on one bill; never split, never back-dated (D-14)
Late entry2026-08 typed after 2026-09 was savedOrdering is by period, never by recorded_at — 2026-09 still resolves to 2026-08Identical to a punctual entry
Double submitThe 2026-09 save posted twiceThe strict < excludes the row being written, so the UPSERT cannot take itself as its own previousStill 10 — a non-event (D-12)
Re-let roomNew tenant's first period after a tenancy endedThe departing tenant's final reading is simply the most recent earlier rowNo baseline edit, no re-entry, no double billing (D-08)
Meter replacedprevious_reading = 1,250, old_final = 1,268, new_start = 0, current_reading = 12The single branch in the path (D-13)(1,268 − 1,250) + (12 − 0) = 30

Ties are impossible under uq_readings_room_period_utility; gaps are legal and never block a run; readings taken while the room was vacant count. Because previous_reading is a snapshot, editing an old month would leave the next month's snapshot stale — which is exactly why D-22 restricts reverting to a room's most recent bill, enforced by a query rather than by a disabled button.

2.6.2 Rate versioning and resolution

rates is append-only and effective-dated. Resolution is keyed to the period, not to the moment Generate was pressed (D-17):

SELECT unit_price
  FROM rates
 WHERE utility_type = ?
   AND effective_from <= CONCAT(?, '-01')   -- first day of the bill's period month
 ORDER BY effective_from DESC
 LIMIT 1;

Worked example. Suppose rates holds:

utility_typeunit_priceeffective_fromApplies to period 2026-09?
water18.002025-10-01Yes — greatest effective_from ≤ 2026-09-01
water19.502026-09-15No — later than 2026-09-01, so it first applies to period 2026-10
electric8.002025-10-01Yes
common35.002025-10-01Yes

Room 205's September bill therefore carries 18.00 / 8.00 / 35.00 and totals 1,790.00 however often generation re-runs and however late the last three rooms are read. A rate saved today never disturbs an approved bill, because the resolved price is copied onto bill_items.unit_price and every screen, print view and LINE message renders from that snapshot. If Draft bills exist for a period when a rate back-dated into it is saved, Settings shows มีบิลฉบับร่าง {N} ใบในรอบนี้ ยังใช้อัตราเดิมอยู่ with a คำนวณบิลฉบับร่างใหม่ button that recalculates Draft bills only.

2.6.3 Money: DECIMAL end to end, arithmetic in SQL

amount = ROUND(quantity × unit_price, 2) · total = SUM(already-rounded amounts) · no second rounding, no satang suppression
Linequantityunit_priceamount
water ค่าน้ำ10.0018.00180.00
electric ค่าไฟ70.008.00560.00
common ค่าส่วนกลาง30.0035.001,050.00
ยอดรวม bills.total1,790.00

JavaScript never multiplies, sums or rounds money. The pool sets decimalNumbers off, so mysql2 returns DECIMAL as a string and no amount can pass through a float — lib/money.js contains formatting and nothing else. Consequences the tester asserts: the printed receipt always equals the sum of its printed lines, and 1789.9999999 cannot occur. Rendering follows D-03: 1,790.00 in tables, on Bill Detail and on the receipt; 1,790 บาท in LINE messages and sentence text; ฿1,790 only in this documentation set and in static mockups.

2.6.4 Receipt numbers: REC-YYYYMM-NNNNNN

receipt_no = 'REC-' + <payment month YYYYMM> + '-' + String(seq).padStart(6, '0') → REC-202609-000852

Allocated inside the payment transaction, after the conditional status update succeeds (D-26, D-27):

INSERT INTO counters (name, seq) VALUES ('receipt', 1)
  ON DUPLICATE KEY UPDATE seq = LAST_INSERT_ID(seq + 1);
SELECT LAST_INSERT_ID();          -- connection-local, so two cashiers cannot collide
sequenceDiagram
  autonumber
  participant A as Admin at the counter
  participant S as Payment service
  participant DB as MySQL
  participant L as LINE Messaging API
  A->>S: ยืนยันรับเงินสด for bill 205, period 2026-09
  S->>DB: BEGIN
  S->>DB: UPDATE bills SET status Paid WHERE id and status Unpaid
  alt 0 rows affected
    S->>DB: ROLLBACK
    S-->>A: 409 บิลนี้ถูกชำระไปแล้ว
  else 1 row affected
    S->>DB: INSERT payments, amount equals bills.total
    S->>DB: UPSERT counters seq, read back LAST_INSERT_ID
    S->>DB: INSERT receipts REC-202609-000852
    S->>DB: INSERT audit_log PAYMENT_CONFIRM
    S->>DB: UPSERT notifications PAID, status queued
    S->>DB: COMMIT
    S->>L: push PAID with the row retry key
    S-->>A: receipt view, ready to print A5
  end
The number is allocated only on the branch that actually took the money, and the LINE push happens strictly after the commit — a LINE timeout can never roll back a payment (D-30).

One continuous counter that never resets; the YYYYMM is a label, not a scope — a monthly reset could not produce the PRD's own 000852 in a 67-room building. The sequence is unique and monotonic but not gapless: a rolled-back transaction consumes a number, and the seed starts at an offset. No document, rationale or test may claim gaplessness. The two assertions are that no two receipts share a number (uq_receipts_no) and that a later payment never receives a lower one.

2.6.5 UTC storage, Asia/Bangkok display

KindTypeColumnsBehaviour
InstantTIMESTAMPcreated_at, paid_at, recorded_at, approved_at, issued_at, last_attempt_at, line_linked_at, expires_at, sent_at, started_at, voided_atMySQL stores it internally as UTC and converts on read using the session time zone. Every connection runs SET time_zone = '+07:00', so storage is UTC and every read is already Asia/Bangkok wall time — both halves of the rule with zero conversion code (ADR A-5).
Business dateDATEdue_date, baseline_date, move_in_date, move_out_date, effective_from, send_date, run_dateCarries no timezone at all. dateStrings: true on the pool delivers '2026-09-05' as a string, so a JS Date constructor cannot shift it across a day boundary and move a bill into Overdue a day early.

Worked example. The demo payment is committed at 14:30 Asia/Bangkok on 3 Sep 2026. payments.paid_at holds 2026-09-03 07:30:00 UTC on disk and returns 2026-09-03 14:30:00 to the application. lib/datetime.js renders it with Intl.DateTimeFormat('th-TH-u-ca-gregory-nu-latn', { timeZone: 'Asia/Bangkok' }): 3 ก.ย. 2026 in lists, 3 กันยายน 2026 on the receipt and in LINE, 14:30 น. for the time. The -u-ca-gregory keyword is load-bearing — plain th-TH renders 2569 Buddhist Era, which D-03 forbids. No UTC value is ever shown to a user and no date arithmetic happens in the browser.

2.6.6 History, soft delete and immutability

TableRetirement mechanismWhy it is never a DELETE
usersstatus='Inactive'payments.received_by_user_id, meter_readings.recorded_by and rates.created_by reference it; an Inactive user cannot log in and keeps every historical attribution (D-46).
roomsis_active=0 (ปิดใช้งานห้อง)Deleting a room referenced by a bill breaks D-09's reprint guarantee. A room with no bills at all may be deleted outright (D-47).
tenantsstatus='Inactive' + move_out_date, LINE binding clearedBills, payments and receipts reference it, and the outgoing tenant stays visible in the room's 12-month history (D-08, D-10).
ratesNone — a new row supersedesInsert-only history is what makes a reprinted receipt and the yearly chart truthful (D-17).
billsNone — status onlyThere is no Cancelled or Void bill status; a wrong bill is reverted, corrected and re-approved (D-22).
paymentsvoided_at / voided_by / void_reasonThe generated active_bill_id goes NULL, freeing the bill for a fresh payment while the void stays on the record (D-25, D-26).
receiptsvoided_at, stamped ยกเลิก in red on every renderKeeping the number taken is precisely what voiding buys; deleting it would invite reuse (D-25, D-27).
announcementsNone — immutable once sentThere is no edit endpoint and no recall; the message has already left the building (D-35).
notificationsNone — UPSERTed in placeEvery attempt rewrites status and increments attempt_count on the same row, so per-bill delivery state and the monthly quota are one query (D-30, D-34).
audit_logNone — append-onlyThe whole point. No update path, no delete path, no global audit browser in v1 (D-38).
sessionsexpires sweep by the libraryThe one table where rows genuinely disappear, and the one table application code never touches (D-37).

Versioning that deliberately does not exist: no room-area history table, no bill version table, no reading revision table, no periods table. Reproducibility comes from the three snapshot columns on bills plus the quantity/price snapshot on bill_items — the billed data on a reprint is identical forever, while the page is not, because the letterhead renders live from Settings and a reprint carries the สำเนา mark (D-09, D-27). No test asserts byte-identity.

2.7 Seed data for the PRD §28 demo scenario

Fixed dates here, relative dates in the shipped seed

The statements below use the PRD's literal calendar — period 2026-09, due 5 Sep 2026, paid 3 Sep 2026, REC-202609-000852 — because a document has to show real values. The shipped db/seed.sql computes every date from CURDATE() (D-44): the current period is DATE_FORMAT(CURDATE(),'%Y-%m'), room 205's reading round is dated the 1st of the current month, and its bill is left Draft so the demo approves it live. The live demo reproduces the arithmetic — 10 หน่วย, 70 หน่วย, 180, 560, 1,050, 1,790 บาท, room 205 at 30 ตร.ม. — never the calendar. The full seed also carries 80 rooms across 5 floors, 67 Occupied and 13 Vacant, 67 tenants of which exactly 54 are LINE-linked, and 12 periods of paid history; only room 205's slice is shown here. In the current period the other 66 billable rooms carry an approved Unpaid bill and room 205's is the only Draft — which is why the money tiles read 66 bills, not 67, until the demo approval lands.

-- db/seed.sql (excerpt) — the PRD §28 demo slice. Ids are the demo fixture
-- ids of §3.1.10, which every payload example and every test reuses.

                        previous_reading, current_reading, previous_period)
VALUES (352, 118, 'water',    10.00, 18.00,  180.00, 1250, 1260, '2026-08'),
       (353, 118, 'electric', 70.00,  8.00,  560.00, 5280, 5350, '2026-08'),
       (354, 118, 'common',   30.00, 35.00, 1050.00, NULL, NULL, NULL);

-- NOTHING ELSE exists for room 205 in this period. There is no payment row, no
-- receipt row and no notifications row: payment 96, receipt 96 and
-- REC-202609-000852 are produced by the demo run, never by the seed (§3.1.10).

The block below is the generation run reproduced for reference, not a seed statement. Nothing in it is executed by db/seed.sql: it is the §4.5.3 writes bound to the room 205 fixture — the same three statements in the same order and the same form, with literal values where the service passes placeholders — and it produces exactly the rows item 8 above wrote by hand. It is what POST /api/bills/generate re-executes when the demo presses ส่งให้นิติตรวจสอบ against a re-armed room 205 (see the callout below). §4.5.3 is normative; this block is documentation. Running it against the seed above yields 1,790.00:

-- 8 · The §4.5.3 writes, bound to the room 205 fixture: period 2026-09,
--     room_id 41, tenant_id 37 (D-18). One transaction per room.
SET @period = '2026-09';
SET @p1     = CONCAT(@period, '-01');    -- first day of the period month (D-17)
SET @due    = 5;                         -- settings.due_day

-- (a) the Draft bill, with its three snapshot columns and the preview due date.
--     services/billing.js has already taken the row with SELECT … FOR UPDATE and
--     inserts only when there is none (§4.5.2), so this is a plain INSERT; the
--     idempotency key is uq_bills(room_id, period), and an existing Draft is
--     recalculated in place by (b) and (c) below without being re-inserted.
INSERT INTO bills (room_id, tenant_id, period, room_code_snapshot, room_area_sqm,
                   tenant_name_snapshot, total, status, due_date)
VALUES (41, 37, @period, '205', 30.00, 'สมชาย ใจดี', 0.00, 'Draft',
        LEAST(DATE_ADD(@p1, INTERVAL @due - 1 DAY), LAST_DAY(@p1)));
SET @bill_id = LAST_INSERT_ID();

-- (b) one item, three times: water, electric, common. The unit prices are what
--     §4.2 resolves for @period against the seeded rates — 18.00 / 8.00 / 35.00 —
--     and are snapshotted here, never joined live (D-17). The row alias AS new
--     needs MySQL 8.0.19 or newer (ADR §2.1); uq_bill_items(bill_id, item_type)
--     makes a fourth or a duplicate item impossible (ENG-2).
INSERT INTO bill_items (bill_id, item_type, quantity, unit_price, amount,
                        previous_reading, current_reading, previous_period)
VALUES (@bill_id, 'water',    10, 18.00, ROUND(10 * 18.00, 2), 1250, 1260, '2026-08') AS new
ON DUPLICATE KEY UPDATE
  quantity         = new.quantity,
  unit_price       = new.unit_price,
  amount           = new.amount,
  previous_reading = new.previous_reading,
  current_reading  = new.current_reading,
  previous_period  = new.previous_period;

INSERT INTO bill_items (bill_id, item_type, quantity, unit_price, amount,
                        previous_reading, current_reading, previous_period)
VALUES (@bill_id, 'electric', 70, 8.00, ROUND(70 * 8.00, 2), 5280, 5350, '2026-08') AS new
ON DUPLICATE KEY UPDATE
  quantity         = new.quantity,
  unit_price       = new.unit_price,
  amount           = new.amount,
  previous_reading = new.previous_reading,
  current_reading  = new.current_reading,
  previous_period  = new.previous_period;

-- The third item is always written, even at 0 units and 0.00 (D-18, D-41). Its
-- quantity is the bill's room_area_sqm snapshot, not rooms.area_sqm (D-09), and
-- its three reading columns are NULL.
INSERT INTO bill_items (bill_id, item_type, quantity, unit_price, amount,
                        previous_reading, current_reading, previous_period)
VALUES (@bill_id, 'common', 30.00, 35.00, ROUND(30.00 * 35.00, 2), NULL, NULL, NULL) AS new
ON DUPLICATE KEY UPDATE
  quantity   = new.quantity,
  unit_price = new.unit_price,
  amount     = new.amount;

-- (c) the total is a SUM of already-rounded rows, never a JS addition (D-16).
--     The status guard is on every statement recalculateBill() issues (§4.2).
UPDATE bills b
   SET b.total = (SELECT COALESCE(SUM(bi.amount), 0)
                    FROM bill_items bi WHERE bi.bill_id = b.id)
 WHERE b.id = @bill_id AND b.status = 'Draft';

Verification — this is the assertion that must never go red (ADR §6.9 tier 1):

SELECT item_type, quantity, unit_price, amount
  FROM bill_items WHERE bill_id = @bill_id
 ORDER BY FIELD(item_type, 'water', 'electric', 'common');
-- water     10.00  18.00    180.00
-- electric  70.00   8.00    560.00
-- common    30.00  35.00   1050.00

SELECT total, status, due_date FROM bills WHERE id = @bill_id;
-- 1790.00   Draft   2026-09-05

Approving on 1 Sep 2026 freezes the due date at MAX(5 Sep 2026, 1 Sep + 3 days) = 5 Sep 2026, matching PRD §28 exactly (D-20). In a live demo the same expression yields approval_date + 3, which is the correct behaviour and not a defect:

UPDATE bills
   SET status      = 'Unpaid',
       approved_by = ?, approved_at = NOW(),
       due_date    = GREATEST(
           LEAST(DATE_ADD(CONCAT(period,'-01'), INTERVAL ? - 1 DAY),
                 LAST_DAY(CONCAT(period,'-01'))),
           DATE_ADD(CURDATE(), INTERVAL 3 DAY))
 WHERE id = ? AND status = 'Draft';

Paying on 3 Sep 2026 then allocates seq = 852 from the seeded counter and writes receipt_no = 'REC-202609-000852', closing the demo on the PRD's own number.

Arming the demo — db/demo-reset.sql and npm run db:demo-arm

The seed above is the finished current period: room 205 is already read and its Draft bill already exists, which is what §3.1.10, §6.2.5, the Dashboard figures of §4.12 and every test case assume. That is deliberately not the state the scripted demo starts from. On a fresh npm run db:reset the Meter Reading page shows room 205 as จดแล้ว and a generation run reports สร้างบิลใหม่ 0 ใบ · อัปเดต 1 ใบ, so the first two actions of the demo script would have nothing to do.

db/demo-reset.sql re-arms exactly one room. It deletes room 205's current-period bill, its items, its audit rows and its two readings, and resets the receipt counter to 851. It changes nothing else, and in particular it touches no settings row — LINE test mode and line_test_user_id stay exactly as the operator left them (§5.8.4). Every date comes from CURDATE() (D-44) and the file is idempotent, so running it twice leaves the same state.

-- db/demo-reset.sql — run immediately BEFORE a demo or a rehearsal, never during one.
-- Re-arms the PRD §28 scenario on room 205 only: no reading, no bill, counter at 851.
SET @room   := (SELECT id FROM rooms WHERE room_code = '205');
SET @period := DATE_FORMAT(CURDATE(), '%Y-%m');
SET @bill   := (SELECT id FROM bills WHERE room_id = @room AND period = @period);

DELETE FROM notifications  WHERE bill_id = @bill;
DELETE FROM receipts       WHERE payment_id IN (SELECT id FROM payments WHERE bill_id = @bill);
DELETE FROM payments       WHERE bill_id = @bill;
DELETE FROM bill_items     WHERE bill_id = @bill;
DELETE FROM audit_log      WHERE bill_id = @bill;
DELETE FROM bills          WHERE id      = @bill;
DELETE FROM meter_readings WHERE room_id = @room AND period = @period;

-- The counter is unique and monotonic but explicitly NOT gapless (D-26, D-27).
UPDATE counters SET seq = 851 WHERE name = 'receipt';

This is the demo-database twin of the test plan's test/fixtures/reset-205.sql, which does the same unwinding and then additionally whitelists a test recipient — one UPDATE settings that belongs only to juristic_test and juristic_e2e and that its own header forbids against a demo database (test plan §2.4.1). The two files exist separately for exactly that reason, and neither is ever run against the other's database.

Where it runs: npm run db:demo-arm (§6.2.4) as step 0 of the demo script (product decisions, Demo script — the eight actions on screen), as the first action of the deployed smoke test (§6.3.2 step 8), and inside npm run rehearse before it walks the path (§6.5.1).

2.8 Assumptions recorded by this section

  1. Money stays DECIMAL(10,2), per D-16 and ADR §6.4, against the section brief's DECIMAL(12,2). Recorded above as a callout so a reviewer can find it.
  2. No line_accounts table and no separate CommonAreaRates table. PRD §21's list is descriptive; ADR A-12 and D-17 are normative. The LINE binding is three columns on tenants; all three rates live in one rates table.
  3. A revert clears approved_by and approved_at. D-22 does not say so explicitly; ck_bills_approved requires it, and the approval history survives in audit_log where D-38 puts it.
  4. Column widths not fixed by a decision are chosen here and are binding on sibling sections: names 120, addresses 255, reasons 200–255, last_error 255, announcements.title 60 and body 400 (the D-35 form limits), settings.office_hours 60.
  5. ck_readings_units is deliberately redundant against INT UNSIGNED. D-13 names the constraint; keeping it documents the invariant where a reader looks for it, at zero cost.
  6. REGEXP inside CHECK is used for period, phone, receipt_no, audience_floors and the link-code alphabet. It is deterministic and legal from MySQL 8.0.16. If a managed provider ever rejects one, the fallback is the same rule in the matching zod schema, executed as runbook R5 — every one of these five is already validated at layer 2 (ADR §6.8).
  7. ON DELETE CASCADE appears exactly once, on fk_bill_items_bill. Bills are never deleted in production; the cascade exists so the integration tier can drop a scratch bill in one statement.
  8. audit_log.entity_id carries no foreign key because it points at four different tables. ix_audit_entity covers the lookup; bill_id, the column that actually drives a screen, is a foreign key.
  9. Sizing. 80 rooms × 12 periods ≈ 960 bills, 2,880 bill items, 1,920 readings and roughly 3,000 notification rows a year. The whole database is under 50 MB after five years, which is why no index exists that is not named in §2.3.12 and why partitioning, archiving and read replicas are all out of scope.

3. API Specification

This section is the complete HTTP contract of Juristic Platform: every route, who may call it, what it accepts, what it returns, and every rule that can reject it. It is written against ADR-001 §6.2 (API style and base path), §6.3 (envelope and error format) and §6.8 (validation in four layers); where a rule comes from the product decision log the decision id is cited inline. Every worked example uses the PRD §28 demo: room 205, 30 sqm, water 1,250 → 1,260 = 10 units × 18 THB = 180, electricity 5,280 → 5,350 = 70 units × 8 THB = 560, common area 30 × 35 = 1,050, total 1,790 THB, due 5 Sep 2026, paid 3 Sep 2026, receipt REC-202609-000852.

The shape of this API is fixed by the ADR and is not a REST API

ADR-001 §6.2 and A-11 are binding: server-rendered pages with POST-Redirect-GET are the default, and exactly seven JSON endpoints exist under /api. Adding an eighth is a decision, not a convenience. Consequently this specification documents two request surfaces with one shared set of conventions — HTML routes that accept application/x-www-form-urlencoded and answer with a redirect, and the seven JSON endpoints that exist only where a page must not reload. There is no /api/v1, no JSON CRUD for rooms, tenants or users, and no GET /api/me. Where this section shows "request/response" for a form route, the request is form fields and the response is a redirect or a re-render — that is the contract an implementer must build.

3.1 Conventions

3.1.1 The two surfaces

SurfaceBase pathRequestSuccessFailureAuth
Page routes (default)/GET, or POST with application/x-www-form-urlencoded + _csrf200 HTML, or 303 See Other + Location + one-shot flashRe-render the same page with 200, every typed value preserved, inline Thai error (D-40). Never a redirect, never a lost form.Session cookie
JSON endpoints (7)/apiapplication/json + X-CSRF-Token on every POST200 with {"data": …}Status per the table below with {"error": …}Session cookie
Machine endpoints (3)—See each200401, empty bodyX-Line-Signature / X-Job-Token / public

Route naming follows ADR §6.1: page routes are lowercase, plural, hyphenated (/bills, /bills/:id, /readings); JSON routes are /api/<resource>[/<action>]. Page identifiers stay English in routes, payloads and test hooks; every visible string is Thai (D-02).

3.1.2 Authentication, session and CSRF

MechanismDetailSource
Sessionexpress-session with the MySQL store; cookie connect.sid (library default, not renamed), httpOnly, SameSite=Lax, Secure in production, rolling 8-hour expiry. No JWT, no bearer token, no Authorization header anywhere in the system.D-37
Role gaterequireAuth then requireRole('admin') / requireRole('admin','staff'), one enforcement point in middleware/auth.js. Navigation is generated from the same matrix, so a link never exists for a page the role cannot open.D-36, ADR A-6
CSRFSynchronizer token stored in the session, compared with crypto.timingSafeEqual. Page forms send it as the hidden input _csrf; the six client-side fetch calls send the identical value as the X-CSRF-Token header. Every POST is protected except the three machine routes.D-37, ADR A-8
Login rate limit10 failed logins per IP per 15 minutes, app.set('trust proxy', 1). Applies to POST /login only — never to the webhook, the job endpoint or /healthz.D-37

3.1.3 Session identity — why there is no GET /api/me

The identity of the caller is never fetched; it is already on the page. On successful login the server stores exactly this object and exposes it to every EJS render as currentUser:

req.session.user = {
  "id": 1,
  "username": "admin",
  "display_name": "นิติบุคคล",   // prints as ผู้รับเงิน on the receipt (D-27, D-46)
  "role": "admin"                // "admin" | "staff"
}

Nothing else is kept in the session but the CSRF token and the flash message. A JSON caller that has lost its session gets 401 UNAUTHENTICATED from the same middleware, and the page surface redirects to /login instead. Adding a /api/me endpoint would be an eighth JSON endpoint serving data the server already rendered.

3.1.4 JSON envelope

Every JSON response carries exactly one top-level key — data or error. No response ever carries both, and no endpoint returns a bare array (ADR §6.3).

{ "data": { "period": "2026-09", "created": 1, "updated": 0, "skipped": 0, "unread": 3 } }
{ "error": {
    "code": "READING_BELOW_PREVIOUS",
    "message": "เลขมิเตอร์ปัจจุบันน้อยกว่าเดือนก่อน (1,250) กรุณาตรวจสอบ",
    "fields": { "current_reading": "ต้องไม่น้อยกว่า 1,250" } } }

code is a stable UPPER_SNAKE identifier that tests assert on; message is Thai and is what the user sees; fields is present only on a validation failure and is keyed by the input name, so the API message and the inline form message are the same string produced by the same zod schema (D-40).

3.1.5 Status codes

StatusMeaningPage surface behaviour
200OK, or a form re-rendered with errorsHTML
303POST accepted, redirect to the result page (POST-Redirect-GET)Location + flash
400Malformed or unparseable requestThai 400 flash, back to the form
401No session (or bad machine credential)Redirect to /login
403Role denied, or CSRF token mismatchThai 403 page with a button back to จดมิเตอร์ (D-40)
404Unknown id or unknown routeThai 404 page
409Lost a state race — a conditional UPDATE affected 0 rows, or a unique key rejected the writeRe-render with the Thai message
422Well-formed but rejected by a business ruleInline field errors, values preserved
429Login rate limitLogin page with the wait message
500Unexpected — generic Thai sentence, stack to stdout onlyThai 500 page
503/healthz only: database unreachable—

3.1.6 Error code catalogue

This list is exhaustive. A handler that needs a code not on this list is adding one, and the new code must be added here first.

CodeStatusRaised whenThai messageRef

3.1.7 Validation error shape

Layer 2 of ADR §6.8 — one zod schema per endpoint, parsed before the handler body runs — produces both payloads below from one definition. Staff saves room 205's water meter as 1,240 against a previous of 1,250:

POST /api/readings          →  422
{ "error": {
    "code": "READING_BELOW_PREVIOUS",
    "message": "เลขมิเตอร์ปัจจุบันน้อยกว่าเดือนก่อน (1,250) กรุณาตรวจสอบ",
    "fields": { "water.current_reading": "ต้องไม่น้อยกว่า 1,250" } } }

The same rule reached through a page form re-renders the form with 200, 1240 still in the input and the identical Thai sentence above it. fields keys are input names, dotted for nested objects, so the client can attach each message to its own input without a lookup table.

3.1.8 Pagination, filters and periods

ConventionRule
Pagination?page=, 1-based, fixed page size 50, applied to /rooms, /tenants, /bills, /announcements and /users. An out-of-range page clamps to the last page rather than 404-ing. The rendered page includes views/partials/pagination.ejs. No JSON endpoint paginates: the only list-shaped JSON response is /api/notifications/drain, capped at one batch by design.
The one exception/readings is never paginated — the floor-grouped reading round must be one continuous page on a phone, and the sticky counter counts the whole period (D-15).
Period?period=YYYY-MM, regex ^\d{4}-(0[1-9]|1[0-2])$. Absent or malformed ⇒ the current calendar month in Asia/Bangkok. There is no periods table and no open/closed state (D-11).
FiltersEnumerated per route and rejected with VALIDATION_FAILED if unknown — never silently ignored.
CSV?format=csv on the four report routes only. Content-Type: text/csv; charset=utf-8, Content-Disposition: attachment; filename="report-arrears-2026-09.csv", UTF-8 BOM so Excel opens Thai correctly (D-42).

3.1.9 Types on the wire

KindWire formatExampleWhy
MoneyJSON string, two decimals, no separators, no currency"1790.00"mysql2 returns DECIMAL as a string and no amount ever passes through a JS float (D-16, ADR A-4). Formatting to 1,790.00 / 1,790 บาท happens at render.
Readings, units, countsJSON number, integer1260, 10INT UNSIGNED, whole units only (D-16)
Area, ratesJSON string"30.00", "18.00"DECIMAL(6,2) / DECIMAL(8,2)
Business dateISO 8601 date"2026-09-05"DATE, no timezone, dateStrings on the pool (D-03, ADR A-5)
InstantYYYY-MM-DD HH:mm:ss, Asia/Bangkok wall time, no offset suffix and no Z"2026-09-03 14:30:00"The connection runs at +07:00; no UTC is ever shown to a user (D-03)
PeriodYYYY-MM"2026-09"String order is chronological (ADR §6.7)
Previous periodYYYY-MM or the literal "baseline""2026-08"VARCHAR(8) (D-06)
StatusEnglish identifier, exactly Draft / Unpaid / Paid / Overdue"Unpaid"Thai is rendered from it; data-status test hooks assert on it (D-02, D-21)

3.1.10 Demo fixture ids

Every example below uses these ids. Sibling documents and tests reuse them so one example never contradicts another. They fall into two groups, and the difference is load-bearing: the first group exists the moment npm run db:reset finishes, the second only after the demo — or a test — has run generate → approve → pay. A payload example, a case precondition or an assertion that reads a second-group id against an untouched seed fails, and that failure is the example's defect, not the seed's.

Group 1 — produced by db/seed.sql. Available on any fresh clone.

EntityidIdentifying value
Admin user1admin / นิติบุคคล
Staff user2staff / เจ้าหน้าที่
Room41room_code "205", floor 2, area_sqm "30.00"
Tenant37สมชาย ใจดี, 0812345678, Active, LINE-linked with the placeholder id Useed0000000000000000000000000037
Meter readings812 water, 813 electriccurrent period, 1,250 → 1,260 and 5,280 → 5,350 (§2.7 item 6)
Bill118current period, total "1790.00", status Draft (§2.7 item 8)
Bill items352 water, 353 electric, 354 common180.00 / 560.00 / 1050.00
Announcement7ปิดน้ำเพื่อซ่อมบำรุง
Receipt countercounters.name = 'receipt'seq = 851 — the next allocation is 852, and no receipt row exists yet

Group 2 — produced by the demo run, not seeded. unavailable until generate → approve → pay has run Room 205's bill is Draft on a fresh seed and only reaches Paid when a human, a rehearsal or a test pays it.

EntityidIdentifying valueCreated by
Bill 118's approval118status = 'Unpaid', approved_by, approved_at, frozen due_datePOST /api/bills/approve (§3.3.9)
Payment96cash, bill 118, 1790.00 — the documentation calendar shows 2026-09-03 14:30:00POST /payments (§3.3.12)
Receipt96REC-202609-000852 — REC-<payment YYYYMM>-000852 on a live runthe same transaction, allocating seq = 852 (§2.6.4)
Notifications—one BILL row and one PAID row for tenant 37approval and payment, drained after commit (§5.6.4)
What this means for an example and for a fixture

Payload examples in §3.3 that show payment 96, receipt 96 or the literal period 2026-09 are showing the documentation calendar and the state after the demo path has run; they are documentation, not preconditions, and none of them is a runnable assertion. Anything that is runnable derives the period — const period = currentPeriod(); from lib/datetime.js — because the seed's current period is DATE_FORMAT(CURDATE(),'%Y-%m') and a hard-coded '2026-09' goes red on 1 October (D-44, §6.5.2). A test that needs those rows must create them, and a test that needs room 205 unread — which the seed's readings 812 / 813 prevent — must first apply test/fixtures/reset-205.sql (test plan §2.4.1), or, on a demo database, db/demo-reset.sql (§2.7).

POST/bills/:id/revertAตีกลับเป็นฉบับร่าง, reason ≥ 10 chars, newest bill only (D-22)
POST/bills/:id/notifyAส่งแจ้งเตือนอีกครั้ง — uncapped manual resend (D-30, D-33)
GET/bills/:id/invoiceAA5 ใบแจ้งค่าใช้จ่าย print view; refused for a Draft bill (D-28)

3.2.7 Payment & Receipt (Admin only — D-36 corrects PRD §12)
