3.3 Endpoint detail

3.3.1 Login, logout, session

POST /login — body username, password, _csrf.

OutcomeResponse
Credentials valid, user Active303 → /dashboard for role admin, 303 → /readings for role staff; the session is regenerated first, so a fixated session id cannot survive login. This is the role landing rule FR-01 asserts and D-40's 403 page assumes.
Wrong password, unknown username, or Inactive user200, login page re-rendered, username preserved, password cleared, LOGIN_INVALID message. The three cases are indistinguishable to the caller by design.
11th failure from one IP in 15 min429, LOGIN_RATE_LIMITED (D-37)

POST /logout — body _csrf only. Deletes the session row, clears the cookie, 303 → /login. It is a POST, not a GET, so a prefetch or an image tag cannot log a cashier out mid-transaction.

POST /password — body current_password, new_password (min 8), confirm_password, _csrf. Re-hashes at bcrypt cost 10 and keeps the session. This is a change, not a reset: D-37's "no self-service password reset" stands, and a locked-out user is still recovered only by an Admin through POST /users/:id/password.

3.3.2 Users

POST /users — fields username (unique, 3–50, ^[a-z0-9._-]+$), display_name (1–100, printed as ผู้รับเงิน on receipts), role (admin|staff), password (min 8). status is Active on create. Success 303 → /users; duplicate username 409 USERNAME_TAKEN re-rendered inline.

POST /users/:id updates display_name, role and status only. username is immutable — it is the login identity and appears in every audit row. There is no delete: payments.received_by_user_id, meter_readings.recorded_by and rates.created_by reference the row forever, so deactivation is the only removal (D-46). An Admin cannot set their own account Inactive; that attempt returns 422 VALIDATION_FAILED with {"status": "ไม่สามารถปิดใช้งานบัญชีของตนเองได้"}, which is what prevents an empty-admin system.

3.3.3 Rooms CRUD

FieldTypeRequiredRule
room_codestring(10)yesUnique system-wide. 205 today, A-205 tolerated (D-04)
floorintyes1–99, entered, never parsed from the code (D-47)
room_typestring(20)noDisplay label only; never affects an amount (D-47)
area_sqmdecimal(6,2)yes0.01–999.99. Demo: 30.00. Change is audited ROOM_AREA_CHANGE (D-09)
baseline_water, baseline_electricintyesEditable only while the room has no reading row at all (D-06)
baseline_datedateyesOne shared date for both meters (D-06)

status is not a field: it is maintained by the tenant transaction and is never independently editable (D-07). POST /rooms/:id/delete succeeds only while no bill references the room; otherwise 409 ROOM_HAS_BILLS and the UI offers ปิดใช้งานห้อง instead (D-47).

Deactivate and activate are a pair. POST /rooms/:id/deactivate takes reason (≥ 10 Thai characters, D-39), sets is_active = 0 and writes ROOM_DEACTIVATE. POST /rooms/:id/activate takes _csrf and nothing else, sets is_active = 1 and writes ROOM_ACTIVATE on the same connection; both answer 303 → /rooms/:id with the Thai flash strip, and both are Admin only. The asymmetry is deliberate: deactivating removes a room from billing, from the reading round and from every count, so D-39 requires a stated reason; activating only puts it back, and a room that is already is_active = 1 is a no-op that still returns 303 rather than a 409, so a double-click on the banner cannot produce an error page. A deactivated room whose tenant is still Active is not a state the endpoints can reach — settlement runs first (D-08) — so activation never has to repair one.

3.3.4 Tenants CRUD and the LINE link code

POST /tenants — full_name (1–100), phone (^0\d{9}$, e.g. 0812345678), room_id, move_in_date. line_user_id is never accepted from a form — it is filled only by the webhook (D-10, D-29). The insert relies on uq_tenants(active_room_id): a second Active tenant for room 41 is rejected by the database, surfaced as 409 TENANT_ROOM_OCCUPIED, not by a SELECT-then-INSERT race (ENG-1).

POST /tenants/:id/end-tenancy — reason (≥ 10 Thai characters). Blocked with 422 TENANCY_END_BLOCKED unless both conditions of D-08 hold. On success, one transaction sets status='Inactive', writes move_out_date = CURDATE(), clears the three LINE columns, sets rooms.status='Vacant' and writes TENANCY_END with the reason (ENG-5).

POST /tenants/:id/link-code — no body beyond _csrf. Response 303 → /tenants/37/link-code/slip. The generated row:

{ "tenant_id": 37, "code": "K7QM4P", "expires_at": "2026-09-08 09:14:02", "used_at": null }

Alphabet ABCDEFGHJKLMNPQRSTUVWXYZ23456789 — no O/0/I/1 — 6 characters, 7 days, single use (D-29). Issuing a new code stamps used_at = NOW() on every unused, unexpired code for that tenant in the same transaction, so a tenant never holds two live codes; the old slip must be destroyed (§5.4.2).

3.3.5 Meter readings — the previous reading is resolved server-side, with no endpoint

There is deliberately no "get previous reading" call. The /readings page renders Previous as read-only text, and POST /api/readings echoes the value it actually used. One query serves both (ADR §7.1):

SELECT current_reading, period
  FROM meter_readings
 WHERE room_id = ? AND utility_type = ? AND period < ?   -- strictly earlier
 ORDER BY period DESC
 LIMIT 1;

No row ⇒ the room's baseline_water / baseline_electric with previous_period = "baseline" (D-06). The strict < excludes the row being written, which is exactly what makes a double-submitted save harmless (D-12, D-14).

3.3.6 Meter readings — POST /api/readings, save one room

POST /api/readings
X-CSRF-Token: <token>
{ "period": "2026-09",
  "room_id": 41,
  "water":    { "current_reading": 1260 },
  "electric": { "current_reading": 5350 } }
200
{ "data": {
    "room_id": 41, "room_code": "205", "period": "2026-09", "complete": true,
    "water":    { "previous_reading": 1250, "previous_period": "2026-08",
                  "current_reading": 1260, "units": 10, "flag": null },
    "electric": { "previous_reading": 5280, "previous_period": "2026-08",
                  "current_reading": 5350, "units": 70, "flag": null },
    "counter":  { "done": 42, "total": 67 },
    "bill_recalculated": false } }

flag is null, "high_usage" (ใช้สูงผิดปกติ) or "no_usage" (ไม่มีการใช้งาน) — soft, never blocking (D-13). counter is recomputed server-side and drives the sticky bar "จดแล้ว 42/67 ห้อง"; a room counts as done only when both utilities are saved (D-15). bill_recalculated is true when a Draft bill for that room and period existed and was recalculated in place on save (D-18). Money never appears in this response, which is what makes the endpoint safe for Staff (D-36).

Meter replacement — the one branch (D-13). Room 205's water meter is swapped at 1,258 and the new dial reads 2:

{ "period": "2026-09", "room_id": 41,
  "water": { "current_reading": 2, "meter_replaced": true,
             "old_final": 1258, "new_start": 0,
             "replace_reason": "มิเตอร์น้ำชำรุด เปลี่ยนใหม่วันที่ 1 ก.ย. 2026" },
  "electric": { "current_reading": 5350 } }
units = (old_final − previous_reading) + (current_reading − new_start) = (1258 − 1250) + (2 − 0) = 8 + 2 = 10 units → 10 × 18.00 = 180.00

The demo total is unchanged, which is the point: a replacement must not move money. With the box ticked the current-below-previous block does not apply; the validations become old_final ≥ previous_reading and current_reading ≥ new_start, both of which keep units ≥ 0 and satisfy the database CHECK.

3.3.7 Meter readings — POST /api/bills/generate, ส่งให้นิติตรวจสอบ

{ "period": "2026-09" }
200
{ "data": { "period": "2026-09",
            "created": 1, "updated": 0, "skipped": 0,
            "unread": 3, "missing_rooms": ["108", "312", "405"] } }

The page renders D-18's Thai sentence from these counts: "สร้างบิลใหม่ 1 ใบ · อัปเดต 0 ใบ · ข้าม (อนุมัติแล้ว) 0 ใบ · ยังไม่ได้จด 3 ห้อง". Running it a second time with nothing changed returns created: 0 and is not an error — uq_bills(room_id, period) is the idempotency key of the whole run (D-18). Staff may call it; the response carries counts and room codes, never amounts.

3.3.8 Billing — GET /bills/:id, the Bill Detail payload

Rendered server-side; the locals below are the contract the EJS page and the test hooks share.

{ "id": 118, "room_code_snapshot": "205", "room_area_sqm": "30.00",
  "tenant_name_snapshot": "สมชาย ใจดี", "period": "2026-09",
  "status": "Unpaid", "display_status": "Unpaid",
  "total": "1790.00", "due_date": "2026-09-05",
  "approved_by": 1, "approved_at": "2026-09-01 09:12:30",
  "items": [
    { "item_type": "water",    "quantity": "10.00", "unit_price": "18.00", "amount": "180.00",
      "previous_reading": 1250, "current_reading": 1260, "previous_period": "2026-08" },
    { "item_type": "electric", "quantity": "70.00", "unit_price": "8.00",  "amount": "560.00",
      "previous_reading": 5280, "current_reading": 5350, "previous_period": "2026-08" },
    { "item_type": "common",   "quantity": "30.00", "unit_price": "35.00", "amount": "1050.00",
      "previous_reading": null, "current_reading": null, "previous_period": null } ],
  "notification": { "type": "BILL", "status": "sent", "attempt_count": 1,
                    "last_attempt_at": "2026-09-01 09:12:34" },
  "can_revert": true, "can_pay": true, "auto_notify_suspended": false }

Always exactly three items, even at 0 units and "0.00" — that is what makes the tiles sum and the receipt print three lines (D-18, D-41). Every figure is the bill's own snapshot; nothing joins live to rooms or tenants (D-09). display_status comes from the one helper, Unpaid + due_date < CURDATE() ⇒ Overdue (D-21), and Overdue is never a value of status.

3.3.9 Billing — POST /api/bills/approve, อนุมัติและส่งบิล

{ "bill_ids": [118] }
200
{ "data": { "approved": 1, "already_approved": 0, "not_found": 0,
            "queued": 1, "skipped_no_line": 0,
            "bills": [ { "id": 118, "room_code": "205", "status": "Unpaid",
                         "due_date": "2026-09-05", "notification": "queued" } ] } }

bill_ids holds 1–200 ids; Bill Detail's single button sends an array of one, Monthly Billing's bulk button sends the selection. A mixed batch is never an error: non-Draft ids are counted in already_approved and left untouched, which makes a double-submit a no-op that returns approved: 0. The due date is computed once per bill and frozen (D-20):

due_date = MAX( LEAST(due_day of the period month, last day of that month), approval_date + 3 days )
the clamp applies to the due-day candidate only; the result may fall in the following month (§4.8.2)
period 2026-09, due_day 5, approved 1 Sep 2026 → MAX(2026-09-05, 2026-09-04) = 2026-09-05

3.3.10 Billing — POST /api/notifications/drain

POST /api/notifications/drain      (no body)

200
{ "data": { "sent": 1, "failed": 0, "skipped_no_line": 0,
            "remaining": 0, "lock": "acquired" } }

One call pushes at most 20 personalised messages, or one multicast chunk of at most 500 announcement recipients (D-34). lock is "acquired" or "busy": when GET_LOCK('notif_drain', 0) fails, the call returns busy with zero counts and the client waits one second and retries — that is what stops the approve screen and the 09:00 job pushing the same rows (ADR §7.6). The client loops while remaining > 0. A dropped connection loses nothing: the queued rows survive and the daily job would finish them anyway.

sequenceDiagram
  autonumber
  participant B as "Admin browser"
  participant A as "POST /api/bills/approve"
  participant D as "POST /api/notifications/drain"
  participant DB as MySQL
  participant L as "LINE Messaging API"
  B->>A: "bill_ids 118"
  A->>DB: "one transaction: status Unpaid, freeze due_date, audit, queue notifications"
  A-->>B: "approved 1, queued 1"
  loop "while remaining greater than 0"
    B->>D: "drain"
    D->>DB: "GET_LOCK notif_drain, claim at most 20 queued rows"
    D->>L: "push with the row's X-Line-Retry-Key"
    D->>DB: "upsert sent or failed, attempt_count plus one, release lock"
    D-->>B: "sent 1, failed 0, remaining 0"
  end
Two round trips, never one (D-19). Money state commits before any message leaves, so a LINE outage cannot roll back a bill.

3.3.11 Billing — POST /bills/:id/revert, ตีกลับเป็นฉบับร่าง

Form fields reason (≥ 10 chars) and _csrf. Refused with 422 REVERT_NOT_NEWEST when SELECT 1 FROM bills WHERE room_id = ? AND period > ? LIMIT 1 returns a row, because a later bill's previous_reading is a snapshot of the value about to be edited (D-12, D-22). Success writes BILL_REVERT with before/after JSON inside the transaction and 303 → /bills/118. The readings unfreeze; re-approving sends BILL_CORRECTED (D-31).

3.3.12 Payments — ยืนยันรับเงินสด

POST /payments
Content-Type: application/x-www-form-urlencoded

bill_id=118&expected_total=1790.00&_csrf=<token>

The amount is not an input. expected_total is a staleness check only: the server compares it with bills.total and returns 409 BILL_TOTAL_CHANGED if the bill was reverted and recalculated while the cashier had the screen open. The stored amount is copied from the bill in SQL, so a payment can never disagree with its bill (D-24). The optional รับเงิน / เงินทอน calculator (รับเงิน 2,000 → เงินทอน 210.00) is screen-only and is never sent.

sequenceDiagram
  autonumber
  participant B as "Admin browser"
  participant P as "POST /payments"
  participant DB as MySQL
  participant R as "GET /receipts/96"
  B->>P: "bill_id 118, expected_total 1790.00"
  P->>DB: "UPDATE bills SET status Paid WHERE id 118 AND status Unpaid"
  alt "0 rows affected"
    P-->>B: "409 บิลนี้ถูกชำระไปแล้ว"
  else "1 row affected"
    P->>DB: "INSERT payments amount from bills.total, unique active_bill_id"
    P->>DB: "allocate receipt number from counters, INSERT receipts"
    P->>DB: "audit PAYMENT_CONFIRM, queue PAID notification"
    P-->>B: "303 to /receipts/96"
    B->>R: "open the A5 receipt"
    R-->>B: "REC-202609-000852, 1,790.00 บาท"
  end
One transaction, one button, no second screen (D-24). The receipt number is allocated after the conditional update succeeds, so two cashiers cannot receive the same number (D-26).

Success 303 → /receipts/96. The receipt page fires one POST /api/notifications/drain on load, which is what delivers the PAID message — no separate send endpoint exists. POST /payments/:id/void takes reason (≥ 10 chars), marks the payment and receipt voided without deleting either, returns the bill to Unpaid, writes PAYMENT_VOID, and sends nothing (D-25). The bill is thereafter excluded from automatic PRE_DUE and OVERDUE sends and shows ระงับการแจ้งเตือนอัตโนมัติ (auto_notify_suspended: true).

GET /payments/history — ประวัติการรับชำระ (FR-25). The same page file as /payments rendered on its second tab, Admin only, read-only, no CSV export. Selection is by payment date: ?period=2026-09 means p.paid_at falls in that month, which is why a bill from รอบบิล 2026-08 settled on 2 ก.ย. appears under กันยายน — the รอบบิล column exists so the difference is visible rather than mysterious (D-41). ?q= matches a room code by prefix or a receipt_no exactly, ?filter=all|live|voided drives the three chips ทั้งหมด · ปกติ · ยกเลิกแล้ว with live counts, and ?room_id=41 scopes the list to one room for Room detail's ดูประวัติทั้งหมด link. The query is the LEFT JOIN payments … p.id = (SELECT MAX(p2.id) …) shape of §4.13.3, never a join on active_bill_id: a voided payment must stay listed, stamped ยกเลิก with its void_reason, or the office loses the evidence that money was taken and returned (D-25). Because the list is scoped by room and payments stay attached to the tenant who made them, a room's history spans tenancies and still shows a now-Inactive tenant's Paid bill (D-08). The summary line รวมรับชำระเดือนนี้ (ไม่รวมรายการที่ยกเลิก) sums live rows only. Row actions link to /receipts/:id; พิมพ์สำเนา is the ordinary reprint path, so it increments print_count through POST /api/receipts/:id/print and the sheet carries the สำเนา mark (D-27). Staff get the Thai 403 page like every other Payment route (D-36, D-40).

3.3.13 Receipts

GET /receipts/96 serves both the on-screen A5 view and the printed page from one route and one stylesheet (D-28). It renders the original while print_count = 0 and carries the สำเนา mark at print_count ≥ 1; a voided receipt is stamped ยกเลิก in red (D-25, D-27). The letterhead is rendered live from Settings and carries only the apartment's own name, address, optional tax id and phone — never the software name (D-01).

POST /api/receipts/96/print      (no body, X-CSRF-Token required)

200
{ "data": { "receipt_id": 96, "receipt_no": "REC-202609-000852",
            "print_count": 1, "copy": true } }

The button POSTs this, then calls window.print(), so the first sheet is the original and copy: true tells the page to relabel its button พิมพ์สำเนา without a reload. Printing from the browser's own menu bypasses the counter and yields an unmarked copy — an accepted limitation (D-05).

Receipt field order (D-27, mandatory and complete). views/print/receipt.ejs renders exactly these blocks, in this order. Values shown are the PRD §28 fixture. FR-38's ใบแจ้งค่าใช้จ่าย is this list minus the four rows marked receipt only.

#BlockRendered for the §28 fixtureSource
1Letterheadศุภาลัย เรสซิเดนซ์ · address · โทร 021234567 · เลขประจำตัวผู้เสียภาษี omitted when settings.tax_id is NULLsettings (D-01)
2Titleใบเสร็จรับเงินfixed
3เลขที่ receipt onlyREC-202609-000852receipts.receipt_no
4วันที่ชำระ receipt only3 กันยายน 2026payments.paid_at
5ห้อง205bills.room_code_snapshot (D-09)

7รอบบิลกันยายน 2026bills.period, formatted by lib/datetime.js
8Line item · ค่าน้ำ10 หน่วย × 18.00 = 180.00bill_items where item_type='water'
9Line item · ค่าไฟ70 หน่วย × 8.00 = 560.00bill_items where item_type='electric'
10Line item · ค่าส่วนกลาง30 ตร.ม. × 35.00 = 1,050.00bill_items where item_type='common'

Rows 8–10 are always three, never fewer, because generation always writes three items (§4.5.3). A receipt that prints two lines is the same defect as a bill whose total does not equal its printed lines (D-16, D-41).

3.3.14 Announcements

GET /api/announcements/preview-count?audience=floors&floors=2,3

200
{ "data": { "rooms": 26, "linked": 21 } }
POST /announcements
title=ปิดน้ำเพื่อซ่อมบำรุง&body=วันที่ 20 กันยายน เวลา 10:00–12:00 น. …&audience=all&_csrf=<token>
→ 303 /announcements/7

title ≤ 60 chars, body ≤ 400 chars, audience is all or floors, and floors is a comma-separated list required only when audience=floors (D-35). Creating and sending are one action: the POST writes the announcement, writes one notifications row per recipient tenant with status='queued', and redirects to the result page, which drains them. Against the seed that is 67 rooms and 54 linked tenants — the 13 unlinked tenants get skipped_no_line rows so the gap is visible rather than silent (D-30, D-44). A sent announcement is immutable: any edit, resend or recall attempt is 409 ANNOUNCEMENT_IMMUTABLE, because LINE offers no recall and the UI must not imply otherwise.

3.3.15 Dashboard summary

GET /dashboard?period=2026-09 renders these locals. Every formula is binding (D-41); Draft bills are excluded from every money figure, and "approved" means the stored status is Unpaid or Paid.

LocalThai tileFormulaRoles
rooms_totalห้องทั้งหมดCOUNT(rooms) = 80A S
rooms_occupiedมีผู้เช่าCOUNT(rooms WHERE status='Occupied') = 67A S
rooms_vacantห้องว่างtotal − occupied = 13A S
reading_progressจดมิเตอร์แล้ว{done, total} over billable rooms for the periodA S
water_totalยอดค่าน้ำเดือนนี้SUM(bill_items.amount) WHERE item_type='water' over approved bills of the periodA

The Staff dashboard query selects only the first four rows; the money columns are absent from the response, not hidden in the template (D-36, ADR A-6).

GET /api/dashboard/charts?period=2026-09        (Admin only)

200
{ "data": {
    "period": "2026-09",
    "labels": ["2025-10","2025-11","2025-12","2026-01","2026-02","2026-03",
               "2026-04","2026-05","2026-06","2026-07","2026-08","2026-09"],
    "water_units":       [612, 640, 655, 601, 588, 630, 671, 702, 715, 690, 668, 655],
    "electric_units":    [4210, 4380, 4520, 4110, 3980, 4300, 4720, 5010, 5180, 4930, 4610, 4480],
    "revenue_collected": ["115046.00","116910.00","118300.00","114048.00","112774.00",
                          "116090.00","120188.00","123066.00","124660.00",
                          "122210.00","119254.00","0.00"],
    "status_counts":     { "Paid": 0, "UnpaidOrOverdue": 66 } } }

Every series carries exactly twelve entries, one per label, so the client zips it onto the axis with no length check. The axis is built from the twelve periods and each series is LEFT JOINed onto it, so the two possible empties are distinct and both are specified (§4.13.2):

  • null — the period has no bill of any status. The chart plots a gap in the line, not a false zero (D-14).
  • "0.00" — the period has at least one approved bill but none of them is Paid. The month exists; nothing has been collected in it yet.

status_counts counts bills of the selected period, not tenants. The values above are the D-44 seed on a fresh database at the current period: the 66 approved bills are Unpaid and room 205's is still Draft and therefore in neither bucket, so nothing is Paid until the demo takes a payment — which is why the twelfth revenue_collected entry is "0.00" and not null. (54 and 13 are the linked-tenant and vacant-room counts and belong to different tiles.) The seed has no gap month, so a fresh database produces no null anywhere in the series; the eleven history figures are water_units × 18.00 + electric_units × 8.00 + 67 × 30 × 35 to the satang, which is the property a test asserts. The arithmetic is reproducible, not the calendar — and PRD §16's 151,350 / 18,500 / 62,500 are illustrative only and must never appear as a system-produced value (D-44, §1.1).

All four are Admin only and all four take ?format=csv, producing report-monthly-2026-09.csv, report-arrears-2026-09.csv, report-room-history-2026-09.csv, report-top-usage-2026-09.csv through one shared helper (D-42). There is no PDF export, no report builder and no column chooser.

3.3.17 Settings and rates

GET /settings renders the apartment profile, due_day, the three current rates with their full history, LINE test mode, and the message counter "ส่งข้อความเดือนนี้: {N}/500" where N counts notifications rows with status='sent' in the current month, in warn colour above 450 (D-34).

POST /settings/rates
utility_type=water&unit_price=18.50&effective_from=2026-10-01&_csrf=<token>
→ 303 /settings

utility_type is water, electric or common; unit_price is DECIMAL(8,2) greater than 0 (7.50 is legal); effective_from defaults to today and may be future-dated. The row is inserted, never updated, so history is complete and auditable; a same-day repeat is 409 RATE_DUPLICATE from uq_rates(utility_type, effective_from) (D-17). Resolution is keyed to the period, not to the click:

SELECT unit_price FROM rates
 WHERE utility_type = ? AND effective_from <= CONCAT(?, '-01')
 ORDER BY effective_from DESC LIMIT 1;

For period 2026-09 that resolves 18.00 / 8.00 / 35.00 against the seed, which is what makes 10 × 18 = 180 reproducible however late generation re-runs. If Draft bills exist for a period into which a rate is back-dated, Settings shows "มีบิลฉบับร่าง {N} ใบในรอบนี้ ยังใช้อัตราเดิมอยู่" with a button that calls POST /api/bills/generate for that period — recalculating Draft bills is exactly what a generation run already does, so no separate endpoint exists.

POST /settings/profile takes apartment_name, address, tax_id (optional — the receipt omits the line when empty), phone, office_hours and due_day (1–28, database CHECK). POST /settings/run-daily-job runs the identical job body with job_name='daily_manual', skips the job_runs guard entirely and flashes "ส่งแจ้งเตือนก่อนครบกำหนด {a} ราย · เลยกำหนด {b} ราย · ส่งซ้ำที่ล้มเหลว {c} ราย" (D-32).

3.3.18 Machine endpoints

POST /internal/jobs/daily
X-Job-Token: <shared secret>

200
{ "data": { "job": "daily", "run_date": "2026-09-03", "already_ran": false,
            "pre_due": 12, "overdue": 5, "retried": 1,
            "sent": 17, "failed": 1, "skipped_no_line": 3 } }

A second call the same day returns already_ran: true with every count at 0 and is still 200 — a retrying scheduler must not see an error (D-32). A wrong or missing token is 401 with an empty body; the login rate limiter never applies here.

GET /healthz     →  200  { "data": { "ok": true, "db": "up" } }
                 →  503  { "error": { "code": "DB_UNAVAILABLE", "message": "-" } }

3.4 Role and permission matrix

Two roles on one users.role column, one middleware, no per-user permission table (D-36). The rule is stronger than navigation: money is absent from the queries that serve Staff, not hidden in the template, and there is one integration test per Staff-forbidden route asserting both 403 and that no amount appears anywhere in the response body (ADR §6.9, A-6).

PRD §12 is corrected here, deliberately

PRD §12 grants Confirm Cash Payment to เจ้าหน้าที่. PRD §4 and D-36 grant รับชำระเงิน and ออกใบเสร็จ to Admin only, and D-36 rules the §12 sentence a drafting error. This specification follows D-36: POST /payments is Admin only. This is the single place a decision overrides literal PRD text (ADR A-13.7).

3.5 LINE webhook — POST /line/webhook

Mounted with express.raw({ type: 'application/json' }) before the JSON body parser, because validateSignature needs the exact bytes. The SDK's middleware() is not used — it processes handlers before responding, which is the behaviour being avoided. The route is exempt from session auth, from CSRF and from the rate limiter (D-29, D-37).

AspectContract
AuthenticationX-Line-Signature verified against LINE_CHANNEL_SECRET. Failure ⇒ 401, empty body, nothing processed, one log line.
Response200 with an empty body — the one documented exception to the envelope rule — returned before any event is processed, so a Render cold start still ACKs inside LINE's timeout and the endpoint is never auto-disabled (D-43).
OrderingEvents are processed after the response is flushed. A processing error is logged and never retried; LINE will not resend an ACKed event.
EventAcceptedEffectReply
followyesUPSERT line_followers with userId and display name, clear unfollowed_atnone
unfollowyesSet line_followers.unfollowed_at. The tenant binding is not cleared — unfollowing is not ending a tenancynone
message, type: "text", 6 chars from the code alphabet after trim + uppercaseyesMatch an unused, unexpired link_codes row; bind line_user_id, line_display_name, line_linked_at to that tenant; set used_at; audit TENANT_LINE_LINKLINK_OK naming the room, or LINK_FAIL if no match
message, any other textyesNothing is writtenThe one fixed auto-reply pointing at the office — the OA is not a chatbot
message, non-text (image, sticker, location…)ignoredNothingnone
join, leave, postback, memberJoined, anything elseignoredNothingnone
 
flowchart TD
  IN["POST /line/webhook"] --> SIG{"X-Line-Signature valid"}
  SIG -->|no| E401["401, empty body, nothing processed"]
  SIG -->|yes| ACK["200 empty body, flushed first"]
  ACK --> EV{"event type"}
  EV -->|follow| F["upsert line_followers"]
  EV -->|unfollow| U["set unfollowed_at, binding untouched"]
  EV -->|"message text"| C{"6 chars from the code alphabet"}
  EV -->|other| IG["ignore"]
  C -->|no| AR["fixed auto-reply"]
  C -->|yes| M{"code unused and not expired"}
  M -->|yes| BIND["bind tenant, expire code, audit TENANT_LINE_LINK"]
  M -->|no| FAIL["reply LINK_FAIL"]
  BIND --> OK["reply LINK_OK naming room 205"]
The ACK is unconditional and first (D-29, D-43). LINK_OK, LINK_FAIL and the auto-reply use the Reply API with the event's reply token and write no notifications row (ENG-4).

3.6 Validation rules

Layers, per ADR §6.8: 1 the form (convenience), 2 zod at the boundary, 3 the service (rules needing the database), 4 the database (the last word). Every rule below names the layer that actually holds under a race.

Layer 4 is the one that still holds under a race

Thirteen unique keys, three generated columns and twenty-one CHECKs carry the guarantees above through a double-click, a forgotten handler check and a hand-typed UPDATE: uq_readings(room_id, period, utility_type), uq_bills(room_id, period), uq_bill_items(bill_id, item_type), uq_payments(active_bill_id), uq_tenants(active_room_id), uq_receipts(payment_id), uq_receipts(receipt_no), uq_notifications(dedupe_key), uq_job_runs(job_name, run_date), uq_rates(utility_type, effective_from), uq_rooms(room_code), uq_users(username), uq_link_codes(code). Layers 1–3 exist to give a human a good Thai message; layer 4 exists to make the wrong number impossible (ADR A-10). None of these may be cut.

3.7 Assumptions recorded by this section

  1. Form routes are the API for CRUD. Rooms, tenants, users, settings, payments, revert, void and announcements have no JSON endpoints, because ADR §6.2 fixes the JSON surface at seven endpoints. Where this section shows a "request body" for those, it is form-encoded and the response is a 303 or a re-render.
  2. No GET /api/me. Identity is req.session.user, rendered into the page. A JSON caller that lost its session learns so from 401 UNAUTHENTICATED.
  3. expected_total on POST /payments is added as a staleness guard, not as an amount input. The stored amount is still copied from bills.total in SQL (D-24); the field only turns a stale screen into a 409 instead of a wrong receipt.
  4. Single approve reuses the bulk endpoint. Bill Detail's button posts {"bill_ids":[118]} to POST /api/bills/approve, so there is one approval code path and one set of guards rather than two.
  5. Recalculating Draft bills reuses POST /api/bills/generate — a run already recalculates every Draft bill of the period in place (D-18), so D-17's คำนวณบิลฉบับร่างใหม่ button needs no endpoint of its own.
  6. The PAID message is drained by the receipt page calling POST /api/notifications/drain once on load; the daily job would deliver it anyway. No per-message send endpoint exists.
  7. A webhook text message is treated as a code attempt when, after trimming and upper-casing, it is exactly 6 characters drawn from the D-29 alphabet. Anything else gets the fixed auto-reply. Without this test the system cannot distinguish a mistyped code from small talk.
  8. D-37's "first login forces a change" is realised as GET/POST /password plus runbook R1 step 5 and a dashboard banner, not as a forced redirect: the ADR's users table carries no must_change_password column, and inventing one here would contradict the schema section.
  9. Page size 50 and the /readings no-pagination exception are chosen here; the ADR fixes neither. Against the seed (80 rooms, ≤80 bills per period) pagination is reachable only on the all-period bill list.
  10. Announcement drains use multicast chunks of at most 500 recipients per call, while personalised types drain at most 20 rows per call, per D-34's delivery split and ADR §7.6's batch cap.

4. Billing Engine & Business Rules

Everything that turns two meter numbers into an amount a tenant owes, and everything that happens to that amount afterwards. Every rule cites the decision (D-xx) it comes from and carries a worked example with full arithmetic. Where a rule needs SQL, the SQL is the specification — JavaScript never multiplies, sums or rounds money (D-16, ADR A-4).

Where these rules live in the code

services/billing.js, services/payment.js, services/jobs.js, services/reports.js, plus the pure helpers lib/billStatus.js, lib/datetime.js and lib/money.js. Routes parse and redirect; services own transactions, rules and their own SQL; lib/ is pure functions (ADR §5, A-7). A req object never reaches a service and a SQL string never appears in a route.

4.1 The billing period and month selection

A billing period is the billing month: a plain CHAR(7) YYYY-MM string stored on meter_readings.period and bills.period (D-11). There is no periods table, no Admin "open period" action, no lock and no closed state — the word does not appear in this document set, in any screen or in any test.

  • Selection. Meter Reading and Monthly Billing each carry one month picker defaulting to the current calendar month in Asia/Bangkok: DATE_FORMAT(CURDATE(), '%Y-%m'). It is passed as ?period=YYYY-MM and validated by zod against /^\d{4}-(0[1-9]|1[0-2])$/; anything else is 422 PERIOD_INVALID.
  • Ordering. CHAR(7) string order is chronological, so ORDER BY period DESC is both correct and index-friendly, and "strictly earlier period" is period < ? (D-14).
  • Period first day. The derived value CONCAT(period, '-01') is used by rate resolution and by the due-date rule. It is referred to below as the period first day.
  • Coverage. The consumption a period bills is whatever accrued since that room's previous reading, which is not guaranteed to be the previous calendar month (D-14). Periods need not be contiguous and a gap never blocks a run.
  • Protection. Data is protected by bill status, not by a period state machine: a reading is editable exactly while its room's bill for that period is ฉบับร่าง or does not yet exist (D-12, §4.7 below).
  • Progress is shown, not stored. Monthly Billing renders one derived line for the selected period: บิลครบทุกห้อง {billed}/{billable} · อนุมัติแล้ว {approved} · ชำระแล้ว {paid} (D-11).

4.2 Rate resolution

rates is append-only and effective-dated; a Settings save INSERTs and never UPDATEs (D-17). Resolution is keyed to the period, never to the moment Generate was pressed, so a late second run cannot put two electricity rates in one month.

-- billing.resolveRate(utilityType, periodFirstDay)
SELECT unit_price
  FROM rates
 WHERE utility_type = ?                       -- 'water' | 'electric' | 'common'
   AND effective_from <= ?                    -- CONCAT(period, '-01')
 ORDER BY effective_from DESC
 LIMIT 1;

Three rates are resolved once per generation run, before the room loop. If any of the three returns no row the whole run is refused with 422 RATE_NOT_FOUND and nothing is written — a run that silently priced water at zero would be worse than a run that did not happen. The resolved unit_price is copied onto the bill_items row next to the quantity, and every screen, print view and LINE message renders from that snapshot (D-09, D-17). Approved bills are never disturbed by a later rate row.

Back-dating a rate into a period that already has Draft bills

Settings shows มีบิลฉบับร่าง {N} ใบในรอบนี้ ยังใช้อัตราเดิมอยู่ with the button คำนวณบิลฉบับร่างใหม่, which re-runs POST /api/bills/generate for that period — a run already recalculates every Draft bill of the period in place, so this button needs no endpoint of its own (D-17, D-18). Approved bills are untouched by construction: recalculateBill() carries AND status = 'Draft' in every statement it issues.

4.3 Previous-reading resolution

One code path, no special cases (D-06, D-14). Opening readings live on rooms.baseline_water / rooms.baseline_electric; no synthetic meter_readings row is ever written, so nothing can collide with a room's first real reading under uq_readings(room_id, period, utility_type).

-- billing.resolvePreviousReading(roomId, utilityType, period)
SELECT current_reading, period
  FROM meter_readings
 WHERE room_id = ? AND utility_type = ? AND period < ?    -- strictly earlier
 ORDER BY period DESC
 LIMIT 1;

-- no row  ->  previous_reading = rooms.baseline_<utility>, previous_period = 'baseline'
PropertyWhy it holds
A month entered late resolves identically to one entered on timeOrdering is by period, never by recorded_at (D-14).
Ties are impossibleuq_readings(room_id, period, utility_type).
An UPSERT can never take itself as its own previousThe strict < excludes the row being written — which is what makes D-12's double submit a non-event.
A first reading is never billed as 1,260 unitsBaselines live on the room, so the fallback is a real opening number, not 0 (D-06).
Readings taken while a room was vacant still countThe lookup does not filter on tenancy (D-07, D-14).

previous_reading, units and previous_period are snapshots computed at save time. That is exactly why reverting is restricted to a room's most recent bill (D-22, §4.9.1): editing an older month would leave the next month's snapshot stale. previous_period is VARCHAR(8) on both meter_readings and bill_items because it holds either a period or the literal 'baseline' (ADR §6.7).

When the resolved previous_period is not the immediately preceding month, Bill Detail shows ครอบคลุมการใช้ตั้งแต่รอบ {previous_period_th} and the BILL LINE message appends the same sentence (D-14, D-31). Units are never split or back-dated across the skipped months and no bill is created for a skipped period.

4.4 Formulas and rounding

units_water = current_reading − previous_reading (INT UNSIGNED, CHECK units >= 0)
units_electric = current_reading − previous_reading
units (meter replaced) = (old_final − previous_reading) + (current_reading − new_start)

amount_water = ROUND(units_water × rate_water, 2)
amount_electric = ROUND(units_electric × rate_electric, 2)
amount_common = ROUND(area_sqm × rate_common, 2)

bills.total = amount_water + amount_electric + amount_common (SQL SUM of the already-rounded items)
QuantityTypeSourceRounding
Meter readings, unitsINT UNSIGNEDTyped by Staff; units computed at save timeNone — whole units only; the input rejects decimals and negatives (D-16)
Unit pricesDECIMAL(8,2)rates.unit_price snapshotted onto the itemStored as entered; 7.50 is legal (D-16)
Room areaDECIMAL(6,2)bills.room_area_sqm snapshot, never a live join (D-09)Stored as entered
Item amountDECIMAL(10,2)ROUND(quantity * unit_price, 2) in the INSERTHalf-up, once, at the item
Bill totalDECIMAL(10,2)SUM(bill_items.amount)None — no second rounding, no rounding to whole baht, no satang suppression (D-16)

The consequence is a guarantee that can be checked by hand on the paper: the total always equals the sum of the printed lines. FLOAT and DOUBLE appear nowhere in the schema, the pool sets decimalNumbers off so mysql2 returns DECIMAL as a string, and lib/money.js contains no arithmetic at all — only formatting: 1,790.00 in tables, on Bill Detail and on the receipt, 1,790 บาท in LINE messages and sentence text (D-03). Rounding is observable only on the common-area fee, because integer units times a two-decimal rate are already exact:

quantityunit_priceexact productstored amountNote
1018.00180.0000180.00Exact — the demo water line
658.50552.5000552.50Exact, carries satang (D-27 words path)
337.50247.5000247.50Exact
30.2535.551,075.38751,075.39Half-up at the item — the only rounding site in the system
30.2335.151,062.58451,062.58One ROUND at the item, never a chain — rounding to 3 dp first would give 1,062.59

4.5 The generation algorithm

Trigger (D-18): the Meter Reading page's primary action ส่งให้นิติตรวจสอบ (Staff and Admin), enabled once at least one room in the period has both readings; Admin may re-run any time from Monthly Billing via สร้าง/อัปเดตบิลฉบับร่าง. Both call POST /api/bills/generate. There is no separate "submitted" state — running generation is the submission.

flowchart TB
  S["Run generation for period P"] --> R["Resolve the three rates at the period first day"]
  R --> L["List billable rooms"]
  L --> N{"Both readings saved?"}
  N -->|no| U["count unread, write nothing"]
  N -->|yes| B{"Bill for this room and P?"}
  B -->|none| C["INSERT Draft bill with snapshots"]
  B -->|"Draft"| K["keep the row, recalculate in place"]
  B -->|"Unpaid or Paid"| SK["count skipped, touch nothing"]
  C --> I["UPSERT exactly three bill_items"]
  K --> I
  I --> T["UPDATE total to the SUM of the items"]
  T --> Z["summary: created, updated, skipped, unread"]
  U --> Z
  SK --> Z
One run, one transaction per room. Steps that write are the only ones that can fail, and a failure isolates to a single room.

4.5.1 The billable-room query (D-07, ENG-1)

-- billing.billableRooms(conn)
SELECT r.id AS room_id, r.room_code, r.area_sqm,
       t.id AS tenant_id, t.full_name AS tenant_name
  FROM rooms r
  JOIN tenants t ON t.active_room_id = r.id     -- generated column, UNIQUE (ENG-1)
 WHERE r.is_active = 1
   AND r.status = 'Occupied'
 ORDER BY r.room_code;

Because tenants.active_room_id is a stored generated column with a unique index, this join returns at most one tenant per room — there is no LIMIT 1 that could silently pick the wrong tenant. A room that is Vacant, deactivated, or has no Active tenant is not in the result set and therefore has no bill, no reading requirement, no common-area fee and no LINE recipient (D-07).

4.5.2 Pseudo-code

// services/billing.js
async function generatePeriod(period, actor) {
  assertPeriod(period);                               // 422 PERIOD_INVALID
  const first = period + '-01';                       // the period first day
  const rate = {
    water:    await resolveRate('water',    first),
    electric: await resolveRate('electric', first),
    common:   await resolveRate('common',   first),
  };
  if (!rate.water || !rate.electric || !rate.common) throw AppError(422, 'RATE_NOT_FOUND');

  const dueDay  = await settings.dueDay();            // 1..28, CHECK-enforced
  const summary = { period, created: 0, updated: 0, skipped: 0, unread: 0, missing_rooms: [] };

  for (const room of await billableRooms()) {
    await withTx(async (conn) => {
      const r = await readingsFor(conn, room.room_id, period);   // { water?, electric? }
      if (!r.water || !r.electric) {                            // D-23: no bill, no placeholder
        summary.unread++; summary.missing_rooms.push(room.room_code); return;
      }

      // Serialise concurrent runs on this room; uq_bills(room_id, period) is the backstop.
      let bill = await selectBillForUpdate(conn, room.room_id, period);

      if (bill && bill.status !== 'Draft') { summary.skipped++; return; }   // D-18 rule 3

      if (!bill) {
        bill = await insertDraftBill(conn, {                    // snapshots, D-09
          room_id: room.room_id, tenant_id: room.tenant_id, period,
          room_code_snapshot: room.room_code,
          room_area_sqm:      room.area_sqm,
          tenant_name_snapshot: room.tenant_name,
          status: 'Draft', total: '0.00',
          due_date: draftDueDate(period, dueDay),               // preview only, D-20
        });
        summary.created++;
      } else {
        summary.updated++;
      }

      await upsertItem(conn, bill.id, 'water',    r.water.units,    rate.water,    r.water);
      await upsertItem(conn, bill.id, 'electric', r.electric.units, rate.electric, r.electric);
      await upsertItem(conn, bill.id, 'common',   bill.room_area_sqm, rate.common, null);
      await refreshTotal(conn, bill.id);
    });
  }
  return summary;      // { period, created, updated, skipped, unread, missing_rooms }
}

4.5.3 The three writes, in SQL

-- (a) the Draft bill, with its three snapshot columns and the preview due date
INSERT INTO bills (room_id, tenant_id, period, room_code_snapshot, room_area_sqm,
                   tenant_name_snapshot, total, status, due_date)
VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 0.00, 'Draft',
        LEAST(DATE_ADD(CONCAT(?, '-01'), INTERVAL ? - 1 DAY),
              LAST_DAY(CONCAT(?, '-01'))));

-- (b) one item, three times per bill: water, electric, common.
--     uq_bill_items(bill_id, item_type) makes a fourth or duplicate item impossible (ENG-2).
INSERT INTO bill_items (bill_id, item_type, quantity, unit_price, amount,
                        previous_reading, current_reading, previous_period)
VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ROUND(? * ?, 2), ?, ?, ?) AS new
ON DUPLICATE KEY UPDATE
  quantity         = new.quantity,
  unit_price       = new.unit_price,
  amount           = new.amount,
  previous_reading = new.previous_reading,
  current_reading  = new.current_reading,
  previous_period  = new.previous_period;

-- (c) the total is a SUM of already-rounded rows, never a JS addition (D-16)
UPDATE bills b
   SET b.total = (SELECT SUM(bi.amount) FROM bill_items bi WHERE bi.bill_id = b.id)
 WHERE b.id = ? AND b.status = 'Draft';

The common item carries quantity = bills.room_area_sqm and previous_reading, current_reading, previous_period all NULL. Exactly three items are always written, even at 0 units and 0.00 (D-18, D-41) — that is what makes the Dashboard tiles sum to the billed total, the receipt print three lines, and D-42's split columns never null. A bill with fewer than three items is a defect with its own assertion.

4.5.4 Idempotency

uq_bills(room_id, period) is the idempotency key of the entire run (D-18). The three rules, stated so they can be tested one at a time:

  1. A run creates a Draft bill for every billable room that has both readings and no bill.
  2. A run recalculates in place every existing Draft bill for the period — the row keeps its id, so audit rows, notification rows and links to it survive.
  3. A run never touches a bill whose stored status is Unpaid or Paid. It is counted in skipped and no statement is issued against it.

Recalculation never re-reads live rooms or tenants values — the three snapshot columns (room_code_snapshot, room_area_sqm, tenant_name_snapshot) are written once, at create, and every item is priced from them (D-09). This is why the common item is upserted with bill.room_area_sqm and not room.area_sqm: after an Admin edits a room's area, a recalculated Draft bill must still print 30 ตร.ม. × 35 บาท = 1,050 บาท, an equation whose printed factors and result agree (D-16).

There is no delete-and-recreate path anywhere in the engine, because such a path could destroy an approved bill. Two operators pressing Generate simultaneously are serialised by SELECT … FOR UPDATE; if the row is created between the SELECT and the INSERT, the unique key raises ER_DUP_ENTRY, the service re-reads the row and counts it as updated.

Scenario (67 billable rooms, 64 read)createdupdatedskippedunreadEffect on money
First run of period 2026-096400364 Draft bills created
Immediate second run (the double-click)06403None — same amounts recomputed onto the same rows
Run after one reading was corrected06403One Draft bill's items and total change
Run after 64 bills were approved00643None — approved bills are inviolable
Run after the 3 missing rooms were read306403 new Draft bills, at the same rates (D-17)

Result string, displayed verbatim (D-18): สร้างบิลใหม่ {created} ใบ · อัปเดต {updated} ใบ · ข้าม (อนุมัติแล้ว) {skipped} ใบ · ยังไม่ได้จด {unread} ห้อง. Whenever unread > 0, Monthly Billing shows the persistent warn callout ยังไม่ได้จดมิเตอร์ {N} ห้อง: 108, 312, 405 — ยังไม่ได้สร้างบิล with the room codes linking to Meter Reading (D-23). Approval is never blocked by missing rooms.

4.6 Worked examples

Every example below is complete arithmetic against real rows. Rates unless stated: น้ำ 18.00 บาท/หน่วย, ไฟ 8.00 บาท/หน่วย, ส่วนกลาง 35.00 บาท/ตร.ม., effective_from before every period shown. Due day 5.

4.6.1 The demo scenario — room 205, period 2026-09 (PRD §28)

Itempreviouscurrentquantityunit_priceamount
ค่าน้ำ1,2501,26010 หน่วย18.00180.00
ค่าไฟ5,2805,35070 หน่วย8.00560.00
ค่าส่วนกลาง——30.00 ตร.ม.35.001,050.00
ยอดรวม1,790.00
1,260 − 1,250 = 10 · 10 × 18.00 = 180.00
5,350 − 5,280 = 70 · 70 × 8.00 = 560.00
30.00 × 35.00 = 1,050.00
180.00 + 560.00 + 1,050.00 = 1,790.00

Approved 1 ก.ย. 2026 → due_date = MAX(2026-09-05, 2026-09-01 + 3 = 2026-09-04) = 2026-09-05. Paid in cash 3 ก.ย. 2026 → receipt REC-202609-000852. Status track: ฉบับร่าง → รอชำระ → ชำระแล้ว.

4.6.2 Edge case A — first reading, no previous month (D-06, D-14)

Room 108, 30.00 ตร.ม., created with baseline_water = 400, baseline_electric = 1,000, baseline_date = 2026-08-15. Its first reading is period 2026-09: water 415, electric 1,120. No earlier meter_readings row exists, so the lookup returns nothing and the room's baselines apply with previous_period = 'baseline'.

Itempreviousprevious_periodcurrentquantityunit_priceamount
ค่าน้ำ400baseline41515 หน่วย18.00270.00
ค่าไฟ1,000baseline1,120120 หน่วย8.00960.00
ค่าส่วนกลาง———30.00 ตร.ม.35.001,050.00
ยอดรวม2,280.00
415 − 400 = 15 → 270.00 · 1,120 − 1,000 = 120 → 960.00 · 30.00 × 35.00 = 1,050.00 · total = 2,280.00

Baselines are Admin-only and editable only while the room has no meter_readings row at all (D-06). After this save the fields become read-only, because changing them would silently rewrite an already-billed quantity. The soft flag ใช้สูงผิดปกติ does not fire: it requires a previous-period usage of at least 10 units for the same utility, and a room whose previous value came from a baseline has none (D-13).

4.6.3 Edge case B — meter rollover / meter replacement (D-13)

Room 210, 30.00 ตร.ม. The 4-digit water dial rolled past 9,999 and the meter was replaced on 2 ก.ย. 2026. Staff tick เปลี่ยนมิเตอร์ในรอบนี้, which reveals เลขสุดท้ายของมิเตอร์เดิม (old_final = 9,999), เลขเริ่มต้นของมิเตอร์ใหม่ (new_start = 0) and a required Thai reason. Previous water reading (period 2026-08) is 9,980. Electricity is an ordinary reading: 3,100 → 3,180.

units_water = (old_final − previous_reading) + (current_reading − new_start)
           = (9,999 − 9,980) + (15 − 0) = 19 + 15 = 34 หน่วย
validation: old_final ≥ previous_reading (9,999 ≥ 9,980 ✓) AND current_reading ≥ new_start (15 ≥ 0 ✓)
Itempreviouscurrentquantityunit_priceamount
ค่าน้ำ (เปลี่ยนมิเตอร์)9,9801534 หน่วย18.00612.00
ค่าไฟ3,1003,18080 หน่วย8.00640.00
ค่าส่วนกลาง——30.00 ตร.ม.35.001,050.00
ยอดรวม2,302.00
Rules this case pins down
  • With the checkbox ticked, the current-below-previous hard block does not apply — otherwise 15 < 9,980 would be rejected. With it off, 15 < 9,980 is 422 READING_BELOW_PREVIOUS with the inline Thai message เลขมิเตอร์ปัจจุบันน้อยกว่าเดือนก่อน (9,980) กรุณาตรวจสอบ.
  • Either validation failing is 422 REPLACEMENT_INVALID. Both keep units >= 0, which the CHECK enforces regardless.
  • There is no automatic rollover detection. A rolled dial is entered as a meter replacement, which is why the branch is available to Staff as well as Admin — they are the ones standing at the meter (D-13).
  • Next month's previous reading is 15, not 9,999: the lookup reads current_reading, which is the new meter's number.
  • The replacement is audited as METER_REPLACEMENT with the reason and the entering user (D-38), and Admin reviews it at the approval gate that already exists.

4.6.4 Edge case C — vacant room (D-07)

Room 409, 30.00 ตร.ม., status = 'Vacant', no Active tenant. Staff record a voluntary reading through the ห้องว่าง filter: water 800 → 806, electric 2,000 → 2,004.

QuestionAnswer
Bill produced?None. The room is not in billableRooms(), so no row is written for it in any period while it is vacant.
Common-area fee?None. There is no owner invoice and no zero-total bill.
Counted in unread?No. unread counts billable rooms only, so the Monthly Billing banner never lists a vacant room.
Counted in the reading counter?No. The ห้องว่าง filter is outside the X/Y denominator (D-15).
Does the reading survive?Yes. Stored as an ordinary meter_readings row with the badge ไม่ออกบิล on screen.
What happens when it is re-let?806 and 2,004 become the incoming tenant's previous readings through the ordinary D-14 lookup — no baseline edit, no re-entry, nothing billed twice.

Effect on the Dashboard: room 409 appears in ห้องทั้งหมด and in ห้องว่าง, and in none of the money tiles. Against the D-44 seed the tile reads 66 × 30 × 35 = 69,300.00 on a freshly reset database, because room 205's current-period bill is still Draft and D-41 scopes the money tiles to bills whose stored status is Unpaid or Paid. The moment the demo approves room 205 it becomes 67 × 30 × 35 = 70,350.00 — the PRD §16 common-area headline, reproduced exactly because vacant rooms are excluded. Both numbers are assertable; which one applies depends on whether the approval has happened.

4.6.5 Edge case D — tenant move-out and move-in mid-period (D-08, D-10)

Room 312, 30.00 ตร.ม. The outgoing tenant leaves on 12 ก.ย. 2026; a new tenant moves in on 16 ก.ย. 2026. Nothing is prorated (D-08, and limitation 1 of D-05). The whole period's charges fall on the tenant who is Active on the generation date, which is why D-08 fixes the order of operations.

  1. 12 ก.ย. — record the final reading for both utilities, period 2026-09: water 2,402 → 2,410, electric 7,205 → 7,300.
  2. 12 ก.ย. — run generation for period 2026-09. The outgoing tenant is still Active, so the room is billable and the bill is created against them.
  3. อนุมัติและส่งบิล → รอชำระ, LINE BILL sent to the outgoing tenant's still-bound account.
  4. Cash at the counter → ชำระแล้ว, receipt issued, LINE PAID sent.
  5. Only now สิ้นสุดสัญญาเช่า unlocks: it sets tenants.status = 'Inactive', writes move_out_date = 2026-09-12, clears the LINE binding and sets rooms.status = 'Vacant' in one transaction, audited as TENANCY_END with the Thai reason (D-10, ENG-5).
  6. 16 ก.ย. — the new tenant record is created Active; the room returns to Occupied.
BillPayerItemprevcurrqtyrateamount
2026-09Outgoing tenantค่าน้ำ2,4022,410818.00144.00
ค่าไฟ7,2057,300958.00760.00
ค่าส่วนกลาง——30.0035.001,050.00
ยอดรวม1,954.00
2026-10Incoming tenantค่าน้ำ2,4102,417718.00126.00
ค่าไฟ7,3007,362628.00496.00
ค่าส่วนกลาง——30.0035.001,050.00
ยอดรวม1,672.00
The consequence, stated plainly

The 2026-10 bill covers consumption from 12 ก.ย. to the October reading, which includes the 12–16 ก.ย. window when the room was vacant, and the incoming tenant pays a full common-area fee for a month they occupied from the 16th. That is the documented no-proration limitation, not a defect (D-05 limitation 1, D-08). There is no on-screen mitigation for this case — the 2026-10 bill's previous reading came from period 2026-09, which is the immediately preceding month, so D-14's ครอบคลุมการใช้ตั้งแต่รอบ … note does not fire on Bill Detail or in the BILL message. The office explains the no-proration rule in person; it is limitation 1 of D-05.

What blocks step 5 if it is attempted early: สิ้นสุดสัญญาเช่า is refused unless (a) a final reading exists for both utilities for that room dated on or after the last period the room was billed for, and (b) the bill covering that reading exists and is Paid. Without this gate the departing tenant's last month would be skipped by the run — the room would be Vacant on the generation date — and silently charged to the next tenant (D-08).

4.6.6 Edge case E — a rate change between periods (D-17)

The electricity rate rises to 8.50 from 1 ต.ค. 2026. Settings INSERTs ('electric', 8.50, '2026-10-01', admin); the existing ('electric', 8.00, …) row is never updated.

PeriodPeriod first dayRate row selectedunit_priceWhy
2026-092026-09-01effective_from 2026-01-018.002026-10-01 > 2026-09-01, so the newer row is not eligible
2026-102026-10-01effective_from 2026-10-018.50Greatest effective_from ≤ the period first day

Room 205 in 2026-10: water 1,260 → 1,272, electric 5,350 → 5,415.

1,272 − 1,260 = 12 · 12 × 18.00 = 216.00
5,415 − 5,350 = 65 · 65 × 8.50 = 552.50
30.00 × 35.00 = 1,050.00
216.00 + 552.50 + 1,050.00 = 1,818.50

The receipt for this bill exercises the satang branch of lib/thaiBaht.js: หนึ่งพันแปดร้อยสิบแปดบาทห้าสิบสตางค์ (D-27). Three further consequences to test:

  • The room 205 bill for 2026-09, already approved at 1,790.00, is unchanged — the item carries unit_price = 8.00 as a snapshot.
  • Re-running generation for 2026-09 after the rate change still produces 8.00, because resolution is keyed to the period, not to the click. Three rooms read late in the same month therefore cannot carry a second rate (D-17).
  • If 8.50 had been back-dated to 2026-09-01 while Draft bills existed for 2026-09, Settings would show มีบิลฉบับร่าง {N} ใบในรอบนี้ ยังใช้อัตราเดิมอยู่; pressing คำนวณบิลฉบับร่างใหม่ recalculates the Drafts to 70 × 8.50 = 595.00 and leaves every approved bill at 560.00.

4.7 Reading submission and review — what is locked when

There is exactly one gate in the whole engine (D-19). Reviewing the readings and approving the bill are the same act; there is no separate reading-approval step and no submitted state.

flowchart LR
  A["Staff walks the round on a phone"] --> B["POST /api/readings, one room per request"]
  B --> C["Row UPSERT on room, period, utility"]
  C --> D{"Room's bill for this period is Draft?"}
  D -->|yes| E["recalculateBill in the same transaction"]
  D -->|no bill yet| F["nothing to recalculate"]
  E --> G["Sticky counter: จดแล้ว X/Y"]
  F --> G
  G --> H["ส่งให้นิติตรวจสอบ, Staff or Admin"]
  H --> I["POST /api/bills/generate for the period"]
  I --> J["Admin reviews on Monthly Billing or Bill Detail"]
  J --> K["อนุมัติและส่งบิล, Admin only"]
Submission is a generation run, not a status. Everything upstream of approval stays editable; everything downstream is frozen.

Per-room save (D-15): each card holds read-only Previous and a numeric Current for both utilities plus its own บันทึก button posting that room alone; a room counts as จดแล้ว only when both utilities are saved. A failed save re-renders inline with the typed values preserved and a ลองอีกครั้ง action, so lost connectivity costs at most the room being typed (D-40).

4.7.1 The lock matrix

Editable thingNo bill / ฉบับร่างรอชำระ / เกินกำหนดชำระแล้วRule
meter_readings.current_readingStaff + Admin✗ frozen✗ frozenD-12
Meter-replacement fields and reasonStaff + Admin✗ frozen✗ frozenD-13
bill_items quantity, unit_price, amountRewritten by every run and every reading save✗ frozen✗ frozenD-18, D-22
bills.totalRecomputed as SUM(items)✗ frozen✗ frozenD-16, D-22
bills.due_datePreview only, recomputed at approval✗ frozen✗ frozenD-20
Bill snapshots (room code, area, tenant name)Rewritten on create only✗ frozen✗ frozenD-09
rooms.area_sqmAdmin, audited — affects future bills onlyAdmin, audited — this bill unaffectedAdmin, audited — this bill unaffectedD-09, D-38
ratesAdmin, insert-only; Drafts recalculable on demandAdmin, insert-only; approved bills never affectedD-17
rooms.baseline_water / baseline_electricAdmin, and only while the room has zero meter_readings rows in any periodD-06
Payment amountNever editable in any state — it is always bills.total, never an inputD-24

The frozen state is shown, not hidden: the Meter Reading form renders read-only fields with บิลอนุมัติแล้ว แก้ไขไม่ได้, and the API refuses the write with 422 READING_FROZEN for every role, including Admin. The only way back is a revert (§4.9).

4.8 Approval — อนุมัติและส่งบิล

4.8.1 Preconditions

PreconditionEnforced byFailure
Actor's role is adminrequireRole('admin') in middleware/auth.js403, Thai 403 page for HTML (D-36, D-40)

Every billable room has a billNot a precondition. The office may approve the bills that existWarn banner only (D-23)

4.8.2 The due date is computed once, at approval, and frozen (D-20)

due_date = MAX( LEAST(due_day of the period month, last day of that month), approval_date + 3 days )
UPDATE bills
   SET status      = 'Unpaid',
       approved_by = ?,
       approved_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
       due_date    = GREATEST(
         LEAST(DATE_ADD(CONCAT(period, '-01'), INTERVAL ? - 1 DAY),   -- settings.due_day
               LAST_DAY(CONCAT(period, '-01'))),
         DATE_ADD(CURDATE(), INTERVAL 3 DAY))
 WHERE id = ? AND status = 'Draft';
Perioddue_dayApproval dateday-of-month candidateapproval + 3frozen due_date
2026-0951 ก.ย. 20262026-09-052026-09-042026-09-05 (PRD §28)
2026-0954 ก.ย. 20262026-09-052026-09-072026-09-07
2026-09520 ก.ย. 20262026-09-052026-09-232026-09-23
2026-022826 ก.พ. 20262026-02-282026-03-012026-03-01

The 1–28 range on settings.due_day is a CHECK, so the LEAST(…, LAST_DAY(…)) clamp can never bite; it is written anyway. The due date is never hand-editable — there is no per-bill due-date field — and every bill approved on the same day in the same batch gets the same date. This is also what guarantees the D−2 reminder is never scheduled in the past.

4.8.3 What becomes immutable

At approval the bill is frozen: readings, units, unit prices, room area, tenant name, line amounts, total and due date (D-22). None may be changed afterwards through any endpoint, by any role. The write guard is a single check on bills.status, and it is expressed as a WHERE clause on every statement rather than as a disabled button.

4.8.4 Sequence

sequenceDiagram
  autonumber
  participant A as "Admin browser"
  participant R as "POST /api/bills/approve"
  participant S as "services/billing.js"
  participant DB as MySQL
  participant N as "POST /api/notifications/drain"
  participant L as "LINE Messaging API"
  A->>R: "selected bill ids plus the CSRF token"
  R->>S: "approveBills(ids, actor)"
  S->>DB: BEGIN
  S->>DB: "UPDATE bills to Unpaid, due_date frozen, WHERE status is Draft"
  S->>DB: "INSERT audit_log BILL_APPROVE with before and after JSON"
  S->>DB: "INSERT notifications type BILL, one row per bill"
  S->>DB: COMMIT
  S-->>A: "approved, already_approved, queued, skipped_no_line — under 5 seconds"
  loop "until remaining is 0"
    A->>N: "drain at most 20 rows"
    N->>DB: "GET_LOCK notif_drain, claim the batch"
    N->>L: "push BILL with the row's stored retry key"
    N->>DB: "UPDATE row to sent or failed, attempt_count plus one"
    N-->>A: "running counts"
  end
Two round trips (D-19). The commit is fast and total; delivery is resumable and loses nothing if the browser closes — the 09:00 job drains whatever is left (D-32).

Money state and messaging state are independent (D-30): the status change commits first and every push happens after the commit, never inside the transaction. A LINE outage can never prevent billing and never rolls anything back. The result summary stays on screen: อนุมัติ {N} ใบ · ส่ง LINE สำเร็จ {x} · ยังไม่ได้ส่ง (ไม่มี LINE) {y} · ส่งไม่สำเร็จ {z}, with the failed and unsent rooms listed and linked.

Bill Detail's single-bill approve is the same endpoint with an array of one: it posts {"bill_ids": [118]} to POST /api/bills/approve, which calls the same approveBills([id], actor), and then drains that one row inline — one approval code path, one set of guards, and the scripted demo path for room 205.

4.9 Corrections after approval

flowchart TB
  Q{"Stored status of the bill?"}
  Q -->|Draft| E["Edit the reading; the Draft recalculates on save"]
  Q -->|"Unpaid, may display as Overdue"| N{"Is it the room's newest bill?"}
  Q -->|Paid| V["Return the cash across the counter FIRST"]
  N -->|no| X["Refused 422 REVERT_NOT_NEWEST — correct the newest period instead"]
  N -->|yes| RV["ตีกลับเป็นฉบับร่าง, Thai reason of at least 10 characters"]
  V --> VD["ยกเลิกการชำระเงิน: payment and receipt voided, bill back to Unpaid"]
  VD --> N
  RV --> E
  E --> AP["อนุมัติและส่งบิล again; LINE resends as BILL_CORRECTED"]
One reversible path covers both cases and keeps exactly three stored statuses. The system never holds a credit, a refund or a balance (D-22, D-25).

4.9.1 Revert to Draft (D-22)

-- inside one transaction, both statements on the same connection
SELECT status FROM bills WHERE id = ? FOR UPDATE;                    -- must be 'Unpaid'
SELECT 1 FROM bills WHERE room_id = ? AND period > ? LIMIT 1;        -- must return no row

UPDATE bills SET status = 'Draft', approved_by = NULL, approved_at = NULL
 WHERE id = ? AND status = 'Unpaid';                                 -- 0 rows -> 409 BILL_NOT_REVERTABLE

-- audit(conn, { action: 'BILL_REVERT', bill_id, before_json, after_json, reason })
GuardValueFailure
Roleadmin only403
ReasonThai text, minimum 10 characters, stored in audit_log.reason422 REASON_TOO_SHORT
Newest bill for the roomNo bill exists for the same room with a greater period422 REVERT_NOT_NEWEST, button disabled with มีบิลรอบถัดไปแล้ว ไม่สามารถแก้ไขย้อนหลังได้ — กรุณาแก้ในรอบล่าสุด
Stored statusUnpaid (which may display as เกินกำหนด)409 BILL_NOT_REVERTABLE — the double-click case, where the bill is already Draft, lands here too

The newest-bill restriction is not a convenience: a later bill's previous_reading is a snapshot of the value being edited (D-12), so allowing an older edit would leave the chain stale. It is enforced by the query above, not by the disabled button.

After the revert the readings unfreeze, the correction is made, and re-approval re-sends the message as BILL_CORRECTED — the BILL body prefixed with 🔄 แก้ไขบิล (ยกเลิกยอดเดิม) (D-31). Its own dedupe_key differs by type, so a same-day correction is not swallowed by the BILL row (ENG-3).

4.9.2 Correcting a Paid bill (D-25, D-22)

A counter transaction, not a system transaction. The cash goes back across the counter first — the confirm dialog says so: คืนเงินสด {total} บาท ให้ผู้เช่าก่อนยืนยันการยกเลิก, with the reason field required before the primary button enables (D-39).

-- the route is keyed on the PAYMENT id, so the guard is too
UPDATE payments SET voided_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP, voided_by = ?, void_reason = ?
 WHERE id = ? AND voided_at IS NULL;               -- 0 rows -> see the mapping below
UPDATE receipts SET voided_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP WHERE payment_id = ?;
UPDATE bills   SET status = 'Unpaid' WHERE id = ? AND status = 'Paid';
-- audit(conn, { action: 'PAYMENT_VOID', bill_id, reason })
0 rows affected because…ResponseThai message
No payments row has that :id404 NOT_FOUNDไม่พบข้อมูลที่ต้องการ
The row exists but voided_at is already set — the double-click409 PAYMENT_ALREADY_VOIDEDการชำระเงินนี้ถูกยกเลิกไปแล้ว

PAYMENT_NOT_FOUND is not raised by this route. It is reserved for the bill-scoped lookup — "void the live payment of bill N" — which Bill Detail uses to resolve a payment id before it can call this endpoint, and which fails when the bill has no live payment at all.

  • Nothing is deleted. The receipt keeps its number, and every view and print of it is stamped ยกเลิก in red across the header (D-25, D-27).
  • payments.active_bill_id becomes NULL, which frees the UNIQUE(active_bill_id) slot so the bill can be paid again later. A plain UNIQUE(bill_id) would make a corrected bill unpayable forever — this mechanism is binding (D-26).
  • The bill returns to Unpaid and therefore displays as เกินกำหนด automatically if the due date has passed.
  • No LINE message is sent on the void itself — the office explains in person.
  • The bill is thereafter excluded from automatic PRE_DUE and OVERDUE sends and shows the badge ระงับการแจ้งเตือนอัตโนมัติ (D-25, D-33). Admin's per-bill manual resend still works.
  • Re-payment issues a new receipt number; the old one stays taken. The corrected LINE message carries ยอดที่ชำระไว้เดิมได้คืนเป็นเงินสดแล้ว กรุณาชำระตามยอดใหม่ (D-22, D-31).

4.10 Cash payment

Full payment only, one live payment per bill (D-24). The stored amount is fixed at bills.total and is not an input: the Payment screen shows ยอดที่ต้องชำระ 1,790.00 บาท as read-only text with the room and the billing period in the header. The รับเงิน / เงินทอน calculator above the button (2,000 → 210.00) is screen-only — neither value is stored, printed or sent to the API. Confirm Cash Payment is Admin only; PRD §12's grant to เจ้าหน้าที่ contradicts PRD §4 and is a drafting error corrected by D-36 (ADR A-13.7).

sequenceDiagram
  autonumber
  participant A as "Admin at the counter"
  participant P as "POST /payments"
  participant S as "services/payment.js"
  participant DB as MySQL
  participant L as "LINE Messaging API"
  A->>P: "ยืนยันรับเงินสด, after the confirm dialog"
  P->>S: "confirmCashPayment(billId, actor)"
  S->>DB: BEGIN
  S->>DB: "UPDATE bills to Paid WHERE id and status is Unpaid"
  Note over S,DB: "0 rows affected returns 409 บิลนี้ถูกชำระไปแล้ว"
  S->>DB: "INSERT payments, amount copied from bills.total"
  S->>DB: "INSERT counters ON DUPLICATE KEY UPDATE seq to LAST_INSERT_ID"
  S->>DB: "INSERT receipts with REC-YYYYMM-NNNNNN"
  S->>DB: "INSERT audit_log PAYMENT_CONFIRM"
  S->>DB: "INSERT notifications type PAID, status queued"
  S->>DB: COMMIT
  S-->>A: "redirect to the A5 receipt view"
  S->>L: "push PAID after the commit, never inside it"
One transaction, one button, no second screen (D-24). Every duplicate-prevention guard is a database constraint, not a disabled button (D-26).
-- 1. the conditional status change is the concurrency guard
UPDATE bills SET status = 'Paid' WHERE id = ? AND status = 'Unpaid';
--    affectedRows = 0  ->  409 BILL_ALREADY_PAID, message "บิลนี้ถูกชำระไปแล้ว"

-- 2. the amount is never an input (D-24)
INSERT INTO payments (bill_id, amount, paid_at, received_by_user_id, method)
SELECT id, total, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP, ?, 'cash' FROM bills WHERE id = ?;
--    UNIQUE(active_bill_id) rejects a second live payment (D-26)

-- 3. receipt number, atomic and connection-local
INSERT INTO counters (name, seq) VALUES ('receipt', 1)
  ON DUPLICATE KEY UPDATE seq = LAST_INSERT_ID(seq + 1);
SELECT LAST_INSERT_ID();          -- e.g. 852

-- 4. REC- + payment month YYYYMM + '-' + String(seq).padStart(6, '0')  ->  REC-202609-000852
INSERT INTO receipts (payment_id, receipt_no, issued_at) VALUES (?, ?, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP);

Step 4 is string formatting, not arithmetic, so it is the one receipt-number operation that lives in JavaScript. The counter is continuous and never resets — the YYYYMM is a label, not a scope, which is the only way a 67-room building reaches 852 (D-27). The sequence is unique and monotonic but not gapless: a rolled-back transaction consumes a number and the seed starts at an offset. No document, rationale or test may claim gaplessness; the two assertions are that no two receipts share a number and that a later payment never receives a lower one.

After the commit the queued PAID notification is pushed: ✅ ยืนยันการชำระเงิน / ห้อง 205 / ได้รับชำระค่าใช้จ่ายจำนวน 1,790 บาท เรียบร้อยแล้ว / วันที่ 3 กันยายน 2026 / เลขที่ใบเสร็จ REC-202609-000852 (D-31). Note the money format: 1,790.00 on the receipt, 1,790 บาท in the message (D-03).

Partial payment does not exist

A tenant who is short is asked to return with the full amount; the bill stays รอชำระ or เกินกำหนด (D-05 limitation 2, D-24). Because the amount always equals the total, overpayment cannot exist, no SUM appears in any payment query, and no fifth status is needed — which is precisely what lets §4.9.2 be a plain void with no refund machinery.

4.11 Overdue, the daily scheduler and the reminder rule

4.11.1 Overdue is derived, never stored (D-21)

bills.status stores only Draft, Unpaid, Paid. The fourth value is produced by one helper in two languages and nowhere else:

CASE WHEN b.status = 'Unpaid' AND b.due_date < CURDATE()
     THEN 'Overdue' ELSE b.status END AS display_status
export const billStatus = (b) =>
  b.status === 'Unpaid' && b.due_date < todayBangkok() ? 'Overdue' : b.status;

The transition is instantaneous at 00:00 Asia/Bangkok on the day after the due date: no job, no drift, nothing to fail, and no late fee, interest or penalty of any kind. Any surface that shows a status to a human, or filters or counts by a status a human can see, goes through the helper; aggregation that deliberately means "approved" (§4.12's money tiles) or "not yet settled" (§4.13's arrears report) reads the stored column directly and is not a violation — D-21 is a display rule. Every element rendering a status carries data-status="Draft|Unpaid|Paid|Overdue" (D-02).

4.11.2 The daily scheduler (D-32)

POST /internal/jobs/daily, authenticated by X-Job-Token, called at 09:00 Asia/Bangkok by an external scheduler. No node-cron: an in-process timer never fires on a sleeping free instance. The 10-minute GET /healthz ping is a separate schedule and is not this job's business (D-43).

  1. INSERT INTO job_runs (job_name, run_date, started_at) VALUES ('daily', CURDATE(), NOW()). A duplicate-key error means today already ran → 200, no-op (D-32).
  2. Queue PRE_DUE rows for every eligible bill where DATEDIFF(due_date, CURDATE()) = 2.
  3. Queue OVERDUE rows where DATEDIFF(CURDATE(), due_date) IN (1, 8, 15).
  4. SELECT GET_LOCK('notif_drain', 0), then drain queued rows and retry failed rows with attempt_count < 3, including anything a bulk approval left behind (D-19). Released in a finally.
  5. UPDATE job_runs SET finished_at, summary_json and return the counts.
-- the eligibility predicate, shared by steps 2 and 3
SELECT b.id, b.tenant_id, b.total, b.due_date, b.room_code_snapshot
  FROM bills b
  JOIN tenants t ON t.id = b.tenant_id
 WHERE b.status = 'Unpaid'                          -- stored status: excludes Draft and Paid
   AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM payments p         -- D-25: a voided payment suppresses the chase
                    WHERE p.bill_id = b.id AND p.voided_at IS NOT NULL)
   AND <the day condition for this message type>;

Settings carries the Admin button รันงานแจ้งเตือนตอนนี้, which calls the identical code path with job_name = 'daily_manual', skips the job_runs guard entirely so a 14:00 demo still works after the 09:00 cron ran, and relies on the notification dedupe key for safety. It reports ส่งแจ้งเตือนก่อนครบกำหนด {a} ราย · เลยกำหนด {b} ราย · ส่งซ้ำที่ล้มเหลว {c} ราย (D-32). The job never writes a bill status.

4.11.3 The reminder rule (D-33)

flowchart LR
  AP["Approved, due_date frozen"] --> D2["due minus 2: PRE_DUE"]
  D2 --> DUE["due date: nothing is sent"]
  DUE --> O1["due plus 1: OVERDUE"]
  O1 --> O8["due plus 8: OVERDUE"]
  O8 --> O15["due plus 15: OVERDUE"]
  O15 --> STOPA["nothing automatic after day 15"]
  PAID["Payment confirmed"] --> STOP["every later automatic send stops"]
  VOID["Payment voided"] --> STOP
At most four automatic messages per bill for its entire life. Everything fires at 09:00 Asia/Bangkok — no evening send, no weekend send.
DayConditionTypeDemo bill (due 5 ก.ย. 2026)
ApprovalQueued in the approve transactionBILL1 ก.ย. 2026, drained immediately
due − 2DATEDIFF(due_date, CURDATE()) = 2PRE_DUE3 ก.ย. 2026 at 09:00
due—none5 ก.ย. 2026 — nothing is sent
due + 1DATEDIFF(CURDATE(), due_date) IN (1, 8, 15)OVERDUE6 ก.ย. 2026
due + 813 ก.ย. 2026
due + 1520 ก.ย. 2026
any dayAdmin presses ส่งแจ้งเตือนอีกครั้ง on Bill Detail → POST /bills/:id/notifyre-send of the bill's own typeNever capped — it is the tool of last resort on the day an automatic send failed

The demo bill is paid on 3 ก.ย. 2026. If the payment is taken after 09:00 that day, the PRE_DUE message goes out first and the tenant receives both it and the PAID confirmation — correct behaviour, not a defect, and worth knowing before the defence. Once the bill is Paid the eligibility predicate excludes it and the three OVERDUE days never fire.

Duplicate protection is uq_notifications(dedupe_key), the stored generated column CONCAT_WS(':', type, IFNULL(bill_id,0), IFNULL(announcement_id,0), tenant_id, send_date) (ENG-3): one message type per bill per tenant per day, whatever calls the code. Automatic retries stop at attempt_count = 3; the manual button is uncapped.

4.12 Dashboard — the exact formula for every PRD §16 figure (D-41)

Every KPI is scoped to one selected billing period, defaulting to the current calendar month, chosen by one month picker at the top of the page. Throughout this section approved means the stored status is Unpaid or Paid; Draft bills are excluded from every money figure.

PRD §16 labelPayload keyFormula / SQLRange
ห้องทั้งหมดrooms_totalSELECT COUNT(*) FROM rooms WHERE is_active = 1Live, not period-scoped
มีผู้เช่าrooms_occupiedSELECT COUNT(*) FROM rooms WHERE is_active = 1 AND status = 'Occupied'Live
ห้องว่างrooms_vacantrooms_total − rooms_occupied (computed, so the three always reconcile)Live
ยอดค่าน้ำเดือนนี้water_totalSELECT bi.item_type, SUM(bi.amount) FROM bill_items bi JOIN bills b ON b.id = bi.bill_id WHERE b.period = ? AND b.status IN ('Unpaid','Paid') GROUP BY bi.item_typeSelected period
ยอดค่าไฟเดือนนี้electric_total
ยอดค่าส่วนกลางcommon_total
ยอดเรียกเก็บทั้งหมดbilled_totalSELECT SUM(total) FROM bills WHERE period = ? AND status IN ('Unpaid','Paid')Selected period
รับชำระแล้วcollected_totalSELECT SUM(total) FROM bills WHERE period = ? AND status = 'Paid' — counted by billing period, not by payment dateSelected period
ค้างชำระoutstanding_totalbilled_total − collected_totalSelected period
ค้างชำระสะสมทุกงวดarrears_all_timeSELECT SUM(total) FROM bills WHERE status = 'Unpaid'All-time, explicitly
Two identities that must hold on screen, and their tests
  • water_total + electric_total + common_total = billed_total — true because every generated bill always carries exactly three items (D-18, D-41). A bill with fewer than three items is a defect with its own assertion.
  • billed_total − collected_total = outstanding_total, and that figure equals the sum of the period's Unpaid bills — the ones displaying as รอชำระ and เกินกำหนด — because Unpaid and Paid are the only two approved statuses.

Staff Dashboard (D-36): only rooms_total, rooms_occupied, rooms_vacant and the reading progress below. Money is absent from the response, not hidden in the template — dashboardKpis(period, 'staff') issues a query that does not select amounts at all, and there is a test asserting no amount appears anywhere in a staff response body.

-- reading progress, the same figure that drives D-15's counter and D-23's banner
SELECT
  (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM rooms r JOIN tenants t ON t.active_room_id = r.id
    WHERE r.is_active = 1 AND r.status = 'Occupied')                        AS billable,
  (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM (
      SELECT mr.room_id FROM meter_readings mr
        JOIN rooms r ON r.id = mr.room_id
        JOIN tenants t ON t.active_room_id = r.id
       WHERE mr.period = ? AND r.is_active = 1 AND r.status = 'Occupied'
       GROUP BY mr.room_id HAVING COUNT(DISTINCT mr.utility_type) = 2) x)   AS read_rooms,
  (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM bills WHERE period = ?)                             AS billed,
  (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM bills WHERE period = ? AND status IN ('Unpaid','Paid')) AS approved,
  (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM bills WHERE period = ? AND status = 'Paid')         AS paid;

The Dashboard local reading_progress is {done: read_rooms, total: billable} (§3.3). billable − billed is the single figure behind both the D-23 warn banner and the D-11 completeness line, so the two can never disagree, and it is the tester's assertion for ทุกห้องที่มีผู้เช่าได้รับบิล: it must reach 0.
